<script>

const SUPABASE_URL = 'https://hqvknahklvoqtlxigejj.supabase.co';
const SUPABASE_ANON_KEY = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6ImhxdmtuYWhrbHZvcXRseGlnZWpqIiwicm9sZSI6ImFub24iLCJpYXQiOjE3Nzk4NjMzMzYsImV4cCI6MjA5NTQzOTMzNn0.viEwHHoyKIJzTsr-pdc_8jgTpZnzHQUUfCbmLS8IfLw';
const DEFAULT_APP_ENV = {
  IMGBB_API_KEY: '8ddd584f7fb55ef50f87523ac0f14905',
  IMGBB_UPLOAD_URL: 'https://api.imgbb.com/1/upload',
  DEFAULT_WA_GROUP_URL: 'https://chat.whatsapp.com/Hh9PJBefjnpC6ao7rt6bWI',

  CHECKOUT_TELEGRAM_URL: 'https://t.me/ArcZNewEra',

  RANDOM_API_BASE: 'https://api.siputzx.my.id/api/r',
  SEARCH_API_BASE: 'https://api.siputzx.my.id/api/s',
  DOWNLOADER_API_BASE: 'https://api.siputzx.my.id/api/d',
  BRAT_API_URL: 'https://api.siputzx.my.id/api/m/brat',
  RANDOM_CAT_IMAGE_URL: 'https://cataas.com/cat?ts=',
  RANDOM_NEKO_API_URL: 'https://nekos.best/api/v2/neko',

  SOCIAL_TIKTOK_URL: 'https://www.tiktok.com/@nevereraacademy',
  SOCIAL_IMAGE_URL: 'https://i.ibb.co.com/4Rs3kt6p/20260608-150559.jpg'
};
const APP_ENV = { ...DEFAULT_APP_ENV, ...(window.NEVERERA_ENV || {}) };
const IMGBB_API_KEY = APP_ENV.IMGBB_API_KEY;

let sbClient = null;
try {
  sbClient = window.supabase.createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
} catch(e) { console.warn('Supabase not configured'); }

let dataip = null;
let previousWebDeviceId = '';
function hashDeviceValue(value = '') {
  let hash = 2166136261;
  for(let i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
    hash ^= value.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(36).toUpperCase();
}

function getWebDeviceId() {
  const storageKey = 'ne_web_device_id';
  const oldStorageKey = 'ne_hardware_device_id';
  const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey) || localStorage.getItem(oldStorageKey) || '';
  let webglInfo = '';
  try {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl') || canvas.getContext('experimental-webgl');
    if(gl) {
      const debugInfo = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
      webglInfo = [
        debugInfo ? gl.getParameter(debugInfo.UNMASKED_VENDOR_WEBGL) : '',
        debugInfo ? gl.getParameter(debugInfo.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL) : ''
      ].join('|');
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  const raw = [
    navigator.platform || '',
    Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '',
    screen?.width || 0,
    screen?.height || 0,
    screen?.availWidth || 0,
    screen?.availHeight || 0,
    screen?.colorDepth || 0,
    screen?.pixelDepth || 0,
    window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
    navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 0,
    navigator.deviceMemory || 0,
    webglInfo
  ].join('|');
  const id = `WEB-${hashDeviceValue(raw)}-${hashDeviceValue(raw.split('').reverse().join(''))}`;
  previousWebDeviceId = saved && saved !== id ? saved : '';
  localStorage.setItem(storageKey, id);
  return id;
}

async function loadClientIp() {
  try {
    state.clientIp = getWebDeviceId();
    dataip = { ip: state.clientIp };
    updateClientIpUI();
    await checkBannedIp();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal membaca Web ID client:', e);
    state.clientIp = '';
    updateClientIpUI();
  }
}

async function ensureClientIp() {
  if(state.clientIp) return state.clientIp;
  await loadClientIp();
  return state.clientIp;
}

async function checkBannedIp() {
  if(!sbClient || !state.clientIp) return false;
  try {
    const webIds = [state.clientIp, previousWebDeviceId].filter(Boolean);
    const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('banned_ips')
      .select('ip_address')
      .in('ip_address', webIds)
      .limit(1);
    if(error) return false;
    if((data || []).length) {
      const bannedWebId = data[0]?.ip_address || state.clientIp;
      state.isBanned = true;
      stopUserAutoRefresh();
      await cleanupBannedUserActivity({ phone: state.phone, ipAddress: bannedWebId });
      resetMemberStorageBecauseAccountMissing();
      showPage('banned'); 
      return true;
    }
    state.isBanned = false;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal cek banned Web ID:', e);
  }
  return false;
}

async function refreshBannedStatus(manual = false) {
  if(state.bannedRefreshing) return;
  if(manual && !canRunDataRequest('banned_status_check')) return;
  state.bannedRefreshing = true;
  try {
    await ensureClientIp();
    if(await checkBannedIp()) return;
    stopBannedAutoRefresh();
    showToast('Blokir Web ID sudah dicabut.');
    const hasSession = await checkSavedSession();
    if(hasSession) return;
    const hasPending = await checkPendingVerification();
    if(hasPending) return;
    showPage('phone');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal refresh status banned:', e);
  } finally {
    state.bannedRefreshing = false;
  }
}

function shouldSkipSilentRefresh(activePage) {
  const interactivePages = ['games','random','pinterest','youtube','lahelu','downloader','brat','combo','shop','needhelp','findpvp'];
  const active = document.activeElement;
  const isTypingOrSelecting = active && active.matches && active.matches('input, select, textarea, button, [contenteditable="true"]');
  return isTypingOrSelecting || interactivePages.includes(activePage) || interactivePages.includes(state.currentPage);
}

async function refreshCurrentView(silent = false, manual = false) {
  if(state.userRefreshing) return;
  if(manual && !canRunDataRequest('user_refresh')) return;
  state.userRefreshing = true;
  const btn = document.getElementById('userRefreshBtn');
  if(!silent) btn?.classList.add('refreshing');
  try {
    const activePage = document.querySelector('.page.active')?.id?.replace('page-', '') || '';
    const skipPageRefresh = silent && shouldSkipSilentRefresh(activePage);
    await ensureClientIp();
    await loadAppConfig();
    if(await checkBannedIp()) return;
    if(activePage === 'updating') {
      if(await enforceUpdatingServerGate()) {
        await navigateTo(getSavedMemberRoute(), { replaceRoute: true });
      }
      return;
    }
    if(state.phone && !['phone','verified','onboarding','admin','banned','updating'].includes(activePage)) {
      if(!(await enforceUpdatingServerGate())) return;
      const profile = await loadUserProfile();
      if(!profile && state.profileLoadStatus === 'missing') {
        returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
        return;
      }
      if(!profile) {
        returnToHomeBecauseProfileBlocked();
        return;
      }
      renderProfileAvatar();
      renderProfileEditCurrentAvatar();
      updateGreeting();
      if(!ensureAccountTuningComplete({ notify: !silent })) return;
    }
    if(skipPageRefresh) return;
    if(['phone','verified','onboarding'].includes(activePage)) {
      if(!silent) showToast('Data diperbarui');
      return;
    }
    if(state.currentPage === 'materi') {
      await initMaterials();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'giveaway') {
      await initGiveaways();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'needhelp') {
      await initNeedHelp();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'findpvp') {
      await initFindPvp();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'shop') {
      await initShop();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'games') {
      await initGames();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'random') {
      await initRandom();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'pinterest') {
      initPinterestSearch();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'youtube') {
      initYoutubeSearch();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'lahelu') {
      initLaheluSearch();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'downloader') {
      initDownloader();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'brat') {
      initBrat();
    } else if(activePage === 'settings' || activePage === 'appearance') {
      const hasUnsaved = document.querySelector('.settings-missing');
      const active = document.activeElement;
      const editingSettings = active && active.closest && (active.closest('#page-settings') || active.closest('#page-appearance'));
      if(!hasUnsaved && !editingSettings) {
        const profile = await loadUserProfile();
        if(!profile && sbClient && state.phone && state.profileLoadStatus === 'missing') {
          returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
          return;
        }
        if(!profile && sbClient && state.phone) {
          returnToHomeBecauseProfileBlocked();
          return;
        }
        initSettings();
      } else {
        markMissingSettings();
      }
    } else {
      await initDashboard();
    }
    await checkWinnerNotification();
    if(!silent) showToast('Data diperbarui');
  } finally {
    state.userRefreshing = false;
    if(!silent) btn?.classList.remove('refreshing');
  }
}

// State untuk materi
let materialState = {
    completed: [],
    currentlyReading: null,
    activeChapterIndex: 0,
    currentChapters: [],
    chapterCompleted: [],
    readTimer: null,
    secondsLeft: 3,
    synced: false
};

function getMaterialProgressKey() {
    const owner = state.uniqueCode || state.phone || 'guest';
    return `ne_material_progress_${owner}`;
}

function normalizeMaterialIds(ids) {
    return [...new Set((ids || [])
        .map(id => Number(id))
        .filter(id => Number.isInteger(id) && MATERI_DATA.some(m => m.id === id))
    )].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

function loadLocalMaterialProgress() {
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(getMaterialProgressKey());
        if (!raw) return [];
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        return normalizeMaterialIds(Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : parsed.completed);
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Gagal membaca progress lokal:', e);
        return [];
    }
}

function saveLocalMaterialProgress() {
    try {
        localStorage.setItem(getMaterialProgressKey(), JSON.stringify({
            completed: normalizeMaterialIds(materialState.completed),
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        }));
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Gagal menyimpan progress lokal:', e);
    }
}

// ========== LOADING ==========
function showLoading(text = 'Memproses...') {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('loadingOverlay');
  const textEl = overlay.querySelector('.loading-text');
  textEl.textContent = text;
  overlay.classList.add('active');
}
function hideLoading() {
  document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList.remove('active');
}

function finishAppBoot() {
  document.body.classList.remove('app-booting');
}

// ========== UTILS ==========
function isAppFullscreen() {
  return !!(
    document.fullscreenElement ||
    document.webkitFullscreenElement ||
    document.msFullscreenElement
  );
}

async function requestAppFullscreen() {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  try {
    if(root.requestFullscreen) await root.requestFullscreen();
    else if(root.webkitRequestFullscreen) await root.webkitRequestFullscreen();
    else if(root.msRequestFullscreen) await root.msRequestFullscreen();
  } catch(e) {
    pushLogNotification('Browser menolak fullscreen. Klik layar sekali lagi.', 'warn', 'Fullscreen');
  } finally {
    updateFullscreenGate();
  }
}

function updateFullscreenGate() {
  const isAdminPage = document.getElementById('page-admin')?.classList.contains('active');
  document.body.classList.toggle('fullscreen-locked', state.requireFullscreen && !isAppFullscreen() && !isAdminPage);
}

async function loadAppConfig() {
  if(!sbClient) return;
  try {
    const { data } = await sbClient.from('app_config').select('key,value');
    const config = Object.fromEntries((data || []).map(item => [item.key, item.value]));
    if(config.require_fullscreen !== undefined) {
      state.requireFullscreen = config.require_fullscreen === true || config.require_fullscreen === 'true';
    }
    if(config.updating_server_enabled !== undefined) {
      state.updatingServer = config.updating_server_enabled === true || config.updating_server_enabled === 'true';
    }
    if(config.updating_server_message !== undefined) {
      state.updatingServerMessage = String(config.updating_server_message || state.updatingServerMessage);
    }
    if(config.whatsapp_group_url !== undefined) {
      state.whatsappGroupUrl = normalizeWhatsappGroupUrl(config.whatsapp_group_url) || state.whatsappGroupUrl;
    }
    updateFullscreenGate();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal load app config:', e);
  }
}

function updateServerMessageUI() {
  const el = document.getElementById('updatingServerMessage');
  if(el) el.textContent = state.updatingServerMessage || 'Server sedang diupdate. Sementara hanya member whitelist yang bisa memakai fitur.';
}

async function isUpdatingServerWhitelisted() {
  if(!state.updatingServer) return true;
  if(adminState.loggedIn) return true;
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone) return false;
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('server_update_whitelist')
      .select('phone')
      .eq('phone', state.phone)
      .maybeSingle();
    if(error) throw error;
    state.updatingWhitelistAllowed = !!data;
    return !!data;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal cek whitelist updating server:', e);
    state.updatingWhitelistAllowed = false;
    return false;
  }
}

async function enforceUpdatingServerGate() {
  await loadAppConfig();
  updateServerMessageUI();
  if(!state.updatingServer) return true;
  if(await isUpdatingServerWhitelisted()) return true;
  state.currentPage = 'updating';
  showPage('updating');
  return false;
}

function pushLogNotification(message, type = 'info', title = '') {
  const stack = document.getElementById('logNotificationStack');
  if(!stack) return;
  const item = document.createElement('div');
  item.className = `log-notification ${type}`;
  const label = title || (type === 'error' ? 'Error / Masalah' : (type === 'warn' ? 'Peringatan' : 'Pemberitahuan'));
  item.innerHTML = `
    <div class="log-notification-title">${escapeHtml(label)}</div>
    <div class="log-notification-message">${escapeHtml(String(message || 'Tidak ada detail'))}</div>
  `;
  stack.appendChild(item);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => item.classList.add('show'));
  setTimeout(() => {
    item.classList.remove('show');
    setTimeout(() => item.remove(), 320);
  }, type === 'error' ? 6500 : 4200);
}

function showToast(msg, dur=3000) {
  const type = dur === 'error' ? 'error' : (dur === 'warn' ? 'warn' : 'info');
  if(type !== 'info') {
    pushLogNotification(msg, type);
    return;
  }
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), dur);
}

function openAppDialog({ title = 'Konfirmasi', message = '', input = false, placeholder = '', confirmText = 'OK', cancelText = 'Batal' }) {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('appDialogModal');
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('appDialogTitle');
    const messageEl = document.getElementById('appDialogMessage');
    const inputEl = document.getElementById('appDialogInput');
    const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('appDialogConfirm');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('appDialogCancel');
    if(!modal || !titleEl || !messageEl || !inputEl || !confirmBtn || !cancelBtn) {
      resolve(input ? null : false);
      return;
    }
    titleEl.innerHTML = `<i class="fas ${input ? 'fa-key' : 'fa-circle-question'}"></i> ${escapeHtml(title)}`;
    messageEl.textContent = message;
    confirmBtn.textContent = confirmText;
    cancelBtn.textContent = cancelText;
    inputEl.style.display = input ? 'block' : 'none';
    inputEl.value = '';
    inputEl.placeholder = placeholder;
    modal.classList.add('open');
    if(input) setTimeout(() => inputEl.focus(), 60);

    const cleanup = (value) => {
      modal.classList.remove('open');
      confirmBtn.onclick = null;
      cancelBtn.onclick = null;
      inputEl.onkeydown = null;
      resolve(value);
    };
    confirmBtn.onclick = () => cleanup(input ? inputEl.value : true);
    cancelBtn.onclick = () => cleanup(input ? null : false);
    inputEl.onkeydown = (event) => {
      if(event.key === 'Enter') cleanup(inputEl.value);
      if(event.key === 'Escape') cleanup(input ? null : false);
    };
  });
}

function appConfirm(message, title = 'Konfirmasi') {
  return openAppDialog({ title, message, confirmText: 'OK', cancelText: 'Batal' });
}

function appPrompt(message, title = 'Kode', placeholder = '') {
  return openAppDialog({ title, message, input: true, placeholder, confirmText: 'Masuk', cancelText: 'Batal' });
}

const REQUEST_RATE_LIMIT_MS = 5000;
const REQUEST_RATE_MAX_HITS = 3;
const REQUEST_FREEZE_KEY = 'ne_request_freeze_until';
const REQUEST_FREEZE_ACTION_KEY = 'ne_request_freeze_action';
const REQUEST_RATE_PREFIX = 'ne_request_rate_';
let requestFreezeInterval = null;

function getRequestFreezeUntil() {
  return Number(localStorage.getItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_KEY) || 0);
}

function setRequestFreeze(ms = REQUEST_RATE_LIMIT_MS, action = 'global') {
  const until = Date.now() + ms;
  localStorage.setItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_KEY, String(until));
  localStorage.setItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_ACTION_KEY, JSON.stringify({
    action,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    until
  }));
  showRequestFreezePage(until);
  return until;
}

function showRequestFreezePage(until = getRequestFreezeUntil()) {
  const page = document.getElementById('requestFreezePage');
  const timer = document.getElementById('requestFreezeTimer');
  if(!page || !timer) return;
  clearInterval(requestFreezeInterval);
  const tick = () => {
    const remaining = Math.max(0, until - Date.now());
    if(remaining <= 0) {
      page.classList.remove('active');
      localStorage.removeItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_ACTION_KEY);
      clearInterval(requestFreezeInterval);
      requestFreezeInterval = null;
      return;
    }
    timer.textContent = `${Math.ceil(remaining / 1000)} detik`;
  };
  page.classList.add('active');
  tick();
  requestFreezeInterval = setInterval(tick, 250);
}

function restoreRequestFreezePage() {
  const until = getRequestFreezeUntil();
  if(until > Date.now()) showRequestFreezePage(until);
  else {
    localStorage.removeItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(REQUEST_FREEZE_ACTION_KEY);
  }
}

function canRunDataRequest(action = 'global') {
  const frozenUntil = getRequestFreezeUntil();
  if(frozenUntil > Date.now()) {
    showRequestFreezePage(frozenUntil);
    return false;
  }
  const key = `${REQUEST_RATE_PREFIX}${action}`;
  const now = Date.now();
  const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || 'null');
  const startedAt = Number(saved?.startedAt || 0);
  const count = Number(saved?.count || 0);
  const nextCount = startedAt && now - startedAt < REQUEST_RATE_LIMIT_MS ? count + 1 : 1;
  const nextStartedAt = nextCount === 1 ? now : startedAt;
  if(nextCount >= REQUEST_RATE_MAX_HITS) {
    localStorage.removeItem(key);
    setRequestFreeze(REQUEST_RATE_LIMIT_MS, action);
    return false;
  }
  localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({ startedAt: nextStartedAt, count: nextCount, updatedAt: now }));
  return true;
}

const MEMBER_PAGE_IDS = ['dashboard','materi','giveaway','needhelp','findpvp','shop','stock','games','random','pinterest','youtube','lahelu','downloader','brat','settings','appearance','report','combo','account-tune'];
const MEMBER_NAV_PAGES = ['beranda','materi','giveaway','needhelp','findpvp','shop','stock','games','random','pinterest','youtube','lahelu','downloader','brat','report','combo','pengaturan','tampilan','penyesuaian'];
const MEMBER_ROUTE_ALIASES = {
  dashboard: 'beranda',
  home: 'beranda',
  search: 'pinterest',
  settings: 'pengaturan',
  appearance: 'tampilan',
  account: 'penyesuaian',
  account_tune: 'penyesuaian',
  'account-tune': 'penyesuaian'
};

function normalizeMemberRoute(page) {
  const raw = String(page || '').replace(/^#\/?/, '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const normalized = MEMBER_ROUTE_ALIASES[raw] || raw;
  return MEMBER_NAV_PAGES.includes(normalized) ? normalized : 'beranda';
}

function getSavedMemberRoute() {
  const hashRoute = normalizeMemberRoute(window.location.hash);
  if(window.location.hash && hashRoute) return hashRoute;
  return normalizeMemberRoute(localStorage.getItem('ne_last_member_page') || 'beranda');
}

function saveMemberRoute(page, replace = false) {
  const route = normalizeMemberRoute(page);
  localStorage.setItem('ne_last_member_page', route);
  const nextUrl = `${window.location.pathname}${window.location.search}#${route}`;
  if(replace) history.replaceState(null, '', nextUrl);
  else if(window.location.hash !== `#${route}`) history.pushState(null, '', nextUrl);
}

function openSidebarForTutorial() {
  document.getElementById('sidebar')?.classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay')?.classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn')?.classList.add('open');
}

function getMainTutorialTarget() {
  const step = MAIN_TUTORIAL_STEPS[state.tutorialStep];
  return step ? document.querySelector(step.selector) : null;
}

function positionMainTutorial() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('mainTutorialOverlay');
  const card = document.getElementById('mainTutorialCard');
  const target = getMainTutorialTarget();
  if(!overlay || !card || !target) return;
  target.scrollIntoView?.({ block: 'center', inline: 'nearest' });
  const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
  const radius = Math.max(42, Math.ceil(Math.max(rect.width, rect.height) / 2) + 14);
  const x = Math.round(rect.left + rect.width / 2);
  const y = Math.round(rect.top + rect.height / 2);
  overlay.style.setProperty('--spot-x', `${x}px`);
  overlay.style.setProperty('--spot-y', `${y}px`);
  overlay.style.setProperty('--spot-r', `${radius}px`);
  const preferRight = rect.right + 24;
  const cardX = preferRight + 320 < window.innerWidth ? preferRight : Math.max(16, rect.left - 344);
  const cardY = Math.min(Math.max(16, rect.top - 18), Math.max(16, window.innerHeight - 230));
  card.style.setProperty('--card-x', `${cardX}px`);
  card.style.setProperty('--card-y', `${cardY}px`);
}

function updateMainTutorialGate() {
  const nextBtn = document.getElementById('mainTutorialNext');
  const hint = document.getElementById('mainTutorialHint');
  if(!nextBtn || !hint) return;
  const ready = state.tutorialReadDone && state.tutorialTargetClicked;
  nextBtn.disabled = !ready;
  nextBtn.innerHTML = state.tutorialStep >= MAIN_TUTORIAL_STEPS.length - 1
    ? 'Selesai <i class="fas fa-check"></i>'
    : 'Lanjut <i class="fas fa-arrow-right"></i>';
  if(!state.tutorialReadDone) {
    hint.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-book-open"></i> Baca dulu 2 detik...';
  } else if(!state.tutorialTargetClicked) {
    hint.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-hand-pointer"></i> Klik area yang disorot.';
  } else {
    hint.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-circle-check"></i> Mantap, boleh lanjut.';
  }
}

function renderMainTutorialStep() {
  const step = MAIN_TUTORIAL_STEPS[state.tutorialStep];
  const overlay = document.getElementById('mainTutorialOverlay');
  if(!step || !overlay) {
    finishMainTutorial();
    return;
  }
  if(state.tutorialStep > 0) openSidebarForTutorial();
  document.getElementById('mainTutorialKicker').textContent = `Tutorial ${state.tutorialStep + 1}/${MAIN_TUTORIAL_STEPS.length}`;
  document.getElementById('mainTutorialTitle').textContent = step.title;
  document.getElementById('mainTutorialText').textContent = step.text;
  state.tutorialReadDone = false;
  state.tutorialTargetClicked = false;
  clearTimeout(state.tutorialTimer);
  state.tutorialTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    state.tutorialReadDone = true;
    updateMainTutorialGate();
  }, 2000);
  updateMainTutorialGate();
  requestAnimationFrame(positionMainTutorial);
}

function handleMainTutorialClick(event) {
  const target = getMainTutorialTarget();
  const step = MAIN_TUTORIAL_STEPS[state.tutorialStep];
  if(!target || !step) return;
  const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
  const pad = 14;
  const inside = event.clientX >= rect.left - pad && event.clientX <= rect.right + pad
    && event.clientY >= rect.top - pad && event.clientY <= rect.bottom + pad;
  if(!inside) {
    showToast('Klik area yang ditunjuk dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  state.tutorialTargetClicked = true;
  if(step.action === 'openSidebar') openSidebarForTutorial();
  updateMainTutorialGate();
  positionMainTutorial();
}

function startMainTutorial({ force = false } = {}) {
  if(!force && localStorage.getItem(MAIN_TUTORIAL_KEY) === 'true') return;
  if(!document.getElementById('page-dashboard')?.classList.contains('active')) return;
  closeNewMenu();
  closeSidebar();
  state.tutorialStep = 0;
  document.body.classList.add('tutorial-active');
  const overlay = document.getElementById('mainTutorialOverlay');
  overlay?.classList.add('open');
  const scrim = document.getElementById('mainTutorialScrim');
  if(scrim && !scrim.dataset.bound) {
    scrim.addEventListener('click', handleMainTutorialClick);
    scrim.dataset.bound = 'true';
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize', positionMainTutorial);
  renderMainTutorialStep();
}

function maybeStartMainTutorial() {
  setTimeout(() => startMainTutorial(), 350);
}

function nextMainTutorialStep() {
  if(!state.tutorialReadDone || !state.tutorialTargetClicked) {
    updateMainTutorialGate();
    return;
  }
  state.tutorialStep += 1;
  renderMainTutorialStep();
}

function finishMainTutorial(skipped = false) {
  clearTimeout(state.tutorialTimer);
  document.getElementById('mainTutorialOverlay')?.classList.remove('open');
  document.body.classList.remove('tutorial-active');
  window.removeEventListener('resize', positionMainTutorial);
  closeSidebar();
  localStorage.setItem(MAIN_TUTORIAL_KEY, 'true');
  if(skipped) showToast('Tutorial bisa diulang dari Pengaturan');
}

async function restartMainTutorial() {
  localStorage.removeItem(MAIN_TUTORIAL_KEY);
  saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
  showPage('dashboard');
  state.currentPage = 'beranda';
  setActiveNavigation('beranda');
  await initDashboard({ resetPage: false });
  startMainTutorial({ force: true });
}

function showPage(id) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('page-'+id).classList.add('active');
  document.body.dataset.activePage = id;
  document.body.classList.toggle('show-user-refresh', MEMBER_PAGE_IDS.includes(id));
  if(id === 'phone') {
    updateSignupJoinGate();
    loadSignupCaptcha();
  }
  if(id === 'banned') {
    stopUserAutoRefresh();
    startBannedAutoRefresh();
  } else {
    stopBannedAutoRefresh();
    stopUserAutoRefresh();
  }
}

function startUserAutoRefresh() {
  stopUserAutoRefresh();
}

function stopUserAutoRefresh() {
  if(state.userRefreshInterval) {
    clearInterval(state.userRefreshInterval);
    state.userRefreshInterval = null;
  }
}

function startBannedAutoRefresh() {
  stopBannedAutoRefresh();
}

function stopBannedAutoRefresh() {
  if(state.bannedRefreshInterval) {
    clearInterval(state.bannedRefreshInterval);
    state.bannedRefreshInterval = null;
  }
}

function applyLocalSessionRealtime(rawSession) {
  if(!rawSession) {
    state.phone = '';
    state.verifyCode = '';
    state.uniqueCode = '';
    stopUserAutoRefresh();
    showPage('phone');
    return;
  }
  try {
    const session = JSON.parse(rawSession);
    if(!session.phone || !session.verified) return;
    state.phone = session.phone;
    state.verifyCode = session.verifyCode || state.verifyCode || '';
    state.uniqueCode = session.uniqueCode || state.uniqueCode || '';
    state.theme = session.theme || state.theme || 'light';
    state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(session.uiMode || state.uiMode || 'old');
    state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(session.accentColor || state.accentColor || '#27a44f');
    state.displayName = session.displayName || state.displayName || '';
    state.avatarBorder = session.avatarBorder || state.avatarBorder || 'white';
    state.winnerDisplayMode = session.winnerDisplayMode || state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
    state.gender = session.gender || state.gender || '';
    state.interests = normalizeInterests(session.interests).length ? normalizeInterests(session.interests) : normalizeInterests(state.interests);
    state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(session.timezone || state.timezone);
    applyAppearancePreferences();
    updateGreeting();
    renderProfileAvatar();
    const userName = document.getElementById('sbUserName');
    if(userName) userName.textContent = session.displayName || 'Member';
    const activePage = document.querySelector('.page.active')?.id?.replace('page-', '') || '';
    if(activePage === 'settings' || activePage === 'appearance') {
      const hasUnsaved = document.querySelector('.settings-missing');
      if(!hasUnsaved) initSettings();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal sync localStorage session:', e);
  }
}

function handleLocalStorageRealtime(event) {
  if(event.key === 'ne_session') {
    applyLocalSessionRealtime(event.newValue);
  }
  if(event.key === 'ne_pending' && event.newValue) {
    checkPendingVerification();
  }
  if(event.key === 'ne_admin_theme' && state.currentPage === 'admin') {
    applyTheme(event.newValue || 'light');
  }
  if(event.key && event.key.startsWith(`ne_seen_winners_${state.phone}`) && state.winnerAlertOpen) {
    dismissWinnerAlert();
  }
}

const ADMIN_CODE_CONFIG_KEY = 'admin_code';
const ADMIN_SESSION_VERSION = 'admin-session-supabase';
const ADMIN_SECRET_CLICK_REQUIRED = 3;
const ADMIN_SECRET_CLICK_WINDOW_MS = 2200;
let adminSecretClickState = { count: 0, lastAt: 0 };

function handleSecretAdminClick(event) {
  event?.preventDefault?.();
  event?.stopPropagation?.();
  const now = Date.now();
  adminSecretClickState.count = now - adminSecretClickState.lastAt <= ADMIN_SECRET_CLICK_WINDOW_MS
    ? adminSecretClickState.count + 1
    : 1;
  adminSecretClickState.lastAt = now;
  if(adminSecretClickState.count >= ADMIN_SECRET_CLICK_REQUIRED) {
    adminSecretClickState = { count: 0, lastAt: 0 };
    openAdminLogin();
  }
}

function openAdminLogin() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('adminLoginModal');
  const input = document.getElementById('adminCodeInput');
  document.body.classList.add('fullscreen-admin-login');
  if(modal) modal.classList.add('open');
  if(input) {
    input.value = '';
    setTimeout(() => input.focus(), 50);
  }
}

function closeAdminLogin() {
  document.getElementById('adminLoginModal')?.classList.remove('open');
  document.body.classList.remove('fullscreen-admin-login');
  if(!adminState.loggedIn) updateFullscreenGate();
}

async function submitAdminCode() {
  const code = document.getElementById('adminCodeInput')?.value.trim();
  if(!code) {
    showToast('Kode admin wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Cek kode admin...');
  let isValidAdminCode = false;
  try {
    isValidAdminCode = await verifyAdminCodeFromSupabase(code);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Admin code check error:', e);
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
  if(!isValidAdminCode) {
    showToast('Kode admin salah', 'error');
    return;
  }
  adminState.loggedIn = true;
  applyTheme(localStorage.getItem('ne_admin_theme') || 'light');
  document.body.classList.remove('fullscreen-admin-login');
  localStorage.setItem('ne_admin_session', JSON.stringify({
    loggedIn: true,
    version: ADMIN_SESSION_VERSION,
    timestamp: Date.now()
  }));
  closeAdminLogin();
  showPage('admin');
  updateFullscreenGate();
  await initAdminDashboard();
  startAdminAutoRefresh();
}

function normalizeAdminCode(code) {
  return String(code || '').trim();
}

async function verifyAdminCodeFromSupabase(code) {
  if(!sbClient) {
    showToast('Supabase belum aktif', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  const inputCode = normalizeAdminCode(code);
  if(!inputCode) return false;
  const { data, error } = await sbClient
    .from('app_config')
    .select('value')
    .eq('key', ADMIN_CODE_CONFIG_KEY)
    .maybeSingle();
  if(error) throw error;
  const savedCode = normalizeAdminCode(data?.value);
  if(!savedCode) {
    showToast('Kode admin belum diset di Supabase', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  return inputCode === savedCode;
}

function adminLogout() {
  stopAdminAutoRefresh();
  stopUserAutoRefresh();
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_admin_session');
  adminState.loggedIn = false;
  showPage('phone');
}

async function checkAdminSession() {
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_admin_session') || '{}');
    if(saved.loggedIn && saved.version === ADMIN_SESSION_VERSION && Date.now() - saved.timestamp < 86400000) {
      adminState.loggedIn = true;
      applyTheme(localStorage.getItem('ne_admin_theme') || 'light');
      showPage('admin');
      updateFullscreenGate();
      await initAdminDashboard();
      startAdminAutoRefresh();
      return true;
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_admin_session');
  return false;
}

function startAdminAutoRefresh() {
  stopAdminAutoRefresh();
}

function stopAdminAutoRefresh() {
  if(adminState.refreshInterval) {
    clearInterval(adminState.refreshInterval);
    adminState.refreshInterval = null;
  }
  document.getElementById('adminRefreshBtn')?.classList.remove('auto-refreshing');
}

function openAdminMenu() {
  document.getElementById('adminTabs')?.classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById('adminDrawerOverlay')?.classList.add('open');
}

function closeAdminMenu() {
  document.getElementById('adminTabs')?.classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('adminDrawerOverlay')?.classList.remove('open');
}

function toggleAdminMenu() {
  const tabs = document.getElementById('adminTabs');
  if(tabs?.classList.contains('open')) closeAdminMenu();
  else openAdminMenu();
}

function switchAdminTab(tab) {
  adminState.tab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll('.admin-tab').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.adminTab === tab);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.admin-panel').forEach(panel => {
    panel.classList.toggle('active', panel.id === `adminPanel-${tab}`);
  });
  closeAdminMenu();
}

async function initAdminDashboard() {
  switchAdminTab(adminState.tab || 'pending');
  await refreshAdminData();
}

async function refreshAdminData(silent = false, manual = false) {
  if(!adminState.loggedIn) return;
  if(adminState.refreshing) return;
  if(manual && !canRunDataRequest('admin_refresh')) return;
  if(!sbClient) {
    showToast('Supabase belum aktif', 'error');
    return;
  }

  adminState.refreshing = true;
  if(!silent) showLoading('Memuat admin...');
  try {
    await cleanupStalePvpRecords();
    const [
      pendingRes,
      usersRes,
      giveawayRes,
      shopRes,
      entryRes,
      winnerRes,
      bannedRes,
      appealRes,
      bugReportRes,
      comboRequestRes,
      configRes,
      whitelistRes,
      materialRes,
      carouselRes,
      ratingRes,
      needHelpPostRes,
      needHelpMessageRes,
      pvpQueueRes,
      pvpMatchRes,
      pvpMessageRes,
      pvpReportRes
    ] = await Promise.all([
      sbClient.from('pending_users').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('users').select('*').order('updated_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('giveaways').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('shop_products').select('*').order('order_num', { ascending: true }).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('giveaway_entries').select('*').order('joined_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('giveaway_winners').select('*').order('announced_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('banned_ips').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('ban_appeals').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('bug_reports').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('combo_requests').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('app_config').select('*'),
      sbClient.from('server_update_whitelist').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('materials').select('*').order('order_num', { ascending: true }).order('id', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient.from('carousel_images').select('*').order('order_num', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient.from('ratings').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('help_posts').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient.from('help_messages').select('*').order('id', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient.from('pvp_queue').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('pvp_matches').select('*').order('started_at', { ascending: false }),
      sbClient.from('pvp_match_messages').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true }).order('id', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient.from('pvp_match_reports').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    ]);

    adminState.pendingUsers = pendingRes.data || [];
    adminState.users = usersRes.data || [];
    adminState.giveaways = giveawayRes.data || [];
    adminState.shopProducts = shopRes.data || [];
    adminState.entries = entryRes.data || [];
    adminState.winners = winnerRes.data || [];
    adminState.bannedIps = bannedRes.data || [];
    adminState.appeals = appealRes.data || [];
    adminState.bugReports = bugReportRes.data || [];
    adminState.comboRequests = comboRequestRes.data || [];
    adminState.appConfig = configRes.data || [];
    adminState.updateWhitelist = whitelistRes.data || [];
    adminState.materials = normalizeMaterialsFromRows(materialRes.data || [], true);
    await loadAdminMaterialChapters();
    const activeMaterials = adminState.materials.filter(item => item.is_active !== false);
    if(activeMaterials.length) MATERI_DATA = activeMaterials;
    adminState.carousel = carouselRes.data || [];
    adminState.ratings = ratingRes.data || [];
    adminState.needHelpPosts = needHelpPostRes.data || [];
    adminState.needHelpMessages = orderNeedHelpMessages(needHelpMessageRes.data || []);
    adminState.pvpQueue = pvpQueueRes.data || [];
    adminState.pvpMatches = pvpMatchRes.data || [];
    adminState.pvpMatchMessages = pvpMessageRes.data || [];
    adminState.pvpMatchReports = pvpReportRes.data || [];

    renderAdminDashboard();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Admin load error:', e);
    if(!silent) showToast('Gagal memuat admin data', 'error');
  } finally {
    adminState.refreshing = false;
    if(!silent) hideLoading();
  }
}

function renderAdminDashboard() {
  const active = document.activeElement;
  const isEditingAdminForm = active && active.closest && active.closest('.admin-form');
  renderAdminPending();
  renderAdminUsers();
  renderAdminWhitelist(!isEditingAdminForm);
  renderAdminAvatarBorders(!isEditingAdminForm);
  renderAdminGiveaways(!isEditingAdminForm);
  renderAdminShop();
  renderAdminWinners();
  renderAdminBannedIps();
  renderAdminAppeals(!isEditingAdminForm);
  renderAdminBugReports();
  renderAdminComboRequests();
  if(!isEditingAdminForm) renderAdminSettings();
  renderAdminMaterials(!isEditingAdminForm);
  renderAdminCarousel();
  renderAdminRatings();
  renderAdminNeedHelp();
  renderAdminPvp();
  initAdminMissingMarkers();
}

function initAdminMissingMarkers() {
  [
    'adminUserPhone',
    'adminUserCode',
    'adminUserIp',
    'adminGiveawayTitle',
    'adminGiveawayDesc',
    'adminGiveawayPrize',
    'adminMaterialId',
    'adminMaterialTitle',
    'adminMaterialDesc',
    'adminMaterialContent',
    'adminMaterialOrder',
    'adminChapterTitle',
    'adminChapterOrder',
    'adminCarouselTitle',
    'adminCarouselImageMode',
    'adminCarouselUrl',
    'adminCarouselOrder'
  ].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el) return;
    el.classList.toggle('admin-missing', !!el.value.trim());
    if(!el.dataset.adminMissingListener) {
      el.addEventListener('input', () => {
        el.classList.toggle('admin-missing', !!el.value.trim());
      });
      el.addEventListener('change', () => {
        el.classList.toggle('admin-missing', !!el.value.trim());
      });
      el.dataset.adminMissingListener = 'true';
    }
  });
}

function adminEmpty(text) {
  return `<div class="admin-empty">${escapeHtml(text)}</div>`;
}

function renderAdminPending() {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminPendingList');
  if(!el) return;
  if(!adminState.pendingUsers.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Tidak ada pending user.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = adminState.pendingUsers.map(user => `
    <div class="admin-row">
      <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(user.display_name || 'Tanpa nama')} - ${escapeHtml(formatPhoneDisplay(user.phone || ''))}</div>
      <div class="admin-row-meta">Kode: ${escapeHtml(user.verify_code || '-')} | Unique: ${escapeHtml(user.unique_code || '-')} | Web ID: ${escapeHtml(user.ip_address || '-')} | Verified: ${user.verified === true}</div>
      <div class="admin-actions">
        <button class="admin-btn" onclick="adminApprovePending('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Acc</button>
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminTogglePending('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}', ${user.verified === true ? 'false' : 'true'})">Toggle Verified</button>
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminRejectPending('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')"><i class="fas fa-xmark"></i> Reject</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeclinePending('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')"><i class="fas fa-ban"></i> Decline + Ban Web ID</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function renderAdminUsers() {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminUsersList');
  if(!el) return;
  if(!adminState.users.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada user.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = adminState.users.map(user => {
    const displayName = user.display_name || 'Tanpa nama';
    const phoneText = formatPhoneDisplay(user.phone || '') || user.phone || '-';
    return `
    <div class="admin-row">
      <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(displayName)} - ${escapeHtml(phoneText)}</div>
      <div class="admin-row-meta">Code: ${escapeHtml(user.unique_code || '-')} | Web ID: ${escapeHtml(user.ip_address || '-')}<br>Progress: ${escapeHtml(user.material_progress_percent ?? 0)}% | Last materi: ${escapeHtml(user.last_material_id || '-')}<br>Reset cooldown combo: ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(user.combo_cooldown_reset_at) || '-')}</div>
      <div class="admin-actions">
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminFillUser('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}', '${escapeHtml(user.unique_code || '')}', '${escapeHtml(user.theme || '')}', '${escapeHtml(user.ip_address || '')}')">Edit</button>
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminResetMaterialProgress('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')">Reset Materi</button>
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminResetComboCooldown('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')">Reset Cooldown Combo</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminBanUser('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')">Ban User</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteUser('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')">Hapus</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderAdminWhitelist(updateControls = true) {
  const select = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistUser');
  const list = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistList');
  if(select && updateControls) {
    const current = select.value || '';
    select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pilih user</option>' + adminState.users.map(user => `
      <option value="${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}">${escapeHtml(user.display_name || formatPhoneDisplay(user.phone || '') || user.phone || 'Member')}</option>
    `).join('');
    if(current && adminState.users.some(user => String(user.phone) === String(current))) {
      select.value = current;
    }
    if(!select.dataset.whitelistListener) {
      select.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const phoneInput = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistPhone');
        if(phoneInput && select.value) phoneInput.value = select.value;
      });
      select.dataset.whitelistListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  if(!list) return;
  if(!adminState.updateWhitelist.length) {
    list.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada member whitelist.');
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = adminState.updateWhitelist.map(item => {
    const user = adminState.users.find(row => String(row.phone || '') === String(item.phone || ''));
    const name = item.display_name || user?.display_name || formatPhoneDisplay(item.phone || '') || 'Member';
    return `
      <div class="admin-row">
        <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(name)}</div>
        <div class="admin-row-meta">Phone: ${escapeHtml(formatPhoneDisplay(item.phone || '') || item.phone || '-')}<br>Catatan: ${escapeHtml(item.note || '-')}<br>Dibuat: ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(item.created_at) || '-')}</div>
        <div class="admin-actions">
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteWhitelistMember('${escapeHtml(item.phone || '')}')"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Hapus</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function isCurrentGiveawayWinner(winner, giveaway) {
  if(!winner || !giveaway) return false;
  if(giveaway.is_active === true) return false;
  if(!giveaway.starts_at || !winner.announced_at) return true;
  return new Date(winner.announced_at).getTime() >= new Date(giveaway.starts_at).getTime();
}

function renderAdminGiveaways(updateFormControls = true) {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminGiveawaysList');
  if(!el) return;
  if(!adminState.giveaways.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada giveaway.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = adminState.giveaways.map(g => {
    const entries = adminState.entries.filter(e => e.giveaway_id === g.id);
    const winners = adminState.winners.filter(w => w.giveaway_id === g.id);
    const activeWinners = winners.filter(w => isCurrentGiveawayWinner(w, g));
    const entryRows = entries.length ? `
      <table class="admin-entry-table">
        <thead>
          <tr><th>Peserta</th><th>Join</th><th>Aksi</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          ${entries.map(entry => {
            const winner = activeWinners.find(w => w.user_phone === entry.user_phone);
            const action = winner
              ? `<button class="admin-entry-pick danger neon" onclick="adminDeleteWinner('${escapeHtml(String(winner.id || ''))}', '${g.id}', '${escapeHtml(entry.user_phone || '')}')"><i class="fas fa-bolt"></i> Hapus Kemenangan</button>`
              : `<button class="admin-entry-pick" onclick="adminPickWinner('${g.id}', '${escapeHtml(entry.user_phone || '')}')">Pilih</button>`;
            return `
              <tr>
                <td>${escapeHtml(formatPhoneDisplay(entry.user_phone || ''))}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(entry.joined_at) || '-')}</td>
                <td>${action}</td>
              </tr>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    ` : '<div class="admin-row-meta" style="margin-top:10px">Belum ada peserta.</div>';
    return `
      <div class="admin-row">
        <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(g.title || 'Giveaway')}</div>
        <div class="admin-row-meta">ID: ${g.id}<br>Status: ${g.is_active ? 'Berlangsung' : 'Selesai'} | Peserta: ${entries.length} | Pemenang Aktif: ${activeWinners.length} | History: ${winners.length}<br>Hadiah: ${escapeHtml(g.prize || '-')}</div>
        <div class="admin-actions">
          <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminFillGiveaway('${g.id}')">Edit</button>
          <button class="admin-btn" onclick="adminSetGiveawayStatus('${g.id}', ${g.is_active ? 'false' : 'true'})">${g.is_active ? 'Selesaikan' : 'Buka Lagi'}</button>
          <button class="admin-btn" onclick="adminRandomWinner('${g.id}')"><i class="fas fa-shuffle"></i> Random Pemenang</button>
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteGiveaway('${g.id}')">Hapus</button>
        </div>
        ${entryRows}
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderAdminWinners() {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminWinnersList');
  if(!el) return;
  if(!adminState.winners.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada pemenang.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = adminState.winners.map(w => {
    const giveaway = adminState.giveaways.find(g => g.id === w.giveaway_id);
    return `
      <div class="admin-row">
        <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(w.winner_name || w.user_phone || 'Pemenang')}</div>
        <div class="admin-row-meta">Giveaway: ${escapeHtml(giveaway?.title || w.giveaway_id)}<br>Phone: ${escapeHtml(w.user_phone || '-')} | Hadiah: ${escapeHtml(w.prize_note || '-')}</div>
        <div class="admin-actions">
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteWinner('${escapeHtml(String(w.id || ''))}', '${escapeHtml(String(w.giveaway_id || ''))}', '${escapeHtml(String(w.user_phone || ''))}')">Hapus Kemenangan</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderAdminShop() {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminShopList');
  if(!el) return;
  const search = document.getElementById('adminShopSearch')?.value.trim().toLowerCase() || '';
  const products = adminState.shopProducts.filter(item => {
    if(!search) return true;
    return String(item.id || '').toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      String(item.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(search);
  });
  if(!products.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada produk shop.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = products.map(item => `
    <div class="admin-row">
      <div class="admin-row-title">${item.is_active ? '' : '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash" title="Nonaktif"></i> '}${escapeHtml(item.name || 'Produk')}</div>
      <div class="admin-row-meta">
        ID: ${item.id}<br>
        Kategori: ${escapeHtml(item.category || '-')} | Harga: ${formatRupiah(Number(item.price || 0))} | Stok: ${item.stock ?? '-'}<br>
        Status: ${item.is_active ? 'Aktif' : 'Nonaktif'} | Badge: ${escapeHtml(item.badge || '-')} | Gambar: ${item.image_url ? 'Ada' : 'Kosong'}
      </div>
      <div class="admin-actions">
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminFillShopProduct('${escapeHtml(String(item.id || ''))}')">Edit</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteById('shop_products', '${escapeHtml(String(item.id || ''))}')">Hapus</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

const APPEAL_FILTERS = [
  { key: 'pending', label: 'Pending' },
  { key: 'reviewed', label: 'Reviewed' },
  { key: 'approved', label: 'Approved' },
  { key: 'rejected', label: 'Rejected' },
  { key: 'all', label: 'Semua' }
];

function getAppealCounts() {
  const counts = { pending: 0, reviewed: 0, approved: 0, rejected: 0, all: adminState.appeals.length };
  adminState.appeals.forEach(item => {
    const status = item.status || 'pending';
    counts[status] = (counts[status] || 0) + 1;
  });
  return counts;
}

function loadAdminAppealSeenCounts() {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_admin_appeal_seen_counts') || '{}');
  } catch(e) {
    return {};
  }
}

function saveAdminAppealSeenCounts() {
  localStorage.setItem('ne_admin_appeal_seen_counts', JSON.stringify(adminState.appealSeenCounts || {}));
}

function markAppealFilterRead(filter) {
  const counts = getAppealCounts();
  adminState.appealSeenCounts = {
    ...loadAdminAppealSeenCounts(),
    [filter]: counts[filter] || 0
  };
  saveAdminAppealSeenCounts();
}

function setAdminAppealFilter(filter) {
  adminState.appealFilter = filter;
  markAppealFilterRead(filter);
  renderAdminAppeals(false);
}

function renderAdminAppealFilterBubbles(activeFilter) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('adminAppealFilterBubbles');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const counts = getAppealCounts();
  const seen = adminState.appealSeenCounts || {};
  wrap.innerHTML = APPEAL_FILTERS.map(item => {
    const count = counts[item.key] || 0;
    const hasNew = count > Number(seen[item.key] || 0);
    return `
      <button class="appeal-filter-btn ${activeFilter === item.key ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setAdminAppealFilter('${item.key}')">
        ${escapeHtml(item.label)}
        <span class="appeal-count-bubble ${hasNew ? 'new' : 'read'}">${count}</span>
      </button>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderAdminAppeals(updateFilter = true) {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminAppealsList');
  if(!el) return;
  const filter = adminState.appealFilter || 'pending';
  if(!adminState.appealSeenCounts || !Object.keys(adminState.appealSeenCounts).length) {
    adminState.appealSeenCounts = loadAdminAppealSeenCounts();
  }
  renderAdminAppealFilterBubbles(filter);
  const appeals = filter === 'all'
    ? adminState.appeals
    : adminState.appeals.filter(item => (item.status || 'pending') === filter);
  if(!appeals.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada appeal.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = appeals.map(item => `
    <div class="admin-row">
      <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(item.contact || 'Tanpa kontak')} - ${escapeHtml(item.status || 'pending')}</div>
      <div class="admin-row-meta">Web ID: ${escapeHtml(item.ip_address || '-')}<br>${escapeHtml(item.message || '')}<br>Dikirim: ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(item.created_at) || '-')}</div>
      <div class="admin-actions">
        <button class="admin-btn" onclick="adminUpdateAppeal(${item.id}, 'approved')">Approve</button>
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminUpdateAppeal(${item.id}, 'reviewed')">Reviewed</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminUpdateAppeal(${item.id}, 'rejected')">Reject</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteById('ban_appeals', ${item.id})"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

        Pesan: ${escapeHtml(item.message || '-')}<br>

}

function getComboRequestMain(item) {
  const note = item?.extra_questions || '';
  return note.match(/^Main: ([^\n]+)/)?.[1] || '-';
}

function getComboRequestNotes(item) {
  const note = item?.extra_questions || '';
  return note.replace(/^Main: [^\n]+\n?/, '').trim() || '-';
}

  const el = document.getElementById('adminComboRequestsList');

  if(!adminState.comboRequests.length) {

  el.innerHTML = adminState.comboRequests.map(item => `
    <div class="admin-row">

      <div class="admin-row-meta">
        Build: Fruit ${escapeHtml(item.fruit || '-')} | Style ${escapeHtml(item.fighting_style || '-')} | Sword ${escapeHtml(item.sword || '-')} | Gun ${escapeHtml(item.gun || '-')}<br>

        Phone: ${escapeHtml(item.user_phone || '-')} | Web ID: ${escapeHtml(item.client_ip || '-')} | Status: ${escapeHtml(item.status || 'open')}<br>

    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function renderAdminSettings() {
  const themeSelect = document.getElementById('adminThemeSetting');
  const adminCodeInput = document.getElementById('adminCodeSetting');
  const fullscreenSelect = document.getElementById('adminRequireFullscreen');
  const updatingSelect = document.getElementById('adminUpdatingServer');
  const updatingMessage = document.getElementById('adminUpdatingServerMessage');
  const whatsappGroupUrl = document.getElementById('adminWhatsappGroupUrl');
  const hasUnsaved = (themeSelect && themeSelect.classList.contains('admin-missing')) ||
    (adminCodeInput && adminCodeInput.classList.contains('admin-missing')) ||
    (fullscreenSelect && fullscreenSelect.classList.contains('admin-missing')) ||
    (updatingSelect && updatingSelect.classList.contains('admin-missing')) ||
    (updatingMessage && updatingMessage.classList.contains('admin-missing')) ||
    (whatsappGroupUrl && whatsappGroupUrl.classList.contains('admin-missing'));
  if(hasUnsaved) return;
  const savedTheme = normalizeTheme(localStorage.getItem('ne_admin_theme') || 'light');
  const adminCodeConfig = adminState.appConfig.find(item => item.key === ADMIN_CODE_CONFIG_KEY);
  const fullscreenConfig = adminState.appConfig.find(item => item.key === 'require_fullscreen');
  const updatingConfig = adminState.appConfig.find(item => item.key === 'updating_server_enabled');
  const updatingMessageConfig = adminState.appConfig.find(item => item.key === 'updating_server_message');
  const whatsappGroupConfig = adminState.appConfig.find(item => item.key === 'whatsapp_group_url');
  adminState.savedTheme = savedTheme;
  adminState.savedAdminCode = normalizeAdminCode(adminCodeConfig?.value || '');
  adminState.savedRequireFullscreen = String(
    fullscreenConfig ? (fullscreenConfig.value === true || fullscreenConfig.value === 'true') : true
  );
  adminState.savedUpdatingServer = String(
    updatingConfig ? (updatingConfig.value === true || updatingConfig.value === 'true') : false
  );
  adminState.savedUpdatingServerMessage = String(
    updatingMessageConfig?.value || state.updatingServerMessage
  );
  adminState.savedWhatsappGroupUrl = normalizeWhatsappGroupUrl(whatsappGroupConfig?.value || state.whatsappGroupUrl);
  if(themeSelect) {
    themeSelect.value = savedTheme;
    if(!themeSelect.dataset.unsavedListener) {
      themeSelect.addEventListener('change', markUnsavedAdminSettings);
      themeSelect.dataset.unsavedListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  if(adminCodeInput) {
    adminCodeInput.value = adminState.savedAdminCode;
    if(!adminCodeInput.dataset.unsavedListener) {
      adminCodeInput.addEventListener('input', markUnsavedAdminSettings);
      adminCodeInput.dataset.unsavedListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  if(fullscreenSelect) {
    fullscreenSelect.value = adminState.savedRequireFullscreen;
    if(!fullscreenSelect.dataset.unsavedListener) {
      fullscreenSelect.addEventListener('change', markUnsavedAdminSettings);
      fullscreenSelect.dataset.unsavedListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  if(updatingSelect) {
    updatingSelect.value = adminState.savedUpdatingServer;
    if(!updatingSelect.dataset.unsavedListener) {
      updatingSelect.addEventListener('change', markUnsavedAdminSettings);
      updatingSelect.dataset.unsavedListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  if(updatingMessage) {
    updatingMessage.value = adminState.savedUpdatingServerMessage;
    if(!updatingMessage.dataset.unsavedListener) {
      updatingMessage.addEventListener('input', markUnsavedAdminSettings);
      updatingMessage.dataset.unsavedListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  if(whatsappGroupUrl) {
    whatsappGroupUrl.value = adminState.savedWhatsappGroupUrl;
    if(!whatsappGroupUrl.dataset.unsavedListener) {
      whatsappGroupUrl.addEventListener('input', markUnsavedAdminSettings);
      whatsappGroupUrl.dataset.unsavedListener = 'true';
    }
  }
  markUnsavedAdminSettings();
}

function markUnsavedAdminSettings() {
  const themeSelect = document.getElementById('adminThemeSetting');
  const adminCodeInput = document.getElementById('adminCodeSetting');
  const fullscreenSelect = document.getElementById('adminRequireFullscreen');
  const updatingSelect = document.getElementById('adminUpdatingServer');
  const updatingMessage = document.getElementById('adminUpdatingServerMessage');
  const whatsappGroupUrl = document.getElementById('adminWhatsappGroupUrl');
  themeSelect?.classList.toggle('admin-missing', themeSelect.value !== adminState.savedTheme);
  adminCodeInput?.classList.toggle('admin-missing', normalizeAdminCode(adminCodeInput.value) !== adminState.savedAdminCode);
  fullscreenSelect?.classList.toggle('admin-missing', fullscreenSelect.value !== adminState.savedRequireFullscreen);
  updatingSelect?.classList.toggle('admin-missing', updatingSelect.value !== adminState.savedUpdatingServer);
  updatingMessage?.classList.toggle('admin-missing', updatingMessage.value !== adminState.savedUpdatingServerMessage);
  whatsappGroupUrl?.classList.toggle('admin-missing', normalizeWhatsappGroupUrl(whatsappGroupUrl.value) !== adminState.savedWhatsappGroupUrl);
}

function cancelAdminThemeSetting() {
  const themeSelect = document.getElementById('adminThemeSetting');
  if(themeSelect) themeSelect.value = adminState.savedTheme || 'light';
  applyTheme(themeSelect?.value || 'light');
  markUnsavedAdminSettings();
  showToast('Tema admin dikembalikan');
}

function cancelAdminFullscreenSetting() {
  const fullscreenSelect = document.getElementById('adminRequireFullscreen');
  if(fullscreenSelect) fullscreenSelect.value = adminState.savedRequireFullscreen || 'true';
  markUnsavedAdminSettings();
  showToast('Aturan fullscreen dikembalikan');
}

function adminApplyThemeSetting() {
  const theme = document.getElementById('adminThemeSetting')?.value || 'light';
  applyTheme(theme);
}

function saveAdminPersonalization() {
  const theme = document.getElementById('adminThemeSetting')?.value || 'light';
  localStorage.setItem('ne_admin_theme', theme);
  adminState.savedTheme = theme;
  applyTheme(theme);
  markUnsavedAdminSettings();
  showToast('Tema admin tersimpan');
}

async function saveAppConfig() {
  const adminCode = normalizeAdminCode(document.getElementById('adminCodeSetting')?.value || '');
  const requireFullscreen = document.getElementById('adminRequireFullscreen')?.value === 'true';
  const updatingServer = document.getElementById('adminUpdatingServer')?.value === 'true';
  const updatingMessage = document.getElementById('adminUpdatingServerMessage')?.value.trim() ||
    'Server sedang diupdate untuk proyek baru. Sementara hanya member whitelist yang bisa memakai fitur.';
  const whatsappGroupUrl = normalizeWhatsappGroupUrl(document.getElementById('adminWhatsappGroupUrl')?.value || '');
  if(!adminCode) {
    showToast('Kode admin wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!whatsappGroupUrl) {
    showToast('Link grup WhatsApp wajib valid', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Menyimpan aturan...');
  try {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('app_config').upsert([
      { key: ADMIN_CODE_CONFIG_KEY, value: adminCode, updated_at: now },
      { key: 'require_fullscreen', value: requireFullscreen, updated_at: now },
      { key: 'updating_server_enabled', value: updatingServer, updated_at: now },
      { key: 'updating_server_message', value: updatingMessage, updated_at: now },
      { key: 'whatsapp_group_url', value: whatsappGroupUrl, updated_at: now }
    ], { onConflict: 'key' });
    if(error) throw error;
    state.requireFullscreen = requireFullscreen;
    state.updatingServer = updatingServer;
    state.updatingServerMessage = updatingMessage;
    state.whatsappGroupUrl = whatsappGroupUrl;
    adminState.savedAdminCode = adminCode;
    adminState.savedRequireFullscreen = String(requireFullscreen);
    adminState.savedUpdatingServer = String(updatingServer);
    adminState.savedUpdatingServerMessage = updatingMessage;
    adminState.savedWhatsappGroupUrl = whatsappGroupUrl;
    updateFullscreenGate();
    updateServerMessageUI();
    await refreshAdminData(true);
    markUnsavedAdminSettings();
    showToast('Aturan pengguna tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal simpan app config:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan aturan', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminResetAllDatabase() {
  const confirmInput = document.getElementById('adminResetConfirm');
  const typed = (confirmInput?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if(typed !== 'RESET ALL') {
    showToast('Ketik RESET ALL dulu untuk konfirmasi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!(await appConfirm('Reset semua data database dari awal? User, pending, giveaway, shop, materi custom, report, chat, PvP, rating, whitelist, dan ban akan dibersihkan.', 'Reset All'))) return;
  const adminCode = normalizeAdminCode(await appPrompt('Masukkan kode admin untuk reset database.', 'Kode Admin', 'Kode admin') || '');
  if(!adminCode) return;
  showLoading('Reset database...');
  try {
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    const { data, error } = await sbClient.rpc('reset_neverera_database', { confirm_admin_code: adminCode });
    if(error) throw error;
    if(confirmInput) confirmInput.value = '';
    Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(key => {
      if(key.startsWith('ne_') && !['ne_web_device_id','ne_admin_session','ne_admin_theme'].includes(key)) {
        localStorage.removeItem(key);
      }
    });
    state.phone = '';
    state.uniqueCode = '';
    state.displayName = '';
    state.savedSettings = null;
    materialState.completed = [];
    await refreshAdminData(true);
    showToast(data?.message || 'Database sudah direset dari awal');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Reset all database error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal reset database. Pastikan function SQL sudah dijalankan.', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function normalizeMaterialsFromRows(rows, includeInactive = false) {
  const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  return source
    .filter(item => item && (includeInactive || item.is_active !== false))
    .map(item => {
      const content = item.content || '';
      const chapters = normalizeMaterialChapters(item.chapters, content);
      return {
        id: Number(item.id),
        title: item.title || `Materi ${item.id}`,
        desc: item.desc || item.description || '',
        content,
        chapters,
        order_num: Number(item.order_num || item.id || 0),
        chapter_id: item.chapter_id === null || item.chapter_id === undefined ? null : Number(item.chapter_id),
        chapter_order: Number(item.chapter_order || item.order_num || item.id || 0),
        is_active: item.is_active !== false
      };
    })
    .filter(item => Number.isInteger(item.id) && item.id > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.order_num || a.id) - (b.order_num || b.id));
}

function normalizeMaterialChapterRows(rows, includeInactive = false) {
  const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  return source
    .filter(item => item && (includeInactive || item.is_active !== false))
    .map(item => ({
      id: Number(item.id),
      title: item.title || `Bab ${item.id}`,
      order_num: Number(item.order_num || item.id || 0),
      is_active: item.is_active !== false
    }))
    .filter(item => Number.isInteger(item.id) && item.id > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.order_num || a.id) - (b.order_num || b.id));
}

function normalizeMaterialChapters(chapters, fallbackContent = '') {
  let source = chapters;
  if(typeof source === 'string') {
    try { source = JSON.parse(source); } catch(e) { source = []; }
  }
  const rows = Array.isArray(source) ? source : [];
  const normalized = rows
    .map((chapter, index) => ({
      title: String(chapter?.title || `Bab ${index + 1}`).trim() || `Bab ${index + 1}`,
      content: String(chapter?.content || chapter?.body || '').trim()
    }))
    .filter(chapter => chapter.title || chapter.content);
  if(normalized.length) return normalized;
  const fallback = String(fallbackContent || '').trim();
  return fallback ? [{ title: 'Bab 1', content: fallback }] : [];
}

async function loadMaterialCatalog() {
  if(!sbClient) {
    MATERI_DATA = [...DEFAULT_MATERI_DATA];
    MATERIAL_CHAPTERS = [];
    return MATERI_DATA;
  }
  try {
    const [materialRes, chapterRes] = await Promise.all([
      sbClient
        .from('materials')
        .select('*')
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .order('order_num', { ascending: true })
        .order('id', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient
        .from('material_chapters')
        .select('*')
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .order('order_num', { ascending: true })
        .order('id', { ascending: true })
    ]);
    if(materialRes.error) throw materialRes.error;
    if(chapterRes.error) {
      console.warn('Gagal memuat bab materi:', chapterRes.error);
      MATERIAL_CHAPTERS = [];
    } else {
      MATERIAL_CHAPTERS = normalizeMaterialChapterRows(chapterRes.data || []);
    }
    const materials = normalizeMaterialsFromRows(materialRes.data || []);
    MATERI_DATA = materials.length ? materials : [...DEFAULT_MATERI_DATA];
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal memuat materi dari Supabase:', e);
    MATERI_DATA = [...DEFAULT_MATERI_DATA];
    MATERIAL_CHAPTERS = [];
  }
  return MATERI_DATA;
}

async function loadAdminMaterialChapters() {
  adminState.materialChapters = [];
  MATERIAL_CHAPTERS = [];
  if(!sbClient) return;
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('material_chapters')
      .select('*')
      .order('order_num', { ascending: true })
      .order('id', { ascending: true });
    if(error) throw error;
    adminState.materialChapters = normalizeMaterialChapterRows(data || [], true);
    MATERIAL_CHAPTERS = adminState.materialChapters.filter(item => item.is_active !== false);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal memuat bab materi admin:', e);
  }
}

function renderAdminMaterials(updateChapterControls = true) {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminMaterialsList');
  if(updateChapterControls) {
    renderAdminMaterialChapterList();
    renderAdminChapterMaterialPicker();
  }
  if(!el) return;
  const rows = adminState.materials.length ? adminState.materials : MATERI_DATA;
  if(!rows.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada materi.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = rows.map(item => `
    <div class="admin-row">
      <div class="admin-row-title">${item.is_active === false ? '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash" title="Nonaktif"></i> ' : ''}${escapeHtml(item.title || `Materi ${item.id}`)}</div>
      <div class="admin-row-meta">ID: ${escapeHtml(String(item.id || '-'))} | Urutan: ${escapeHtml(String(item.order_num || item.id || '-'))} | Bab: ${escapeHtml(getMaterialChapterTitle(item))}<br>${escapeHtml(item.desc || '')}</div>
      <div class="admin-actions">
        <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminFillMaterial('${escapeHtml(String(item.id || ''))}')">Edit</button>
        <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteMaterial('${escapeHtml(String(item.id || ''))}')">Hapus</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function getMaterialChapterTitle(material) {
  const chapter = adminState.materialChapters.find(item => Number(item.id) === Number(material?.chapter_id));
  return chapter?.title || 'Tanpa Bab';
}

function renderAdminMaterialChapterList() {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminMaterialChapterList');
  if(!el) return;
  if(!adminState.materialChapters.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada bab. Buat bab dulu, lalu centang materi yang masuk.');
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = adminState.materialChapters.map(chapter => {
    const count = adminState.materials.filter(material => Number(material.chapter_id) === Number(chapter.id)).length;
    return `
      <div class="admin-row">
        <div class="admin-row-title">${chapter.is_active === false ? '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash" title="Nonaktif"></i> ' : ''}${escapeHtml(chapter.title || `Bab ${chapter.id}`)}</div>
        <div class="admin-row-meta">ID: ${escapeHtml(String(chapter.id))} | Urutan: ${escapeHtml(String(chapter.order_num || 0))} | ${count} materi</div>
        <div class="admin-actions">
          <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminFillMaterialChapter('${escapeHtml(String(chapter.id))}')">Edit Isi Bab</button>
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeleteMaterialChapter('${escapeHtml(String(chapter.id))}')">Hapus Bab</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderAdminChapterMaterialPicker() {
  const picker = document.getElementById('adminChapterMaterialPicker');
  if(!picker) return;
  const rows = adminState.materials.length ? adminState.materials : MATERI_DATA;
  if(!rows.length) {
    picker.innerHTML = '<div class="admin-empty">Buat materi dulu, baru bisa dicentang masuk bab.</div>';
    return;
  }
  const chapterId = getSelectedAdminChapterId();
  picker.innerHTML = rows.map(material => {
    const checked = chapterId && Number(material.chapter_id) === chapterId;
    const currentChapter = getMaterialChapterTitle(material);
    return `
      <label class="admin-material-pick">
        <input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(String(material.id))}" ${checked ? 'checked' : ''}>
        <span>
          <span class="admin-material-pick-title">${escapeHtml(material.title || `Materi ${material.id}`)}</span>
          <span class="admin-material-pick-meta">ID ${escapeHtml(String(material.id))} · ${escapeHtml(currentChapter)}</span>
        </span>
      </label>
    `;
  }).join('');
}
