function getAdminUserWebId(phone = '') {
  const user = adminState.users.find(item => String(item.phone || '') === String(phone || ''));
  return user?.ip_address || '';
}

async function loadAdminPvpData({ cleanup = true, render = true } = {}) {
  if(!sbClient) return;
  if(cleanup) await cleanupStalePvpRecords();
  const [queueRes, matchRes, messageRes, reportRes] = await Promise.all([
    sbClient.from('pvp_queue').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
    sbClient.from('pvp_matches').select('*').order('started_at', { ascending: false }),
    sbClient.from('pvp_match_messages').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true }).order('id', { ascending: true }),
    sbClient.from('pvp_match_reports').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
  ]);
  if(queueRes.error) throw queueRes.error;
  if(matchRes.error) throw matchRes.error;
  if(messageRes.error) throw messageRes.error;
  if(reportRes.error) throw reportRes.error;
  adminState.pvpQueue = queueRes.data || [];
  adminState.pvpMatches = matchRes.data || [];
  adminState.pvpMatchMessages = messageRes.data || [];
  adminState.pvpMatchReports = reportRes.data || [];
  if(render) renderAdminPvp();
}

async function adminRefreshPvp(event) {
  event?.preventDefault?.();
  event?.stopPropagation?.();
  if(!canRunDataRequest('admin_pvp_refresh')) return;
  const btn = event?.currentTarget;
  btn?.classList.add('refreshing');
  if(btn) btn.disabled = true;
  try {
    await loadAdminPvpData({ cleanup: true, render: true });
    showToast('PvP admin diperbarui');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Admin PvP refresh error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal refresh PvP admin', 'error');
  } finally {
    btn?.classList.remove('refreshing');
    if(btn) btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

function renderAdminPvp() {
  const el = document.getElementById('adminPvpList');
  if(!el) return;
  const queue = adminState.pvpQueue || [];
  const matches = adminState.pvpMatches || [];
  const reports = adminState.pvpMatchReports || [];
  if(!queue.length && !matches.length && !reports.length) {
    el.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada antrean, match, atau report PvP.');
    return;
  }

  const queueHtml = queue.length ? `
    <div class="admin-row">
      <div class="admin-row-title">Antrean PvP (${queue.length})</div>
      ${queue.map(entry => `
        <div class="admin-row" style="margin-top:8px">
          <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(entry.display_name || entry.user_phone || 'Member')} - ${escapeHtml(entry.status || 'waiting')}</div>
          <div class="admin-row-meta">
            Phone: ${escapeHtml(entry.user_phone || '-')} | Web ID: ${escapeHtml(getAdminUserWebId(entry.user_phone) || '-')}<br>
            Masuk: ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(entry.created_at) || '-')} | Match ID: ${escapeHtml(entry.matched_id || '-')}
          </div>
          <div class="admin-actions">
            <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeletePvpQueue('${escapeHtml(String(entry.id || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Hapus Queue</button>
            <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminBanPvpUser('${escapeHtml(String(entry.user_phone || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-ban"></i> Ban User</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  ` : '';

  const matchHtml = matches.length ? matches.map(match => {
    const messages = (adminState.pvpMatchMessages || []).filter(message => String(message.match_id) === String(match.id));
    const matchReports = reports.filter(report => String(report.match_id) === String(match.id));
    const messageHtml = messages.length
      ? messages.map(message => `
        <div class="admin-row" style="margin-top:8px">
          <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(message.display_name || message.user_phone || 'Member')}</div>
          <div class="admin-row-meta">
            ${escapeHtml(message.body || '')}<br>
            Phone: ${escapeHtml(message.user_phone || '-')} | ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(message.created_at) || '-')}
          </div>
          <div class="admin-actions">
            <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeletePvpMessage('${escapeHtml(String(message.id || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Hapus Chat</button>
            <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminBanPvpUser('${escapeHtml(String(message.user_phone || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-ban"></i> Ban User</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('')
      : '<div class="admin-empty" style="margin-top:8px">Belum ada chat di match ini.</div>';
    const reportHtml = matchReports.length
      ? matchReports.map(report => `
        <div class="admin-row" style="margin-top:8px">
          <div class="admin-row-title">Report: ${escapeHtml(report.reporter_name || report.reporter_phone || 'Member')} -> ${escapeHtml(report.reported_name || report.reported_phone || 'Member')}</div>
          <div class="admin-row-meta">
            Alasan: ${escapeHtml(report.reason || '-')}<br>
            Reporter: ${escapeHtml(report.reporter_phone || '-')} | Reported: ${escapeHtml(report.reported_phone || '-')}<br>
            Waktu: ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(report.created_at) || '-')}
          </div>
          <div class="admin-actions">
            <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminBanPvpUser('${escapeHtml(String(report.reported_phone || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-ban"></i> Ban Reported</button>
            <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeletePvpReport('${escapeHtml(String(report.id || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Hapus Report</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('')
      : '<div class="admin-empty" style="margin-top:8px">Belum ada report untuk match ini.</div>';
    return `
      <div class="admin-row">
        <div class="admin-row-title">Match ${escapeHtml(match.status || 'active')} - ${escapeHtml(match.requester_name || 'Member 1')} vs ${escapeHtml(match.opponent_name || 'Member 2')}</div>
        <div class="admin-row-meta">
          ID: ${escapeHtml(match.id || '-')}<br>
          Requester: ${escapeHtml(match.requester_phone || '-')} (${match.requester_accepted ? 'ACC' : 'Belum ACC'}) | Web ID: ${escapeHtml(getAdminUserWebId(match.requester_phone) || '-')}<br>
          Opponent: ${escapeHtml(match.opponent_phone || '-')} (${match.opponent_accepted ? 'ACC' : 'Belum ACC'}) | Web ID: ${escapeHtml(getAdminUserWebId(match.opponent_phone) || '-')}<br>
          Mulai: ${escapeHtml(formatDateTime(match.started_at) || '-')} | Chat: ${messages.length} | Report: ${matchReports.length}
        </div>
        <div class="admin-actions">
          <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminCompletePvpMatch('${escapeHtml(String(match.id || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-check-double"></i> Set Selesai</button>
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeletePvpMatchMessages('${escapeHtml(String(match.id || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-comment-slash"></i> Hapus Semua Chat</button>
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminDeletePvpMatch('${escapeHtml(String(match.id || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Hapus Match</button>
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminBanPvpUser('${escapeHtml(String(match.requester_phone || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-ban"></i> Ban Requester</button>
          <button class="admin-btn danger" onclick="adminBanPvpUser('${escapeHtml(String(match.opponent_phone || ''))}')"><i class="fas fa-ban"></i> Ban Opponent</button>
        </div>
        ${reportHtml}
        ${messageHtml}
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('') : '';

  el.innerHTML = `${queueHtml}${matchHtml}`;
}

async function adminApprovePending(phone) {
  const pending = adminState.pendingUsers.find(user => user.phone === phone);
  if(!pending) return;
  showLoading('Acc user...');
  try {
    let uniqueCode = pending.unique_code || generateUniqueCode();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    let pendingUpdate = await sbClient.from('pending_users')
      .update({ verified: true, unique_code: uniqueCode })
      .eq('phone', phone);
    if(pendingUpdate.error && pendingUpdate.error.code === '23505' && /unique_code/i.test(getSupabaseErrorText(pendingUpdate.error))) {
      uniqueCode = generateUniqueCode();
      pendingUpdate = await sbClient.from('pending_users')
        .update({ verified: true, unique_code: uniqueCode })
        .eq('phone', phone);
    }
    if(pendingUpdate.error && isMissingSpecificColumnError(pendingUpdate.error, 'unique_code')) {
      pendingUpdate = await sbClient.from('pending_users')
        .update({ verified: true })
        .eq('phone', phone);
    }
    if(pendingUpdate.error) throw pendingUpdate.error;
    const approvedUserPayload = {
      phone,
      unique_code: uniqueCode,
      ip_address: pending.ip_address || null,
      display_name: pending.display_name || null,
      material_completed: [],
      material_progress_percent: 0,
      last_material_id: null,
      updated_at: now
    };
    let { error } = await upsertUserWithSchemaFallback(approvedUserPayload);
    if(error && error.code === '23505' && /unique_code/i.test(getSupabaseErrorText(error))) {
      uniqueCode = generateUniqueCode();
      approvedUserPayload.unique_code = uniqueCode;
      await sbClient.from('pending_users')
        .update({ unique_code: uniqueCode })
        .eq('phone', phone);
      const retry = await upsertUserWithSchemaFallback(approvedUserPayload);
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(error && error.code === '23505' && /ip_address|users_ip_address_idx/i.test(getSupabaseErrorText(error))) {
      delete approvedUserPayload.ip_address;
      const retry = await upsertUserWithSchemaFallback(approvedUserPayload);
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(error) throw error;
    const deleteResult = await sbClient.from('pending_users').delete().eq('phone', phone);
    if(deleteResult.error) {
      console.warn('Pending cleanup failed after approve:', deleteResult.error);
      showToast('User di-acc, tapi pending belum terhapus otomatis', 'warn');
    } else {
      showToast('User berhasil di-acc');
    }
    try {
      await refreshAdminData();
    } catch(refreshError) {
      console.warn('Refresh after approve failed:', refreshError);
      showToast('User sudah di-acc. Refresh admin gagal, klik Refresh.', 'warn');
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Approve error:', e);
    const text = getSupabaseErrorText(e);
    showToast(text ? `Gagal acc user: ${text.slice(0, 90)}` : 'Gagal acc user', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminTogglePending(phone, verified) {
  showLoading('Update pending...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pending_users').update({ verified }).eq('phone', phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    await refreshAdminData();
  } catch(e) {
    showToast('Gagal update pending', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminRejectPending(phone) {
  const pending = adminState.pendingUsers.find(user => user.phone === phone);
  if(!pending) return;
  if(!(await appConfirm('Reject pending user ini tanpa ban Web ID?'))) return;
  showLoading('Reject pending...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pending_users').delete().eq('phone', phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Pending user dihapus tanpa ban');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Reject pending error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal reject pending', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminDeclinePending(phone) {
  const pending = adminState.pendingUsers.find(user => user.phone === phone);
  if(!pending) return;
  if(!(await appConfirm('Decline pending user dan ban Web ID ini?'))) return;
  showLoading('Decline dan ban Web ID...');
  try {
    await banAndDeleteUserRecords(phone, pending.ip_address, `Decline pending user ${phone}`);
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('User ditolak, dihapus, dan Web ID masuk ban list');
  } catch(e) {
    showToast('Gagal decline pending', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function toDateTimeLocalValue(value) {
  if(!value) return '';
  const date = new Date(value);
  if(Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
}

function renderAdminAvatarBorders(updateControls = true) {
  const userSelect = document.getElementById('adminAvatarUserPhone');
  const list = document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderList');
  if(userSelect && updateControls) {
    const current = userSelect.value || '';
    userSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pilih user</option>' + adminState.users.map(user => `
      <option value="${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}">${escapeHtml(user.display_name || formatPhoneDisplay(user.phone || '') || user.phone || 'Member')}</option>
    `).join('');
    if(current && adminState.users.some(user => String(user.phone) === String(current))) {
      userSelect.value = current;
    }
  }
  if(list) {
    if(!adminState.users.length) {
      list.innerHTML = adminEmpty('Belum ada user.');
    } else {
      list.innerHTML = adminState.users.map(user => {
        const border = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(user.avatar_border || 'white');
        const name = user.display_name || formatPhoneDisplay(user.phone || '') || 'Member';
        return `
          <div class="admin-row">
            <div class="admin-row-title">${escapeHtml(name)}</div>
            <div class="admin-row-meta">Phone: ${escapeHtml(formatPhoneDisplay(user.phone || ''))}<br>Border avatar: ${escapeHtml(getNeedHelpAvatarBorderLabel(border))}</div>
            <div class="admin-actions">
              <button class="admin-btn secondary" onclick="adminOpenAvatarBorder('${escapeHtml(user.phone || '')}')">Atur Border</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }
  }
  updateAdminAvatarBorderUI();
}

function getSelectedAdminAvatarUser() {
  const phone = document.getElementById('adminAvatarUserPhone')?.value || '';
  return adminState.users.find(user => String(user.phone || '') === String(phone));
}

function selectAdminAvatarUser() {
  const user = getSelectedAdminAvatarUser();
  const select = document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderValue');
  if(select) select.value = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(user?.avatar_border || 'white');
  updateAdminAvatarBorderUI();
}

function adminOpenAvatarBorder(phone) {
  switchAdminTab('avatarborder');
  const userSelect = document.getElementById('adminAvatarUserPhone');
  if(userSelect) userSelect.value = phone || '';
  selectAdminAvatarUser();
}

function getPhoneVariants(phone) {
  const raw = parsePhoneRaw(phone || '');
  return [...new Set([
    String(phone || '').trim(),
    raw,
    raw ? `+${raw}` : ''
  ].filter(Boolean))];
}

function setAdminAvatarBorder(value) {
  const select = document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderValue');
  if(!select) return;
  select.value = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(value);
  updateAdminAvatarBorderUI();
}

function updateAdminAvatarBorderUI() {
  const select = document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderValue');
  const preview = document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderPreview');
  if(!select || !preview) return;
  const value = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(select.value || 'white');
  const user = getSelectedAdminAvatarUser();
  const name = user?.display_name || user?.phone || 'A';
  select.value = value;
  preview.innerHTML = renderNeedHelpAvatar(name, value);
  document.querySelectorAll('#adminAvatarBorderOptions .admin-avatar-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.avatarBorder === value);
  });
}

async function adminSaveAvatarBorder() {
  const phone = document.getElementById('adminAvatarUserPhone')?.value || '';
  const avatarBorder = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderValue')?.value || 'white');
  if(!phone) {
    showToast('Pilih user dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan border avatar...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .update({ avatar_border: avatarBorder, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('phone', phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    showToast('Border avatar tersimpan');
    await refreshAdminData();
    document.getElementById('adminAvatarUserPhone').value = phone;
    document.getElementById('adminAvatarBorderValue').value = avatarBorder;
    updateAdminAvatarBorderUI();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Avatar border save error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan border avatar', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminFillUser(phone, uniqueCode, theme, ipAddress = '') {
  document.getElementById('adminUserPhone').value = phone;
  document.getElementById('adminUserCode').value = uniqueCode;
  document.getElementById('adminUserIp').value = ipAddress;
  document.getElementById('adminUserTheme').value = normalizeTheme(theme || 'light');
}

function adminCancelUserEdit(silent = false) {
  ['adminUserPhone','adminUserCode','adminUserIp'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) {
      el.value = '';
      el.classList.remove('admin-missing');
    }
  });
  const theme = document.getElementById('adminUserTheme');
  if(theme) {
    theme.value = 'light';
    theme.classList.remove('admin-missing');
  }
  if(!silent) showToast('Edit user dibatalkan');
}

async function adminSaveUser() {
  const phone = parsePhoneRaw(document.getElementById('adminUserPhone').value);
  const uniqueCode = document.getElementById('adminUserCode').value.trim().toUpperCase() || generateUniqueCode();
  const ipAddress = document.getElementById('adminUserIp').value.trim();
  const theme = document.getElementById('adminUserTheme').value;
  if(!phone) {
    showToast('Phone wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan user...');
  try {
    const payload = { phone, unique_code: uniqueCode, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    if(ipAddress) payload.ip_address = ipAddress;
    if(theme) payload.theme = theme;
    let { error } = await sbClient.from('users').upsert(payload, { onConflict: 'phone' });
    if(error) throw error;
    document.getElementById('adminUserPhone').value = '';
    document.getElementById('adminUserCode').value = '';
    document.getElementById('adminUserIp').value = '';
    document.getElementById('adminUserTheme').value = 'light';
    await refreshAdminData();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Save user error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan user', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminBanUser(phone) {
  const user = adminState.users.find(item => item.phone === phone);
  if(!user) return;
  if(!(await appConfirm('Ban user ini dan blokir Web ID-nya?'))) return;
  showLoading('Ban user...');
  try {
    await banAndDeleteUserRecords(phone, user.ip_address, `Ban user ${phone}`);
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('User dihapus dan Web ID masuk ban list');
  } catch(e) {
    showToast('Gagal ban user', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminSaveWhitelistMember() {
  const selectedPhone = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistUser')?.value || '';
  const manualPhone = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistPhone')?.value || '';
  const phone = parsePhoneRaw(manualPhone || selectedPhone);
  const note = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistNote')?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!phone) {
    showToast('Nomor whitelist wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const user = adminState.users.find(item => String(item.phone || '') === String(phone));
  showLoading('Simpan whitelist...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('server_update_whitelist').upsert({
      phone,
      display_name: user?.display_name || null,
      note: note || null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    }, { onConflict: 'phone' });
    if(error) throw error;
    document.getElementById('adminWhitelistPhone').value = '';
    document.getElementById('adminWhitelistNote').value = '';
    const select = document.getElementById('adminWhitelistUser');
    if(select) select.value = '';
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Member masuk white list updating server');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal simpan whitelist:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan white list', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminResetMaterialProgress(phone) {
  if(!phone || !(await appConfirm('Reset semua progress materi user ini?'))) return;
  showLoading('Reset progress materi...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .update({
        material_completed: [],
        material_progress_percent: 0,
        last_material_id: null,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('phone', phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    if(String(state.phone || '') === String(phone || '')) {
      materialState.completed = [];
      saveLocalMaterialProgress();
      if(state.currentPage === 'materi') renderMaterialsPath();
    }
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Progress materi user direset');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Reset material progress error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal reset progress materi', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

  showLoading('Reset semua progress materi...');
  try {

      .update({
        material_completed: [],
        material_progress_percent: 0,
        last_material_id: null,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .not('phone', 'is', null);
    if(error) throw error;
    materialState.completed = [];
    saveLocalMaterialProgress();
    if(state.currentPage === 'materi') renderMaterialsPath();

    showToast('Semua progress materi user direset');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Reset all material progress error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal reset semua progress materi', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function banIpAddress(ipAddress, reason = '') {
  if(!ipAddress) return false;
  const { error } = await sbClient.from('banned_ips').upsert({
    ip_address: ipAddress,
    reason,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString()
  }, { onConflict: 'ip_address' });
  if(error) throw error;
  return true;
}

async function getPhonesForBannedIdentity({ phone = '', ipAddress = '' } = {}) {
  const phones = new Set();
  const normalizedPhone = parsePhoneRaw(phone);
  if(normalizedPhone) phones.add(normalizedPhone);
  const webIds = ipAddress
    ? [ipAddress]
    : [state.clientIp, previousWebDeviceId].filter(Boolean);
  if(!sbClient || !webIds.length) return [...phones];
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .select('phone')
      .in('ip_address', webIds);
    if(error) throw error;
    (data || []).forEach(user => {
      const userPhone = parsePhoneRaw(user.phone);
      if(userPhone) phones.add(userPhone);
    });
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal mencari user dari Web ID banned:', e);
  }
  return [...phones];
}

async function cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhone(phone = '') {
  const cleanedPhone = parsePhoneRaw(phone);
  if(!sbClient || !cleanedPhone) return;
  const phoneVariants = getPhoneVariants(cleanedPhone);
  try {
    const { data: posts, error: postLoadError } = await sbClient.from('help_posts')
      .select('id')
      .in('user_phone', phoneVariants);
    if(postLoadError) throw postLoadError;
    const postIds = (posts || []).map(post => post.id).filter(Boolean);
    if(postIds.length) {
      const deletePostMessages = await sbClient.from('help_messages').delete().in('post_id', postIds);
      if(deletePostMessages.error) throw deletePostMessages.error;
    }
    const deleteOwnMessages = await sbClient.from('help_messages').delete().in('user_phone', phoneVariants);
    if(deleteOwnMessages.error) throw deleteOwnMessages.error;
    const deletePosts = await sbClient.from('help_posts').delete().in('user_phone', phoneVariants);
    if(deletePosts.error) throw deletePosts.error;

    state.needHelpPosts = (state.needHelpPosts || []).filter(post => !phoneVariants.includes(String(post.user_phone || '')));
    state.needHelpMessages = (state.needHelpMessages || []).filter(message => !phoneVariants.includes(String(message.user_phone || '')));
    if(state.activeNeedHelpPostId && postIds.some(id => String(id) === String(state.activeNeedHelpPostId))) {
      closeNeedHelpThread();
    } else if(state.currentPage === 'needhelp') {
      renderNeedHelpPosts();
      renderNeedHelpMessages();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn(`Cleanup Need & Help user ${cleanedPhone} gagal:`, e);
  }
}

async function cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhones(phones = []) {
  const uniquePhones = [...new Set((phones || []).map(phone => parsePhoneRaw(phone)).filter(Boolean))];
  for(const phone of uniquePhones) {
    await cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhone(phone);
  }
}

async function cleanupBannedUserActivity({ phone = '', ipAddress = '' } = {}) {
  if(!sbClient) return;
  const phones = await getPhonesForBannedIdentity({ phone, ipAddress });
  if(!phones.length) return;

  for(const bannedPhone of phones) {
    try {
      await cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhone(bannedPhone);

      const deleteQueue = await sbClient.from('pvp_queue').delete().eq('user_phone', bannedPhone);
      if(deleteQueue.error) throw deleteQueue.error;
      const { data: matches, error: matchLoadError } = await sbClient.from('pvp_matches')
        .select('id')
        .or(`requester_phone.eq.${bannedPhone},opponent_phone.eq.${bannedPhone}`);
      if(matchLoadError) throw matchLoadError;
      const matchIds = (matches || []).map(match => match.id).filter(Boolean);
      if(matchIds.length) {
        const deleteMessages = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_messages').delete().in('match_id', matchIds);
        if(deleteMessages.error) throw deleteMessages.error;
        const deleteReports = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_reports').delete().in('match_id', matchIds);
        if(deleteReports.error) throw deleteReports.error;
        const deleteMatches = await sbClient.from('pvp_matches').delete().in('id', matchIds);
        if(deleteMatches.error) throw deleteMatches.error;
      }
      const deleteUserReports = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_reports')
        .delete()
        .or(`reporter_phone.eq.${bannedPhone},reported_phone.eq.${bannedPhone}`);
      if(deleteUserReports.error) throw deleteUserReports.error;
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn(`Cleanup data banned user ${bannedPhone} gagal:`, e);
    }
  }
}

async function banAndDeleteUserRecords(phone, ipAddress, reason = '') {
  if(!ipAddress) throw new Error('Web ID user kosong, tidak bisa masuk ban list');
  await banIpAddress(ipAddress, reason);
  await cleanupBannedUserActivity({ phone, ipAddress });
  const pendingDelete = await sbClient.from('pending_users').delete().eq('phone', phone);
  if(pendingDelete.error) throw pendingDelete.error;
  const userDelete = await sbClient.from('users').delete().eq('phone', phone);
  if(userDelete.error) throw userDelete.error;
}

async function adminDeleteUser(phone) {
  if(!phone || !(await appConfirm('Hapus user ini dari database?'))) return;
  showLoading('Menghapus user...');
  try {
    await cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhone(phone);
    const pendingDelete = await sbClient.from('pending_users').delete().eq('phone', phone);
    if(pendingDelete.error) throw pendingDelete.error;
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('users').delete().eq('phone', phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('User berhasil dihapus');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Delete user error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal hapus user', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function submitBanAppeal() {
  if(!sbClient) {
    showToast('Database belum aktif', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const contact = document.getElementById('appealContactInput')?.value.trim();
  const message = document.getElementById('appealMessageInput')?.value.trim();
  const ipAddress = state.clientIp || await ensureClientIp();
  if(!contact || !message) {
    showToast('Kontak dan alasan appeal wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Mengirim appeal...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('ban_appeals').insert({
      ip_address: ipAddress,
      contact,
      message,
      status: 'pending',
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    document.getElementById('appealContactInput').value = '';
    document.getElementById('appealMessageInput').value = '';
    showToast('Appeal terkirim. Tunggu review admin.');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Appeal error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal mengirim appeal', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminUpdateAppeal(id, status) {
  showLoading('Update appeal...');
  try {
    const appeal = adminState.appeals.find(item => item.id === id);
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('ban_appeals')
      .update({ status, reviewed_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('id', id);
    if(error) throw error;
    if(status === 'approved' && appeal?.ip_address) {
      await sbClient.from('banned_ips').delete().eq('ip_address', appeal.ip_address);
    }
    if(status === 'rejected' && appeal?.ip_address) {
      await banIpAddress(appeal.ip_address, `Appeal rejected: ${appeal.contact || 'tanpa kontak'}`);
    }
    await refreshAdminData();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Appeal update error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal update appeal', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

}

async function adminSaveGiveaway() {
  const id = document.getElementById('adminGiveawayId').value.trim();
  const title = document.getElementById('adminGiveawayTitle').value.trim();
  const description = document.getElementById('adminGiveawayDesc').value.trim();
  const prize = document.getElementById('adminGiveawayPrize').value.trim() || 'Lampu neon';
  const isActive = document.getElementById('adminGiveawayActive').value === 'true';
  const endsAt = document.getElementById('adminGiveawayEnds').value;
  if(!title) {
    showToast('Judul giveaway wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan giveaway...');
  try {
    const payload = {
      title,
      description,
      prize,
      is_active: isActive,
      ends_at: endsAt ? new Date(endsAt).toISOString() : null
    };
    const result = id
      ? await sbClient.from('giveaways').update(payload).eq('id', id)
      : await sbClient.from('giveaways').insert({ ...payload, starts_at: new Date().toISOString() });
    if(result.error) throw result.error;
    adminCancelGiveawayEdit(true);
    await refreshAdminData();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Giveaway save error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan giveaway', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminFillShopProduct(id) {
  const item = adminState.shopProducts.find(product => String(product.id) === String(id));
  if(!item) return;
  document.getElementById('adminShopId').value = item.id || '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopName').value = item.name || '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopCategory').value = item.category || '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopPrice').value = item.price || 0;
  document.getElementById('adminShopImageUrl').value = item.image_url || '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopImageMode').value = item.image_url ? 'url' : 'icon';
  document.getElementById('adminShopIcon').value = item.icon || '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopBadge').value = item.badge || '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopStock').value = item.stock ?? '';
  document.getElementById('adminShopActive').value = String(item.is_active !== false);
  document.getElementById('adminShopDesc').value = item.description || '';
  updateAdminShopImageMode();
}

function adminCancelShopEdit(silent = false) {
  ['adminShopId','adminShopName','adminShopCategory','adminShopPrice','adminShopImageUrl','adminShopIcon','adminShopBadge','adminShopStock','adminShopDesc'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) {
      el.value = '';
      el.classList.remove('admin-missing');
    }
  });
  const modeEl = document.getElementById('adminShopImageMode');
  if(modeEl) modeEl.value = 'url';
  const fileEl = document.getElementById('adminShopImageFile');
  if(fileEl) fileEl.value = '';
  updateAdminShopFileName();
  updateAdminShopImageMode();
  const active = document.getElementById('adminShopActive');
  if(active) {
    active.value = 'true';
    active.classList.remove('admin-missing');
  }
  if(!silent) showToast('Edit produk dibatalkan');
}

function getNextShopOrderNum(id = '') {
  if(id) {
    const current = adminState.shopProducts.find(item => String(item.id) === String(id));
    if(current?.order_num !== undefined && current?.order_num !== null) return Number(current.order_num) || 0;
  }
  const maxOrder = adminState.shopProducts.reduce((max, item) => Math.max(max, Number(item.order_num || 0)), 0);
  return maxOrder + 1;
}

function updateAdminShopImageMode() {
  const mode = document.getElementById('adminShopImageMode')?.value || 'url';
  document.getElementById('adminShopImageUrlWrap')?.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'url');
  document.getElementById('adminShopImageFileWrap')?.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'file');
}

function updateAdminShopFileName() {
  const file = document.getElementById('adminShopImageFile')?.files?.[0];
  const nameEl = document.getElementById('adminShopImageFileName');
  if(nameEl) nameEl.textContent = file ? file.name : 'Belum ada file dipilih';
  if(file) {
    const modeEl = document.getElementById('adminShopImageMode');
    if(modeEl) modeEl.value = 'file';
    updateAdminShopImageMode();
  }
}

function updateAdminCarouselImageMode() {
  const mode = document.getElementById('adminCarouselImageMode')?.value || 'url';
  document.getElementById('adminCarouselUrlWrap')?.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'url');
  document.getElementById('adminCarouselFileWrap')?.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'file');
}

function updateAdminCarouselFileName() {
  const file = document.getElementById('adminCarouselFile')?.files?.[0];
  const nameEl = document.getElementById('adminCarouselFileName');
  if(nameEl) nameEl.textContent = file ? file.name : 'Belum ada file dipilih';
  if(file) {
    const modeEl = document.getElementById('adminCarouselImageMode');
    if(modeEl) modeEl.value = 'file';
    updateAdminCarouselImageMode();
  }
}

function validateCarouselImageFile(file) {
  if(!file) return;
  if(!String(file.type || '').startsWith('image/')) {
    throw new Error('File carousel harus berupa gambar');
  }
  if(file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
    throw new Error('Ukuran gambar carousel maksimal 2MB');
  }
}

function ensureSquareImageFile(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    img.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      if(img.naturalWidth !== img.naturalHeight) {
        reject(new Error('Gambar produk harus rasio 1:1'));
        return;
      }
      resolve(true);
    };
    img.onerror = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      reject(new Error('Gagal membaca gambar'));
    };
    img.src = url;
  });
}

function ensureSquareImageUrl(url) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => {
      if(img.naturalWidth !== img.naturalHeight) {
        reject(new Error('URL gambar produk harus rasio 1:1'));
        return;
      }
      resolve(true);
    };
    img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Gagal membaca URL gambar'));
    img.src = url;
  });
}

function fileToBase64(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result).split(',')[1] || '');
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Gagal membaca file gambar'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

function fileToDataUrl(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || ''));
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Gagal membaca file gambar'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

async function uploadImageToImgBB(file) {
  const urls = await uploadImageToImgBBDetails(file);
  return urls.directUrl;
}

function getImgBBUrlInfo(result) {
  const data = result?.data || {};
  const candidates = [
    data.medium?.url,
    data.image?.url,
    data.display_url,
    data.url,
    data.thumb?.url
  ].filter(Boolean);
  const directUrl = candidates[0] || '';
  return { directUrl, candidates };
}

function canLoadImageUrl(url) {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    if(!url) {
      resolve(false);
      return;
    }
    const img = new Image();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      img.onload = null;
      img.onerror = null;
      resolve(false);
    }, 5000);
    img.onload = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      resolve(true);
    };
    img.onerror = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      resolve(false);
    };
    img.src = url;
  });
}

async function pickLoadableImageUrl(candidates = []) {
  const unique = [...new Set(candidates.filter(Boolean))];
  for(const url of unique) {
    if(await canLoadImageUrl(url)) return url;
  }
  return unique[0] || '';
}

async function uploadImageToImgBBDetails(file) {
  const image = await fileToBase64(file);
  const formData = new FormData();
  formData.append('key', IMGBB_API_KEY);
  formData.append('image', image);
  const response = await fetch('https://api.imgbb.com/1/upload', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: formData
  });
  const result = await response.json();
  if(!response.ok || !result?.success) {
    throw new Error(result?.error?.message || 'Upload gambar ImgBB gagal');
  }
  const urls = getImgBBUrlInfo(result);
  urls.directUrl = await pickLoadableImageUrl(urls.candidates);
  if(!urls.directUrl) throw new Error('URL gambar ImgBB kosong');
  return urls;
}

function isMissingShopImageColumnError(error) {
  const message = String(error?.message || error?.details || error || '').toLowerCase();
  return message.includes('image_url') && (
    message.includes('schema cache') ||
    message.includes('could not find') ||
    message.includes('column')
  );
}

async function saveShopProductPayload(id, payload) {
  const query = id
    ? sbClient.from('shop_products').update(payload).eq('id', id)
    : sbClient.from('shop_products').insert(payload);
  const result = await query;
  if(result.error && isMissingShopImageColumnError(result.error)) {
    const fallbackPayload = { ...payload };
    delete fallbackPayload.image_url;
    const retry = id
      ? await sbClient.from('shop_products').update(fallbackPayload).eq('id', id)
      : await sbClient.from('shop_products').insert(fallbackPayload);
    if(retry.error) throw retry.error;
    return { savedWithoutImage: true };
  }
  if(result.error) throw result.error;
  return { savedWithoutImage: false };
}

async function adminSaveShopProduct() {
  const id = document.getElementById('adminShopId').value.trim();
  const name = document.getElementById('adminShopName').value.trim();
  const category = document.getElementById('adminShopCategory').value.trim() || 'Lainnya';
  const price = Number(document.getElementById('adminShopPrice').value || 0);
  const imageMode = document.getElementById('adminShopImageMode')?.value || 'url';
  let imageUrl = document.getElementById('adminShopImageUrl').value.trim();
  const imageFile = document.getElementById('adminShopImageFile')?.files?.[0];
  const icon = document.getElementById('adminShopIcon').value.trim() || 'fa-box';
  const badge = document.getElementById('adminShopBadge').value.trim();
  const stockRaw = document.getElementById('adminShopStock').value;
  const orderNum = getNextShopOrderNum(id);
  const isActive = document.getElementById('adminShopActive').value === 'true';
  const description = document.getElementById('adminShopDesc').value.trim();
  if(!name || price < 0) {
    showToast('Nama dan harga produk wajib valid', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan produk shop...');
  try {
    if(imageMode === 'file') {
      if(!imageFile && !id) {
        showToast('Pilih file gambar dulu atau ganti ke URL/icon', 'error');
        return;
      }
      if(imageFile) imageUrl = await fileToDataUrl(imageFile);
    } else if(imageMode === 'icon') {
      imageUrl = '';
    }
    const payload = {
      name,
      category,
      price,
      image_url: imageUrl || null,
      icon,
      badge: badge || null,
      stock: stockRaw === '' ? null : Number(stockRaw),
      order_num: orderNum,
      is_active: isActive,
      description: description || null,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    const result = await saveShopProductPayload(id, payload);
    adminCancelShopEdit(true);
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast(result.savedWithoutImage
      ? 'Produk tersimpan, tapi gambar belum tersimpan karena kolom image_url belum ada di Supabase'
      : 'Produk shop tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Shop save error:', e);
    showToast(e?.message || 'Gagal simpan produk shop', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminPickWinner(giveawayId, userPhone) {
  const giveaway = adminState.giveaways.find(item => item.id === giveawayId);
  const winnerUser = adminState.users.find(user => user.phone === userPhone);
  const winnerDisplay = winnerUser?.winner_display_mode === 'phone'
    ? formatPhoneDisplay(userPhone)
    : (winnerUser?.display_name || 'Member');
  if(!giveawayId || !userPhone) return;
  const activeWinners = adminState.winners.filter(w => w.giveaway_id === giveawayId && isCurrentGiveawayWinner(w, giveaway));
  if(activeWinners.some(w => w.user_phone === userPhone)) {
    showToast('Peserta ini sudah jadi pemenang giveaway tersebut', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!(await appConfirm(`Jadikan ${winnerDisplay} sebagai pemenang ${giveaway?.title || 'giveaway'}?`))) return;
  showLoading('Menambahkan pemenang...');
  try {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('giveaway_winners').insert({
      giveaway_id: giveawayId,
      user_phone: userPhone,
      winner_name: winnerDisplay,
      prize_note: giveaway?.prize || null,
      announced_at: now
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    const { error: giveawayError } = await sbClient
      .from('giveaways')
      .update({ is_active: false })
      .eq('id', giveawayId);
    if(giveawayError) throw giveawayError;
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Pemenang berhasil ditambahkan, giveaway otomatis selesai');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Pick winner error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal menambahkan pemenang', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminRandomWinner(giveawayId) {
  const giveaway = adminState.giveaways.find(item => item.id === giveawayId);
  const pickedPhones = new Set(adminState.winners
    .filter(w => w.giveaway_id === giveawayId && isCurrentGiveawayWinner(w, giveaway))
    .map(w => w.user_phone));
  const entries = adminState.entries.filter(entry => entry.giveaway_id === giveawayId && !pickedPhones.has(entry.user_phone));
  if(!entries.length) {
    showToast('Belum ada peserta baru untuk giveaway ini', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const winner = entries[Math.floor(Math.random() * entries.length)];
  await adminPickWinner(giveawayId, winner.user_phone);
}

async function adminDeleteWinner(id, giveawayId = '', userPhone = '') {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Hapus kemenangan peserta ini?'))) return;
  showLoading('Menghapus kemenangan...');
  try {
    let query = sbClient.from('giveaway_winners').delete();
    if(id) {
      query = query.eq('id', id);
    } else if(giveawayId && userPhone) {
      query = query.eq('giveaway_id', giveawayId).eq('user_phone', userPhone);
    } else {
      await refreshAdminData();
      showToast('Kemenangan peserta dihapus');
      return;
    }
    const { error } = await query;
    if(error) throw error;
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Kemenangan dihapus');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Delete winner error:', e);
    showToast(e?.message || 'Gagal hapus kemenangan', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminFillMaterialChapter(id) {
  const chapter = adminState.materialChapters.find(item => String(item.id) === String(id));
  if(!chapter) return;
  document.getElementById('adminChapterId').value = chapter.id || '';
  document.getElementById('adminChapterTitle').value = chapter.title || '';
  document.getElementById('adminChapterOrder').value = chapter.order_num ?? '';
  document.getElementById('adminChapterActive').value = String(chapter.is_active !== false);
  renderAdminChapterMaterialPicker();
  initAdminMissingMarkers();
}

function adminCancelMaterialChapterEdit(silent = false) {
  ['adminChapterId','adminChapterTitle','adminChapterOrder'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) {
      el.value = '';
      el.classList.remove('admin-missing');
    }
  });
  const active = document.getElementById('adminChapterActive');
  if(active) active.value = 'true';
  renderAdminChapterMaterialPicker();
  if(!silent) showToast('Edit bab dibatalkan');
}

function getAdminChapterPickedMaterialIds() {
  return [...document.querySelectorAll('#adminChapterMaterialPicker input[type="checkbox"]:checked')]
    .map(input => Number(input.value))
    .filter(id => Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0);
}

async function adminSaveMaterialChapter() {
  const id = Number(document.getElementById('adminChapterId')?.value || 0);
  const title = document.getElementById('adminChapterTitle')?.value.trim() || '';
  const orderNum = Number(document.getElementById('adminChapterOrder')?.value || id || 0);
  const isActive = document.getElementById('adminChapterActive')?.value !== 'false';
  const selectedIds = getAdminChapterPickedMaterialIds();
  if(!title) {
    showToast('Nama bab wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan bab...');
  try {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    let chapterId = id;
    const payload = { title, order_num: orderNum || 0, is_active: isActive, updated_at: now };
    if(chapterId) {
      const { error } = await sbClient.from('material_chapters').update(payload).eq('id', chapterId);
      if(error) throw error;
    } else {
      const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('material_chapters')
        .insert({ ...payload, created_at: now })
        .select('id')
        .single();
      if(error) throw error;
      chapterId = Number(data?.id || 0);
    }
    if(!chapterId) throw new Error('ID bab tidak terbaca');

    const currentIds = adminState.materials
      .filter(material => Number(material.chapter_id) === chapterId)
      .map(material => Number(material.id));
    const toClear = currentIds.filter(materialId => !selectedIds.includes(materialId));
    if(selectedIds.length) {
      const assignResults = await Promise.all(selectedIds.map((materialId, index) =>
        sbClient.from('materials')
          .update({ chapter_id: chapterId, chapter_order: index + 1, updated_at: now })
          .eq('id', materialId)
      ));
      const assignError = assignResults.find(result => result.error)?.error;
      if(assignError) throw assignError;
    }
    if(toClear.length) {
      const clearResults = await Promise.all(toClear.map(materialId =>
        sbClient.from('materials')
          .update({ chapter_id: null, chapter_order: 0, updated_at: now })
          .eq('id', materialId)
      ));
      const clearError = clearResults.find(result => result.error)?.error;
      if(clearError) throw clearError;
    }
    adminCancelMaterialChapterEdit(true);
    await refreshAdminData();
    if(state.currentPage === 'materi') renderMaterialsPath();
    showToast('Bab materi tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Material chapter save error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan bab. Jalankan SQL terbaru dulu kalau tabel belum ada.', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminDeleteMaterialChapter(id) {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Hapus bab ini? Materinya tidak dihapus, hanya dilepas dari bab.'))) return;
  showLoading('Menghapus bab...');
  try {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const clearResult = await sbClient.from('materials')
      .update({ chapter_id: null, chapter_order: 0, updated_at: now })
      .eq('chapter_id', id);
    if(clearResult.error) throw clearResult.error;
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('material_chapters').delete().eq('id', id);
    if(error) throw error;
    adminCancelMaterialChapterEdit(true);
    await refreshAdminData();
    if(state.currentPage === 'materi') renderMaterialsPath();
    showToast('Bab berhasil dihapus');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Delete material chapter error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal hapus bab', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminFillMaterial(id) {
  const item = adminState.materials.find(material => String(material.id) === String(id))
    || MATERI_DATA.find(material => String(material.id) === String(id));
  if(!item) return;
  document.getElementById('adminMaterialId').value = item.id || '';
  document.getElementById('adminMaterialTitle').value = item.title || '';
  document.getElementById('adminMaterialDesc').value = item.desc || '';
  document.getElementById('adminMaterialContent').value = item.content || '';
  document.getElementById('adminMaterialOrder').value = item.order_num || item.id || '';
  document.getElementById('adminMaterialActive').value = String(item.is_active !== false);
  initAdminMissingMarkers();
}

function renderAdminMaterialChapters(chapters = []) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('adminMaterialChapters');
  if(!wrap) return;
  const rows = normalizeMaterialChapters(chapters);
  if(!rows.length) rows.push({ title: 'Bab 1', content: '' });
  wrap.innerHTML = rows.map((chapter, index) => `
    <div class="admin-chapter-item" data-admin-chapter="${index}">
      <div class="admin-chapter-fields">
        <input class="admin-input admin-material-chapter-title" placeholder="Judul bab ${index + 1}" value="${escapeHtml(chapter.title || `Bab ${index + 1}`)}">
        <textarea class="admin-textarea admin-material-chapter-content" placeholder="Isi bab ${index + 1}">${escapeHtml(chapter.content || '')}</textarea>
      </div>
      <button class="admin-chapter-remove" type="button" onclick="removeAdminMaterialChapter(${index})" title="Hapus Bab"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function getAdminMaterialChapters() {
  const items = [...document.querySelectorAll('#adminMaterialChapters .admin-chapter-item')];
  return items.map((item, index) => {
    const title = item.querySelector('.admin-material-chapter-title')?.value.trim() || `Bab ${index + 1}`;
    const content = item.querySelector('.admin-material-chapter-content')?.value.trim() || '';
    return { title, content };
  }).filter(chapter => chapter.title || chapter.content);
}

function addAdminMaterialChapter() {
  const chapters = getAdminMaterialChapters();
  chapters.push({ title: `Bab ${chapters.length + 1}`, content: '' });
  renderAdminMaterialChapters(chapters);
}

function removeAdminMaterialChapter(index) {
  const chapters = getAdminMaterialChapters();
  chapters.splice(index, 1);
  renderAdminMaterialChapters(chapters.length ? chapters : [{ title: 'Bab 1', content: '' }]);
}

async function adminSaveMaterial() {
  const id = Number(document.getElementById('adminMaterialId').value || 0);
  const title = document.getElementById('adminMaterialTitle').value.trim();
  const desc = document.getElementById('adminMaterialDesc').value.trim();
  const content = document.getElementById('adminMaterialContent').value.trim();
  const orderNum = Number(document.getElementById('adminMaterialOrder').value || id || 0);
  const isActive = document.getElementById('adminMaterialActive').value === 'true';
  if(!Number.isInteger(id) || id < 1) {
    showToast('ID materi wajib angka minimal 1', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!title || !content) {
    showToast('Judul dan isi materi wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan materi...');
  try {
    const payload = {
      id,
      title,
      desc,
      content,
      order_num: orderNum || id,
      is_active: isActive,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    let { error } = await sbClient.from('materials').upsert(payload, { onConflict: 'id' });
    if(error && isMissingColumnError(error)) {
      const fallbackPayload = { ...payload };
      delete fallbackPayload.chapter_id;
      delete fallbackPayload.chapter_order;
      const retry = await sbClient.from('materials').upsert(fallbackPayload, { onConflict: 'id' });
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(error) throw error;
    adminCancelMaterialEdit(true);
    await refreshAdminData();
    if(state.currentPage === 'materi') renderMaterialsPath();
    showToast('Materi tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Material save error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal simpan materi. Pastikan tabel materials sudah dibuat.', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminCancelMaterialEdit(silent = false) {
  ['adminMaterialId','adminMaterialTitle','adminMaterialDesc','adminMaterialContent','adminMaterialOrder'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) {
      el.value = '';
      el.classList.remove('admin-missing');
    }
  });
  const active = document.getElementById('adminMaterialActive');
  if(active) active.value = 'true';
  if(!silent) showToast('Edit materi dibatalkan');
}

async function adminDeleteMaterial(id) {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Hapus materi ini? Progress member untuk ID ini tidak ikut dihapus.'))) return;

    await refreshAdminData();
    if(state.currentPage === 'materi') renderMaterialsPath();
    showToast('Materi berhasil dihapus');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Delete material error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal hapus materi', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminFillCarousel(id) {
  const item = adminState.carousel.find(carousel => String(carousel.id) === String(id));
  if(!item) return;
  document.getElementById('adminCarouselId').value = item.id || '';
  document.getElementById('adminCarouselTitle').value = item.title || '';
  document.getElementById('adminCarouselUrl').value = item.url || '';
  document.getElementById('adminCarouselOrder').value = item.order_num ?? 0;
  const modeEl = document.getElementById('adminCarouselImageMode');
  if(modeEl) modeEl.value = 'url';
  const fileEl = document.getElementById('adminCarouselFile');
  if(fileEl) fileEl.value = '';
  updateAdminCarouselFileName();
  updateAdminCarouselImageMode();
}

async function adminSaveCarousel() {
  const id = document.getElementById('adminCarouselId').value.trim();
  const title = document.getElementById('adminCarouselTitle').value.trim();
  const imageMode = document.getElementById('adminCarouselImageMode')?.value || 'url';
  let url = document.getElementById('adminCarouselUrl').value.trim();
  const imageFile = document.getElementById('adminCarouselFile')?.files?.[0];
  const orderNum = Number(document.getElementById('adminCarouselOrder').value || 0);
  if(!title) {
    showToast('Judul carousel wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(imageMode === 'url' && !url) {
    showToast('URL gambar wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(imageMode === 'file' && !imageFile && !id) {
    showToast('Pilih file gambar carousel dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Simpan carousel...');
  try {
    if(imageMode === 'file') {
      if(imageFile) {
        validateCarouselImageFile(imageFile);
        url = await fileToDataUrl(imageFile);
      } else if(id) {
        const current = adminState.carousel.find(carousel => String(carousel.id) === String(id));
        url = current?.url || url;
      }
    }
    if(!url) {
      throw new Error('Gambar carousel wajib diisi');
    }
    const payload = {
      title,
      url,
      order_num: orderNum,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    const result = id
      ? await sbClient.from('carousel_images').update(payload).eq('id', id)
      : await sbClient.from('carousel_images').insert(payload);
    const { error } = result;
    if(error) throw error;
    adminCancelCarouselEdit(true);
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Carousel tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Carousel save error:', e);
    showToast(e?.message || 'Gagal simpan carousel', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function adminCancelCarouselEdit(silent = false) {
  ['adminCarouselId','adminCarouselTitle','adminCarouselUrl','adminCarouselOrder'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) {
      el.value = '';
      el.classList.remove('admin-missing');
    }
  });
  const modeEl = document.getElementById('adminCarouselImageMode');
  if(modeEl) {
    modeEl.value = 'url';
    modeEl.classList.remove('admin-missing');
  }
  const fileEl = document.getElementById('adminCarouselFile');
  if(fileEl) fileEl.value = '';
  updateAdminCarouselFileName();
  updateAdminCarouselImageMode();
  if(!silent) showToast('Edit carousel dibatalkan');
}

    await loadPvpMatchMessages();
