}

async function adminDeletePvpMatchMessages(matchId) {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Hapus semua chat di match PvP ini?'))) return;
  showLoading('Menghapus chat match...');
  try {
    const { error, count } = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_messages')
      .delete({ count: 'exact' })
      .eq('match_id', matchId);
    if(error) throw error;
    await loadAdminPvpData({ cleanup: false, render: true });
    if(String(state.pvpActiveMatch?.id || '') === String(matchId)) await loadPvpMatchMessages();
    showToast(`${count || 0} chat match PvP dihapus`);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Delete PvP match messages error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal hapus chat match PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminCompletePvpMatch(matchId) {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Set match PvP ini selesai dan hapus dari database?'))) return;
  showLoading('Menyelesaikan match PvP...');
  try {
    await deletePvpMatchCompletely(matchId);
    if(String(state.pvpActiveMatch?.id || '') === String(matchId)) {
      state.pvpActiveMatch = null;
      state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
      state.pvpMatchReports = [];
      await loadPvpState();
    }
    await refreshAdminData();
    showToast('Match PvP diselesaikan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Complete PvP match error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal set match PvP selesai', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminDeletePvpMatch(matchId) {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Hapus match PvP ini beserta chat dan reportnya?'))) return;
  showLoading('Menghapus match PvP...');
  try {
    await deletePvpMatchCompletely(matchId);
    if(String(state.pvpActiveMatch?.id || '') === String(matchId)) {
      state.pvpActiveMatch = null;
      state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
      state.pvpMatchReports = [];
    }
    await refreshAdminData();
    await loadPvpState();
    showToast('Match PvP dihapus');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Delete PvP match error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal hapus match PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function adminBanPvpUser(phone) {
  const normalizedPhone = parsePhoneRaw(phone);
  if(!normalizedPhone) return;
  const user = adminState.users.find(item => String(item.phone || '') === String(normalizedPhone));
  if(!user?.ip_address) {
    showToast('Web ID user kosong, tidak bisa ban otomatis', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!(await appConfirm('Ban user PvP ini dan bersihkan Need & Help/PvP dia?'))) return;
  showLoading('Ban user PvP...');
  try {
    await banAndDeleteUserRecords(normalizedPhone, user.ip_address, `Ban PvP user ${normalizedPhone}`);
    await refreshAdminData();
    await loadPvpState();
    showToast('User PvP diban dan data terkait dibersihkan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Ban PvP user error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal ban user PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function normalizeTheme(theme) {
  return theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';
}

function normalizeUiMode(mode) {
  return mode === 'new' || mode === 'glass' ? 'new' : 'old';
}

function normalizeInterests(value) {
  if(Array.isArray(value)) {
    return [...new Set(value.map(item => String(item || '').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  }
  if(typeof value === 'string') {
    const raw = value.trim();
    if(!raw) return [];
    try {
      return normalizeInterests(JSON.parse(raw));
    } catch(e) {
      return [raw];
    }
  }
  if(value && typeof value === 'object') {
    return normalizeInterests(value.interests || value.selected || value.items || []);
  }
  return [];
}

function normalizeAccentColor(color) {
  const value = String(color || '').trim();
  return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(value) ? value.toLowerCase() : '#27a44f';
}

function hexToRgb(hex) {
  const value = normalizeAccentColor(hex).slice(1);
  return {
    r: parseInt(value.slice(0, 2), 16),
    g: parseInt(value.slice(2, 4), 16),
    b: parseInt(value.slice(4, 6), 16)
  };
}

function mixRgb(a, b, weight = 0.5) {
  const w = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, weight));
  return {
    r: Math.round(a.r * (1 - w) + b.r * w),
    g: Math.round(a.g * (1 - w) + b.g * w),
    b: Math.round(a.b * (1 - w) + b.b * w)
  };
}

function rgbToHex({ r, g, b }) {
  return `#${[r, g, b].map(v => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, v)).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')}`;
}

function applyTheme(theme) {
  const t = normalizeTheme(theme);
  state.theme = t;
  document.body.setAttribute('data-theme', t);
}

function applyUiMode(mode) {
  const nextMode = normalizeUiMode(mode);
  state.uiMode = nextMode;
  document.body.setAttribute('data-ui-mode', nextMode);
  if(nextMode === 'new') startRatingFocusEffect();
}

function applyAccentColor(color) {
  const accent = normalizeAccentColor(color);
  const base = hexToRgb(accent);
  const dark = rgbToHex(mixRgb(base, { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 }, 0.56));
  const mid = rgbToHex(mixRgb(base, { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 }, 0.22));
  const light = rgbToHex(mixRgb(base, { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 }, 0.24));
  const pale = rgbToHex(mixRgb(base, { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 }, 0.68));
  const wash = rgbToHex(mixRgb(base, { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 }, 0.9));
  state.accentColor = accent;
  document.body.style.setProperty('--green-1', dark);
  document.body.style.setProperty('--green-2', mid);
  document.body.style.setProperty('--green-3', accent);
  document.body.style.setProperty('--green-4', light);
  document.body.style.setProperty('--green-5', pale);
  document.body.style.setProperty('--green-6', wash);
  document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.setAttribute('content', accent);
}

function applyAppearancePreferences() {
  applyTheme(state.theme || 'light');
  applyUiMode(state.uiMode || 'old');
  applyAccentColor(state.accentColor || '#27a44f');
}

// ========== PHONE FORMAT ==========
function formatPhoneDisplay(raw) {
  let s = raw.replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(s.startsWith('62')) {
    let num = s.slice(2);
    let parts = [];
    if(num.length > 0) parts.push(num.slice(0,3));
    if(num.length > 3) parts.push(num.slice(3,7));
    if(num.length > 7) parts.push(num.slice(7));
    return '+62 ' + parts.join('-');
  } else if(s.startsWith('60')) {
    let num = s.slice(2);
    let parts = [];
    if(num.length > 0) parts.push(num.slice(0,2));
    if(num.length > 2) parts.push(num.slice(2,6));
    if(num.length > 6) parts.push(num.slice(6));
    return '+60 ' + parts.join('-');
  }
  return '+' + s;
}

function parsePhoneRaw(input) {
  let s = input.replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(s.startsWith('0')) s = '62' + s.slice(1);
  return s;
}

function isValidPhone(raw) {
  return /^(62|60)\d{8,12}$/.test(raw);
}

const SIGNUP_CAPTCHA_API = 'https://api.siputzx.my.id/api/canvas/captcha';

function normalizeCaptchaText(value = '') {
  return String(value || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '').toUpperCase();
}

function getNestedValue(source, paths = []) {
  for(const path of paths) {
    const value = path.split('.').reduce((obj, key) => obj?.[key], source);
    if(value !== undefined && value !== null && String(value).trim() !== '') return value;
  }
  return '';
}

function createLocalCaptcha() {
  const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
  let answer = '';
  for(let i = 0; i < 5; i++) answer += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
  const noise = Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, i) => {
    const x = Math.floor(Math.random() * 220);
    const y = Math.floor(Math.random() * 70);
    const opacity = (0.16 + Math.random() * 0.22).toFixed(2);
    return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="${1 + Math.random() * 2}" fill="#ffffff" opacity="${opacity}"/>`;
  }).join('');
  const lines = Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => {
    const x1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 220);
    const x2 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 220);
    const y1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 70);
    const y2 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 70);
    return `<line x1="${x1}" y1="${y1}" x2="${x2}" y2="${y2}" stroke="#a8f5c2" stroke-width="1.4" opacity="0.28"/>`;
  }).join('');
  const letters = answer.split('').map((char, i) => {
    const x = 35 + i * 34;
    const y = 43 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7);
    const rotate = -13 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 27);
    return `<text x="${x}" y="${y}" fill="#ffffff" font-family="Plus Jakarta Sans, Arial" font-size="27" font-weight="900" transform="rotate(${rotate} ${x} ${y})">${char}</text>`;
  }).join('');
  const svg = `
    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="220" height="70" viewBox="0 0 220 70">
      <defs>
        <linearGradient id="g" x1="0" x2="1" y1="0" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0" stop-color="#0a3d1f"/>
          <stop offset="1" stop-color="#27a44f"/>
        </linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <rect width="220" height="70" rx="14" fill="url(#g)"/>
      ${lines}
      ${noise}
      ${letters}
    </svg>
  `;
  return {
    answer,
    image: `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encodeURIComponent(svg)}`
  };
}

function renderCaptchaImage(src, label = '') {
  const preview = document.getElementById('captchaPreview');
  if(!preview) return;
  preview.innerHTML = `<img src="${escapeHtml(src)}" alt="${escapeHtml(label || 'Captcha')}">`;
}

function setLocalCaptcha() {
  const captcha = createLocalCaptcha();
  state.signupCaptchaAnswer = normalizeCaptchaText(captcha.answer);
  state.signupCaptchaSource = 'local';
  renderCaptchaImage(captcha.image, 'Captcha lokal');
}

async function loadSignupCaptcha(force = false) {
  const refreshBtn = document.getElementById('captchaRefreshBtn');
  const input = document.getElementById('captchaInput');
  const error = document.getElementById('captchaError');
  if(!force && state.signupCaptchaAnswer) return;
  refreshBtn?.classList.add('refreshing');
  if(error) error.style.display = 'none';
  if(input) input.value = '';
  try {
    const response = await fetch(SIGNUP_CAPTCHA_API, { method: 'GET', cache: 'no-store' });
    if(!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
    const type = response.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if(type.includes('application/json')) {
      const json = await response.json();
      const image = getNestedValue(json, [
        'data.image', 'data.img', 'data.url', 'data.base64', 'image', 'img', 'url', 'base64', 'result.image', 'result.url'
      ]);
      const answer = getNestedValue(json, [
        'data.text', 'data.answer', 'data.code', 'data.captcha', 'text', 'answer', 'code', 'captcha', 'result.text', 'result.answer'
      ]);
      if(!image || !answer) throw new Error('Captcha API tidak mengirim kode verifikasi');
      state.signupCaptchaAnswer = normalizeCaptchaText(answer);
      state.signupCaptchaSource = 'api';
      const imageSrc = String(image).startsWith('data:') || /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(image))
        ? String(image)
        : `data:image/png;base64,${String(image)}`;
      renderCaptchaImage(imageSrc, 'Captcha API');
    } else if(type.startsWith('image/')) {
      throw new Error('Captcha API hanya mengirim gambar tanpa kode verifikasi');
    } else {
      throw new Error('Format captcha API tidak dikenal');
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Captcha API fallback:', e);
    setLocalCaptcha();
  } finally {
    refreshBtn?.classList.remove('refreshing');
  }
}

function validateSignupCaptcha() {
  const input = document.getElementById('captchaInput');
  const error = document.getElementById('captchaError');
  const value = normalizeCaptchaText(input?.value || '');
  const answer = normalizeCaptchaText(state.signupCaptchaAnswer || '');
  const valid = !!value && !!answer && value === answer;
  if(error) error.style.display = valid ? 'none' : 'block';
  if(!valid) input?.focus();
  return valid;
}

function showSignupStep(step = 'identity') {
  const identity = document.getElementById('signupStepIdentity');
  const verify = document.getElementById('signupStepVerify');
  if(identity) identity.hidden = step !== 'identity';
  if(verify) verify.hidden = step !== 'verify';
  if(step === 'verify') {
    loadSignupCaptcha();
    document.getElementById('captchaInput')?.focus();
  } else {
    document.getElementById('profileNameInput')?.focus();
  }
}

function validateSignupIdentity() {
  const displayNameInput = document.getElementById('profileNameInput');
  const displayName = displayNameInput?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!displayName) {
    document.getElementById('profileNameError').style.display = 'block';
    displayNameInput?.closest('.phone-input-wrap')?.classList.add('invalid');
    displayNameInput?.focus();
    showToast('Nama profil wajib diisi dulu', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  const phoneInput = document.getElementById('phoneInput');
  const raw = parsePhoneRaw(phoneInput?.value || '');
  if(!isValidPhone(raw)) {
    document.getElementById('phoneError').style.display = 'block';
    phoneInput?.focus();
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function goSignupVerifyStep() {
  if(!validateSignupIdentity()) return;
  showSignupStep('verify');
}

document.getElementById('phoneInput')?.addEventListener('input', function() {
  let raw = parsePhoneRaw(this.value);
  if(raw.length > 2) this.value = formatPhoneDisplay(raw);
  else this.value = this.value;
  document.getElementById('phoneError').style.display = 'none';
});

document.getElementById('profileNameInput')?.addEventListener('input', function() {
  document.getElementById('profileNameError').style.display = 'none';
  this.closest('.phone-input-wrap')?.classList.remove('invalid');
});

document.getElementById('captchaInput')?.addEventListener('input', function() {
  document.getElementById('captchaError').style.display = 'none';
});

// ========== GENERATE UNIQUE CODE ==========
function generateUniqueCode() {
  const chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789';
  let rnd = '';
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++) rnd += chars[Math.floor(Math.random()*chars.length)];
  return 'NE_' + rnd;
}

// ========== SAVE TO SUPABASE ==========
function isMissingColumnError(error) {
  const text = `${error?.message || ''} ${error?.details || ''} ${error?.hint || ''}`;
  return error?.code === '42703' || /column .* does not exist|schema cache|display_name|avatar_border|profile_picture_url/i.test(text);
}

function isMissingSpecificColumnError(error, column) {
  const text = `${error?.message || ''} ${error?.details || ''} ${error?.hint || ''}`.toLowerCase();
  return error?.code === '42703' && text.includes(column.toLowerCase())
    || new RegExp(`\\b${column}\\b`, 'i').test(text);
}

function getSupabaseErrorText(error) {
  return String(error?.message || error?.details || error?.hint || error || '').trim();
}

async function upsertUserWithSchemaFallback(payload, options = {}) {
  const optionalColumns = options.optionalColumns || [
    'display_name',
    'ip_address',
    'ui_mode',
    'accent_color',
    'material_completed',
    'material_progress_percent',
    'last_material_id',
    'updated_at'
  ];
  const nextPayload = { ...payload };
  let result = await sbClient.from('users').upsert(nextPayload, { onConflict: 'phone' });
  while(result.error && isMissingColumnError(result.error)) {
    const before = Object.keys(nextPayload).length;
    optionalColumns.forEach(column => {
      if(column in nextPayload && isMissingSpecificColumnError(result.error, column)) {
        delete nextPayload[column];
      }
    });
    if(Object.keys(nextPayload).length === before) {
      const column = optionalColumns.find(name => name in nextPayload);
      if(!column) break;
      delete nextPayload[column];
    }
    result = await sbClient.from('users').upsert(nextPayload, { onConflict: 'phone' });
  }
  return result;
}

function normalizeDisplayNameKey(name = '') {
  return String(name || '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
}

function escapeIlikePattern(value = '') {
  return String(value || '').replace(/([\\%_])/g, '\\$1');
}

async function savePendingUser(phone, code, displayName = '') {
  if(!sbClient) return false;
  try {
    const ipAddress = await ensureClientIp();
    if(!ipAddress) {
      showToast('Gagal membaca Web ID. Coba refresh halaman.', 'error');
      return false;
    }

    const { data: existingUsers } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .select('phone')
      .eq('phone', phone)
      .limit(1);

    if(existingUsers && existingUsers.length > 0) {
      showToast('Nomor ini sudah terdaftar.', 'error');
      return false;
    }

    const { data: existingPending } = await sbClient.from('pending_users')
      .select('phone, verified')
      .eq('phone', phone)
      .limit(1);

    if(existingPending && existingPending.length > 0) {
      showToast('Nomor ini sudah punya proses verifikasi pending.', 'error');
      return false;
    }

    const displayNameKey = normalizeDisplayNameKey(displayName);
    if(displayNameKey) {
      const escapedDisplayName = escapeIlikePattern(displayName.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' '));
      const [{ data: nameUsers }, { data: namePending }] = await Promise.all([
        sbClient.from('users')
          .select('phone, display_name')
          .ilike('display_name', escapedDisplayName)
          .limit(5),
        sbClient.from('pending_users')
          .select('phone, display_name')
          .ilike('display_name', escapedDisplayName)
          .limit(5)
      ]);
      const nameTaken = [...(nameUsers || []), ...(namePending || [])]
        .some(item => normalizeDisplayNameKey(item.display_name) === displayNameKey);
      if(nameTaken) {
        showToast('Nama profil ini sudah dipakai. Pilih nama lain.', 'error');
        return false;
      }
    }

    const uniqueCode = generateUniqueCode();
    const payload = {
      phone, verify_code: code, verified: false, unique_code: uniqueCode, ip_address: ipAddress,
      display_name: displayName || null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    let { error } = await sbClient.from('pending_users').upsert(payload, { onConflict: 'phone' });
    if(error && isMissingColumnError(error)) {
      if(isMissingSpecificColumnError(error, 'display_name')) delete payload.display_name;
      const retry = await sbClient.from('pending_users').upsert(payload, { onConflict: 'phone' });
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(error) {
      console.warn('Pending save error:', error);
      if(error.code === '23505') {
        showToast('Nomor atau nama profil sudah dipakai.', 'error');
        return false;
      }
      showToast('Gagal menyimpan verifikasi ke database', 'error');
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Pending save exception:', e);
    showToast('Gagal menyimpan verifikasi ke database', 'error');
    return false;
  }
}

async function checkVerified(phone, code) {
  if(!sbClient) return false;
  try {
    let {data, error} = await sbClient.from('pending_users')
      .select('verified, unique_code, ip_address, display_name').eq('phone', phone).eq('verify_code', code).single();
    if(error && isMissingColumnError(error)) {
      const retry = await sbClient.from('pending_users')
        .select('verified, unique_code, ip_address').eq('phone', phone).eq('verify_code', code).single();
      data = retry.data;
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(data?.verified === true) {
      state.uniqueCode = data.unique_code || generateUniqueCode();
      state.displayName = data.display_name || state.displayName || '';
      await saveUserIpBinding(phone, state.uniqueCode, data.ip_address || state.clientIp, state.displayName);
      return true;
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  try {
    const {data, error} = await sbClient.from('users')
      .select('*')
      .eq('phone', phone)
      .single();
    if(error || !data) return false;
    const ipOk = await validateOrBindUserIp(data);
    if(!ipOk) return false;
    state.uniqueCode = data.unique_code || generateUniqueCode();
    state.theme = data.theme || state.theme;
    state.displayName = data.display_name || state.displayName || '';
    state.profilePictureUrl = normalizeProfilePictureUrl(data.profile_picture_url || '');
    state.avatarBorder = data.avatar_border || state.avatarBorder || 'white';
    state.winnerDisplayMode = data.winner_display_mode || state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
    state.gender = data.gender || state.gender;
    state.interests = normalizeInterests(data.interests).length ? normalizeInterests(data.interests) : normalizeInterests(state.interests);
    state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(data.timezone || state.timezone);
    if(!data.unique_code) await updateUniqueCode(phone, state.uniqueCode);
    return true;
  } catch(e) { return false; }
}

async function checkUserHasRated(phone) {
  if(!sbClient) return false;
  try {
    const {data} = await sbClient.from('ratings')
      .select('id').eq('phone', phone).limit(1);
    return data && data.length > 0;
  } catch(e) { return false; }
}

async function getUserByUniqueCode(code) {
  if(!sbClient) return null;
  try {
    const {data} = await sbClient.from('users')
      .select('*')
      .eq('unique_code', code)
      .maybeSingle();
    if(data) return data;

    const lowerCode = code.toLowerCase();
    if(lowerCode !== code) {
      const { data: lowerData } = await sbClient.from('users')
        .select('*')
        .eq('unique_code', lowerCode)
        .maybeSingle();
      if(lowerData) return lowerData;
    }

    const rawCode = document.getElementById('loginCodeInput')?.value.trim() || '';
    if(rawCode && rawCode !== code && rawCode !== lowerCode) {
      const { data: rawData } = await sbClient.from('users')
        .select('*')
        .eq('unique_code', rawCode)
        .maybeSingle();
      if(rawData) return rawData;
    }
    return null;
  } catch(e) { return null; }
}

async function validateOrBindUserIp(user) {
  const currentIp = await ensureClientIp();
  if(!currentIp) return true;
  const oldBinding = String(user.ip_address || '');
  const isLegacyInternetIp = /^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$/.test(oldBinding);
  const isPreviousLocalWebId = previousWebDeviceId && oldBinding === previousWebDeviceId;
  if(!oldBinding || oldBinding !== currentIp || isLegacyInternetIp || isPreviousLocalWebId) {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .update({ ip_address: currentIp, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('phone', user.phone);
    if(error) {
      console.warn('Gagal menyimpan Web ID akun:', error);
      return true;
    }
    user.ip_address = currentIp;
  }
  return true;
}

async function saveUserIpBinding(phone, uniqueCode, ipAddress, displayName = '') {
  const finalIp = ipAddress || await ensureClientIp();
  if(!finalIp) return false;
  const payload = {
    phone,
    unique_code: uniqueCode,
    ip_address: finalIp,
    display_name: displayName || null,
    theme: state.theme || 'light',
    ui_mode: state.uiMode || 'old',
    accent_color: state.accentColor || '#27a44f',
    material_completed: [],
    material_progress_percent: 0,
    last_material_id: null,
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  let { error } = await upsertUserWithSchemaFallback(payload);
  if(!error) return true;
  console.warn('User save with Web ID failed, retry without Web ID:', error);
  if(error.code === '23505' && /ip_address|users_ip_address_idx/i.test(getSupabaseErrorText(error))) {
    delete payload.ip_address;
    const retry = await upsertUserWithSchemaFallback(payload);
    return !retry.error;
  }
  return false;
}

async function updateUniqueCode(phone, newCode) {
  if(!sbClient) return false;
  try {
    const {data, error} = await sbClient.from('users')
      .update({
        unique_code: newCode,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('phone', phone)
      .select('phone')
      .maybeSingle();
    if(!error && !data && String(phone || '') === String(state.phone || '')) {
      returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
      return false;
    }
    return !error;
  } catch(e) { return false; }
}

async function loadUserProfile() {
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone) return null;
  state.profileLoadStatus = 'loading';
  try {
    const {data, error} = await sbClient.from('users')
      .select('*')
      .eq('phone', state.phone)
      .single();
    if(error || !data) {
      state.profileLoadStatus = 'missing';
      return null;
    }
    const ipOk = await validateOrBindUserIp(data);
    if(!ipOk) {
      state.profileLoadStatus = 'blocked';
      return null;
    }

    state.uniqueCode = data.unique_code || state.uniqueCode || generateUniqueCode();
    state.theme = data.theme || state.theme;
    state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(data.ui_mode || state.uiMode || 'old');
    state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(data.accent_color || state.accentColor || '#27a44f');
    state.displayName = data.display_name || state.displayName || '';
    state.profilePictureUrl = normalizeProfilePictureUrl(data.profile_picture_url || '');
    state.avatarBorder = data.avatar_border || state.avatarBorder || 'white';
    state.winnerDisplayMode = data.winner_display_mode || state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
    state.gender = data.gender || state.gender;
    state.interests = normalizeInterests(data.interests).length ? normalizeInterests(data.interests) : normalizeInterests(state.interests);
    applyMemberEffectFromProfile(data);
    subscribeMemberEffect();
    if(!data.unique_code) await updateUniqueCode(state.phone, state.uniqueCode);

    const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
    session.phone = state.phone;
    session.uniqueCode = state.uniqueCode;
    session.theme = state.theme;
    session.uiMode = state.uiMode;
    session.accentColor = state.accentColor;
    session.gender = state.gender;
    session.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
    session.displayName = state.displayName;
    delete session.profilePictureUrl;
    session.avatarBorder = state.avatarBorder;
    session.winnerDisplayMode = state.winnerDisplayMode;
    session.timezone = state.timezone;
    session.verified = true;
    session.timestamp = Date.now();
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
    applyAppearancePreferences();
    state.profileLoadStatus = 'ok';
    return data;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal memuat profil user:', e);
    state.profileLoadStatus = 'error';
    return null;
  }
}

function resetMemberStorageBecauseAccountMissing() {
  const keepKeys = new Set([
    'ne_web_device_id',
    'ne_admin_session',
    'ne_admin_theme',
    'ne_admin_appeal_seen_counts'
  ]);
  Object.keys(localStorage).forEach(key => {
    if(key.startsWith('ne_') && !keepKeys.has(key)) {
      localStorage.removeItem(key);
    }
  });
  materialState.completed = [];
  materialState.currentlyReading = null;
  materialState.activeChapterIndex = 0;
  materialState.currentChapters = [];
  materialState.chapterCompleted = [];
  materialState.synced = false;
  state.savedSettings = null;
  state.shopCart = [];
  state.pvpActiveEntry = null;
  state.pvpActiveMatch = null;
  state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
  state.pvpMatchReports = [];
  state.needHelpMessages = [];
  state.activeNeedHelpPostId = '';
}

async function returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing() {
  stopUserAutoRefresh();
  const missingPhone = state.phone;
  if(sbClient) {
    await ensureClientIp();
    if(await checkBannedIp()) return;
    await cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhone(missingPhone);
  }
  resetMemberStorageBecauseAccountMissing();
  state.phone = '';
  state.verifyCode = '';
  state.uniqueCode = '';
  state.displayName = '';
  state.winnerDisplayMode = 'display_name';
  state.gender = '';
  state.interests = [];
  const phoneForm = document.getElementById('phone-form-area');
  const waitingState = document.getElementById('waitingState');
  const phoneInput = document.getElementById('phoneInput');
  if(phoneForm) phoneForm.style.display = 'block';
  if(waitingState) waitingState.style.display = 'none';
  if(phoneInput) phoneInput.value = '';
  showPage('phone');
  showToast('Akun tidak ditemukan. Silakan daftar atau login ulang.', 'error');
}

async function returnToHomeBecauseProfileBlocked() {
  stopUserAutoRefresh();
  const blockedPhone = state.phone;
  if(sbClient) {
    await ensureClientIp();
    if(await checkBannedIp()) return;
    await cleanupNeedHelpActivityForPhone(blockedPhone);
  }
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_session');
  state.phone = '';
  state.verifyCode = '';
  state.uniqueCode = '';
  const phoneForm = document.getElementById('phone-form-area');
  const waitingState = document.getElementById('waitingState');
  if(phoneForm) phoneForm.style.display = 'block';
  if(waitingState) waitingState.style.display = 'none';
  showPage('phone');
}

async function ensureCurrentUserStillExists() {
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone) return true;
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('users')
      .select('phone')
      .eq('phone', state.phone)
      .maybeSingle();
    if(error) return true;
    if(!data) {
      state.profileLoadStatus = 'missing';
      returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal cek user masih ada:', e);
    return true;
  }
}

const DEFAULT_WA_GROUP_URL = 'https://chat.whatsapp.com/Hh9PJBefjnpC6ao7rt6bWI';
const SIGNUP_GROUP_JOIN_KEY = 'ne_signup_group_join_clicked';

function normalizeWhatsappGroupUrl(url) {
  const value = String(url || '').trim();
  if(!value) return '';
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(value);
    if(!['http:', 'https:'].includes(parsed.protocol)) return '';
    return parsed.href;
  } catch(e) {
    return '';
  }
}

function getWhatsappGroupUrl() {
  return normalizeWhatsappGroupUrl(state.whatsappGroupUrl) || DEFAULT_WA_GROUP_URL;
}

function hasClickedSignupGroupJoin() {
  return localStorage.getItem(SIGNUP_GROUP_JOIN_KEY) === 'true';
}

function updateSignupJoinGate() {
  const joined = hasClickedSignupGroupJoin();
  const joinButtons = [
    document.getElementById('btnJoinGroupFirst'),
    document.getElementById('btnJoinGroupOnboard')
  ].filter(Boolean);
  const statuses = [
    document.getElementById('joinGroupStatus'),
    document.getElementById('onboardJoinGroupStatus')
  ].filter(Boolean);
  const onboardNext = document.getElementById('onboardJoinNext');
  if(onboardNext) onboardNext.disabled = !joined;
  joinButtons.forEach(joinBtn => {
    joinBtn.classList.toggle('secondary', joined);
    joinBtn.innerHTML = joined
      ? '<i class="fab fa-whatsapp"></i> Buka Grup Lagi'
      : '<i class="fab fa-whatsapp"></i> Join Grup NeverEra Academy';
  });
  statuses.forEach(status => status.classList.toggle('active', joined));
}

function markSignupGroupJoined() {
  localStorage.setItem(SIGNUP_GROUP_JOIN_KEY, 'true');
  updateSignupJoinGate();
  window.open(getWhatsappGroupUrl(), '_blank');
  showToast('Grup info dan pembelajaran dibuka. Lanjut penyesuaian.');
}

// ========== HANDLE SEND ==========
async function handleSendPhone() {
  if(!validateSignupIdentity()) return;
  if(!validateSignupCaptcha()) {
    showToast('Captcha wajib benar sebelum daftar', 'error');
    await loadSignupCaptcha(true);
    return;
  }
  const displayName = document.getElementById('profileNameInput')?.value.trim() || '';
  const raw = parsePhoneRaw(document.getElementById('phoneInput')?.value || '');
  state.displayName = displayName;
  state.phone = raw;
  state.verifyCode = generateVerifyCode();

  const btn = document.getElementById('btnSend');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Mengirim...';
  showLoading('Mendaftarkan nomor...');

  try {
    const saved = await savePendingUser(raw, state.verifyCode, displayName);
    if(!saved) {
      state.phone = '';
      state.verifyCode = '';
      localStorage.removeItem('ne_pending');
      document.getElementById('phone-form-area').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('waitingState').style.display = 'none';
      return;
    }

    localStorage.setItem('ne_pending', JSON.stringify({
      phone: raw,
      code: state.verifyCode,
      displayName,
      timestamp: Date.now()
    }));

    document.getElementById('phone-form-area').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('waitingState').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('verifyCodeDisplay').textContent = state.verifyCode;

    showToast('Menunggu verifikasi. Klik Cek Status Verifikasi setelah admin acc.');
  } catch(e) {
    showToast('Gagal: ' + e.message, 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = 'Kirim Verifikasi';
  }
}

function generateVerifyCode() {
  const chars = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789';
  let rnd = '';
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++) rnd += chars[Math.floor(Math.random()*chars.length)];
  return 'NE_A' + rnd;
}

function startPolling() {
  if(state.pollInterval) clearInterval(state.pollInterval);
  state.pollInterval = null;
}

async function checkVerificationNow() {
  if(!canRunDataRequest('verification_check')) return;
  const pending = (() => {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_pending') || '{}');
    } catch(e) {
      return {};
    }
  })();
  const phone = state.phone || pending.phone || '';
  const code = state.verifyCode || pending.code || '';
  if(!phone || !code) {
    showToast('Data pending tidak ditemukan', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Cek status verifikasi...');
  try {
    state.phone = phone;
    state.verifyCode = code;
    const ok = await checkVerified(phone, code);
    if(ok) {
      triggerVerified();
      return;
    }
    showToast('Belum di-acc admin. Coba cek lagi nanti.', 'warn');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Cek verifikasi error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal cek status verifikasi', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function cancelVerification() {
  const pendingPhone = state.phone || (() => {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_pending') || '{}')?.phone || '';
    } catch(e) {
      return '';
    }
  })();
  if(state.pollInterval) {
    clearInterval(state.pollInterval);
    state.pollInterval = null;
  }
  if(sbClient && pendingPhone) {
    try {
      await sbClient.from('pending_users').delete().eq('phone', pendingPhone);
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('Gagal hapus pending saat batal verifikasi:', e);
      showToast('Pending lokal dibatalkan, tapi database gagal dibersihkan', 'error');
    }
  }
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_pending');
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_session');
  document.getElementById('phone-form-area').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('waitingState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('phoneInput').value = '';
  const profileInput = document.getElementById('profileNameInput');
  if(profileInput) profileInput.value = '';
  const captchaInput = document.getElementById('captchaInput');
  if(captchaInput) captchaInput.value = '';
  document.getElementById('btnSend').textContent = 'Kirim Verifikasi';
  updateSignupJoinGate();
  showSignupStep('identity');
  state.phone = '';
  state.verifyCode = '';
  state.displayName = '';
  state.signupCaptchaAnswer = '';
  loadSignupCaptcha(true);
  showToast('Verifikasi dibatalkan');
}

function triggerVerified() {
  clearInterval(state.pollInterval);
  const fmt = formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone);
  document.getElementById('verifiedPhoneDisplay').textContent = fmt;
  
  if(!state.uniqueCode) {
    state.uniqueCode = generateUniqueCode();
  }
  updateUniqueCode(state.phone, state.uniqueCode);
  
  localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify({
    phone: state.phone,
    verifyCode: state.verifyCode,
    uniqueCode: state.uniqueCode,
    displayName: state.displayName || '',
    theme: state.theme || 'light',
    uiMode: state.uiMode || 'old',
    accentColor: state.accentColor || '#27a44f',
    winnerDisplayMode: state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name',
    verified: true,
    timestamp: Date.now()
  }));
  
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_pending');
  spawnConfetti();
  showPage('verified');
}

function spawnConfetti() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('confettiWrap');
  wrap.innerHTML = '';
  const colors = ['#4fd97b','#27a44f','#a8f5c2','#fff','#f39c12'];
  for(let i=0;i<24;i++) {
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'confetti-dot';
    d.style.cssText = `
      left:${Math.random()*100}%;
      top:${-10-Math.random()*40}px;
      background:${colors[Math.floor(Math.random()*colors.length)]};
      animation-delay:${Math.random()*1.2}s;
      animation-duration:${1.2+Math.random()*0.8}s;
      width:${6+Math.random()*8}px;
      height:${6+Math.random()*8}px;
    `;
    wrap.appendChild(d);
  }
}

// ========== LOGIN WITH UNIQUE CODE ==========
function openLoginModal() {
  document.getElementById('loginModal').classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById('loginCodeInput').value = '';
}

function closeLoginModal() {
  document.getElementById('loginModal').classList.remove('open');
}

async function loginWithCode() {
  const code = document.getElementById('loginCodeInput').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!code) {
    showToast('Masukkan kode unik!', 'error');
    return;
  }
  
  showLoading('Memverifikasi kode...');
  const user = await getUserByUniqueCode(code);
  hideLoading();
  
  if(user) {
    const ipOk = await validateOrBindUserIp(user);
    if(!ipOk) return;

    state.phone = user.phone;
    state.uniqueCode = user.unique_code;
    state.verifyCode = '';
    state.theme = user.theme || state.theme || 'light';
    state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(user.ui_mode || state.uiMode || 'old');
    state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(user.accent_color || state.accentColor || '#27a44f');
    state.displayName = user.display_name || '';
    state.winnerDisplayMode = user.winner_display_mode || 'display_name';
    state.gender = user.gender || '';
    state.interests = normalizeInterests(user.interests);
    state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(user.timezone || state.timezone);
    
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify({
      phone: state.phone,
      uniqueCode: state.uniqueCode,
      theme: state.theme,
      uiMode: state.uiMode,
      accentColor: state.accentColor,
      gender: state.gender,
      interests: normalizeInterests(state.interests),
      displayName: state.displayName,
      winnerDisplayMode: state.winnerDisplayMode,
      timezone: state.timezone,
      verified: true,
      timestamp: Date.now()
    }));
    
    closeLoginModal();
    applyAppearancePreferences();
    showToast('Login berhasil!');
    if(!(await enforceUpdatingServerGate())) return;
    saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
    showPage('dashboard');
    await initDashboard();
    ensureAccountTuningComplete();
  } else {
    showToast('Kode unik tidak valid atau belum diverifikasi', 'error');
  }
}

// ========== UNIQUE CODE UI ==========
function toggleCodeVisibility() {
  state.codeVisible = !state.codeVisible;
  const codeSpan = document.getElementById('codeValue');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleCodeBtn');
  if(state.codeVisible) {
    codeSpan.textContent = state.uniqueCode || 'Belum ada';
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash"></i>';
  } else {
    codeSpan.textContent = '••••••';
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye"></i>';
  }
}

function toggleCodeVisibility() {
  state.codeVisible = !state.codeVisible;
  const codeSpan = document.getElementById('codeValue');
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggleCodeBtn');
  if(state.codeVisible) {
    codeSpan.textContent = state.uniqueCode || 'Belum ada';
  } else {
    codeSpan.textContent = '••••••';
  }
  if(btn) btn.innerHTML = state.codeVisible ? '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash"></i>' : '<i class="fas fa-eye"></i>';
}

function copyUniqueCode() {
  if(state.uniqueCode) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(state.uniqueCode);
    showToast('Kode disalin!');
  }
}

function maskIp(ip) {
  if(!ip) return 'Memuat...';
  const value = String(ip);
  if(value.length <= 10) return '••••••••';
  return `${value.slice(0, 6)}••••${value.slice(-4)}`;
}

function updateClientIpUI() {
  const ipEl = document.getElementById('dashIp');
  const iconEl = document.getElementById('toggleIpIcon');
  if(ipEl) ipEl.textContent = state.ipVisible ? (state.clientIp || 'Tidak tersedia') : maskIp(state.clientIp);
  if(iconEl) iconEl.className = state.ipVisible ? 'fas fa-eye-slash' : 'fas fa-eye';
}

function toggleIpVisibility() {
  state.ipVisible = !state.ipVisible;
  updateClientIpUI();
}

function showEditCodeBox() {
  document.getElementById('editCodeInput').value = '';
  document.getElementById('codeEditBox').classList.add('active');
}

function hideEditCodeBox() {
  document.getElementById('codeEditBox').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('editCodeInput').value = '';
}

async function saveUniqueCode() {
  const newCode = document.getElementById('editCodeInput').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  if(!newCode) {
    showToast('Kode tidak boleh kosong', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(newCode.length < 4) {
    showToast('Kode minimal 4 karakter', 'error');
    return;
  }
  
  showLoading('Menyimpan kode...');
  const success = await updateUniqueCode(state.phone, newCode);
  hideLoading();
  
  if(success) {
    state.uniqueCode = newCode;
    const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
    session.uniqueCode = newCode;
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
    
    const codeSpan = document.getElementById('codeValue');
    codeSpan.textContent = '••••••';
    state.codeVisible = false;
    document.getElementById('toggleCodeBtn').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye"></i>';
    document.getElementById('toggleCodeBtn').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye"></i>';
    showToast('Kode unik berhasil diubah!');
    document.getElementById('toggleCodeBtn').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye"></i>';
    hideEditCodeBox();
  } else {
    showToast('Gagal menyimpan kode', 'error');
  }
}

// ========== ONBOARDING ==========
let currentStep = 0;

function goToOnboarding() {
  state.theme = normalizeTheme(localStorage.getItem('ne_theme_draft') || state.theme || 'light');
  currentStep = 0;
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="step-"]').forEach((step, index) => {
    step.style.display = index === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.step-dot').forEach((dot, index) => {
    dot.classList.remove('active','done');
    if(index === 0) dot.classList.add('active');
  });
  const displayNameInput = document.getElementById('onboardDisplayName');
  if(displayNameInput) displayNameInput.value = state.displayName || '';
  document.querySelectorAll('.theme-opt').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('selected', el.dataset.theme === state.theme);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.gender-opt').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('selected', el.dataset.gender === state.gender);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.interest-opt').forEach(el => {
    const selected = state.interests.includes(el.dataset.interest);
    el.classList.toggle('selected', selected);
    const chk = el.querySelector('.check');
    if(chk) chk.style.display = selected ? 'inline' : 'none';
  });
  clearOnboardingInvalid();
  showPage('onboarding');
  updateSignupJoinGate();
  applyAppearancePreferences();
}

function nextStep(to) {
  if(!validateOnboardingStep(currentStep)) return;
  showOnboardingStep(to);
}

function showOnboardingStep(to) {
  document.getElementById('step-'+currentStep).style.display='none';
  currentStep = to;
  document.getElementById('step-'+currentStep).style.display='block';
  document.querySelectorAll('.step-dot').forEach((d,i)=>{
    d.classList.remove('active','done');
    if(i<to) d.classList.add('done');
    else if(i===to) d.classList.add('active');
  });
}

function clearOnboardingInvalid(scope = '') {
  if(!scope || scope === 'profile') {
    document.getElementById('onboardDisplayName')?.classList.remove('invalid');
  }
  if(!scope || scope === 'gender') {
    document.querySelector('.gender-options')?.classList.remove('invalid');
  }
  if(!scope || scope === 'interests') {
    document.querySelector('.interest-options')?.classList.remove('invalid');
  }
}

function validateOnboardingStep(step = currentStep) {
  clearOnboardingInvalid();
  if(step === 1) {
    const input = document.getElementById('onboardDisplayName');
    const name = input?.value.trim() || '';
    if(!name) {
      input?.classList.add('invalid');
      input?.focus();
      showToast('Nama display wajib diisi dulu', 'error');
      return false;
    }
    state.displayName = name;
  }
  if(step === 2 && !state.gender) {
    document.querySelector('.gender-options')?.classList.add('invalid');
    showToast('Pilih gender dulu', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  if(step === 3 && state.interests.length === 0) {
    document.querySelector('.interest-options')?.classList.add('invalid');
    showToast('Pilih minimal satu minat dulu', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function selectTheme(theme, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.theme-opt').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  state.theme = theme;
  localStorage.setItem('ne_theme_draft', theme);
  applyTheme(theme);
}

function selectGender(g, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.gender-opt').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  state.gender = g;
  clearOnboardingInvalid('gender');
}

function selectWinnerDisplayMode(mode, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.privacy-opt').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  state.winnerDisplayMode = mode;
}

function toggleInterest(key, el) {
  el.classList.toggle('selected');
  const chk = el.querySelector('.check');
  if(el.classList.contains('selected')) {
    if(!state.interests.includes(key)) state.interests.push(key);
    chk.style.display='inline';
  } else {
    state.interests = state.interests.filter(i=>i!==key);
    chk.style.display='none';
  }
  clearOnboardingInvalid('interests');
}

async function enterDashboardAfterVerified() {
  if(!(await enforceUpdatingServerGate())) return;
  saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
  showPage('dashboard');
  await initDashboard();
  ensureAccountTuningComplete();
}

function syncAccountTuneStateFromSession() {
  const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  state.displayName = String(state.displayName || session.displayName || '').trim();
  state.gender = String(state.gender || session.gender || '').trim();
  state.winnerDisplayMode = session.winnerDisplayMode || state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
  const interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests).length ? normalizeInterests(state.interests) : normalizeInterests(session.interests);
  state.interests = interests;
}

function getAccountTuneMissingStep() {
  syncAccountTuneStateFromSession();
  if(!state.displayName) return 1;
  if(!state.gender) return 2;
  if(!normalizeInterests(state.interests).length) return 3;
  return 0;
}

function isAccountTuningComplete() {
  return getAccountTuneMissingStep() === 0;
}

function renderDashboardAccountTuning() {
  renderAccountTuneFlow(getAccountTuneMissingStep() || 1);
}

function renderAccountTuneFlow(step = 1) {
  syncAccountTuneStateFromSession();
  const input = document.getElementById('accountTuneDisplayName');
  if(input && document.activeElement !== input) input.value = state.displayName || '';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tune-winner-mode]').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('selected', el.dataset.tuneWinnerMode === (state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name'));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tune-gender]').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('selected', el.dataset.tuneGender === state.gender);
  });
  const interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tune-interest]').forEach(el => {
    const selected = interests.includes(el.dataset.tuneInterest);
    el.classList.toggle('selected', selected);
    const chk = el.querySelector('.check');
    if(chk) chk.style.display = selected ? 'inline' : 'none';
  });
  showAccountTuneStep(step);
}

function showAccountTuneStep(step) {
  const target = Math.max(1, Math.min(3, Number(step) || 1));
  document.querySelectorAll('.account-tune-step').forEach(el => {
    el.style.display = el.id === `accountTuneStep-${target}` ? 'block' : 'none';
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tune-dot]').forEach(dot => {
    const index = Number(dot.dataset.tuneDot);
    dot.classList.remove('active','done');
    if(index < target) dot.classList.add('done');
    else if(index === target) dot.classList.add('active');
  });
  const focusMap = {
    1: 'accountTuneDisplayName'
  };
  if(focusMap[target]) setTimeout(() => document.getElementById(focusMap[target])?.focus(), 80);
}

function clearAccountTuneInvalid(scope = '') {
  if(!scope || scope === 'profile') document.getElementById('accountTuneDisplayName')?.classList.remove('invalid');
  if(!scope || scope === 'gender') document.getElementById('accountTuneGenderOptions')?.classList.remove('invalid');
  if(!scope || scope === 'interests') document.getElementById('accountTuneInterestOptions')?.classList.remove('invalid');
}

function validateAccountTuneStep(step) {
  clearAccountTuneInvalid();
  if(step === 1) {
    const input = document.getElementById('accountTuneDisplayName');
    const name = input?.value.trim() || '';
    if(!name) {
      input?.classList.add('invalid');
      input?.focus();
      showToast('Nama display wajib diisi dulu', 'error');
      return false;
    }
    state.displayName = name;
  }
  if(step === 2 && !state.gender) {
    document.getElementById('accountTuneGenderOptions')?.classList.add('invalid');
    showToast('Pilih gender dulu', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  state.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  if(step === 3 && !state.interests.length) {
    document.getElementById('accountTuneInterestOptions')?.classList.add('invalid');
    showToast('Pilih minimal satu minat dulu', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

async function nextAccountTuneStep(step) {
  const current = Math.max(1, Math.min(3, Number(step) - 1 || 1));
  if(!validateAccountTuneStep(current)) return;
  const missing = getAccountTuneMissingStep();
  if(!missing) {
    const saved = await saveAccountTuneData();
    if(!saved) return;
    showToast('Penyesuaian akun tersimpan');
    saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
    showPage('dashboard');
    state.currentPage = 'beranda';
    setActiveNavigation('beranda');
    await initDashboard();
    maybeStartMainTutorial();
    return;
  }
  renderAccountTuneFlow(Math.max(Number(step) || 1, missing));
}

function setAccountTuneWinnerDisplayMode(mode) {
  state.winnerDisplayMode = mode === 'phone' ? 'phone' : 'display_name';
  renderAccountTuneFlow(Number(document.querySelector('.account-tune-step[style*="block"]')?.id?.split('-').pop()) || getAccountTuneMissingStep() || 1);
}

function setAccountTuneGender(gender) {
  state.gender = gender;
  clearAccountTuneInvalid('gender');
  renderAccountTuneFlow(2);
}

function toggleAccountTuneInterest(key) {
  state.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  if(state.interests.includes(key)) state.interests = state.interests.filter(item => item !== key);
  else state.interests = [...state.interests, key];
  clearAccountTuneInvalid('interests');
  renderAccountTuneFlow(3);
}

function remindAccountTuning() {
  showPage('account-tune');
  state.currentPage = 'penyesuaian';
  saveMemberRoute('penyesuaian', true);
  setActiveNavigation('penyesuaian');
  renderAccountTuneFlow(getAccountTuneMissingStep() || 1);
  showToast('Anda lupa mengisi penyesuaian akun', 'error');
}

function ensureAccountTuningComplete({ notify = true } = {}) {
  if(isAccountTuningComplete()) return true;
  if(notify) remindAccountTuning();
  return false;
}

async function saveAccountTuneData() {
  state.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  showLoading('Menyimpan penyesuaian...');
  try {
    if(sbClient && state.phone) {
      const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users').update({
        display_name: state.displayName || null,
        winner_display_mode: state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name',
        gender: state.gender || null,
        interests: normalizeInterests(state.interests),
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }).eq('phone', state.phone).select('phone').maybeSingle();
      if(error) throw error;
      if(!data) {
        returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
        return false;
      }
    }
    const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
    session.displayName = state.displayName;
    session.winnerDisplayMode = state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
    session.gender = state.gender || '';
    session.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
    session.timestamp = Date.now();
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
    updateGreeting();
    const sbUserName = document.getElementById('sbUserName');
    if(sbUserName) sbUserName.textContent = state.displayName || 'Member';
    renderProfileAvatar();
    return true;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Simpan penyesuaian akun error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal menyimpan penyesuaian', 'error');
    return false;
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function finishAccountTuneFlow() {
  if(!validateAccountTuneStep(3)) return;
  if(!validateAccountTuneStep(1) || !validateAccountTuneStep(2)) {
    renderAccountTuneFlow(getAccountTuneMissingStep() || 1);
    return;
  }
  const saved = await saveAccountTuneData();
  if(!saved) return;
  showToast('Penyesuaian akun tersimpan');
  saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
  showPage('dashboard');
  state.currentPage = 'beranda';
  setActiveNavigation('beranda');
  await initDashboard();
  maybeStartMainTutorial();
}

async function finishOnboarding() {
  const displayNameInput = document.getElementById('onboardDisplayName');
  state.displayName = displayNameInput?.value.trim() || state.displayName || '';
  if(!state.displayName) {
    showOnboardingStep(1);
    validateOnboardingStep(1);
    return;
  }
  if(!state.gender) {
    showOnboardingStep(2);
    validateOnboardingStep(2);
    return;
  }
  if(!validateOnboardingStep(3)) return;
  
  showLoading('Menyimpan preferensi...');
  if(!state.uniqueCode) state.uniqueCode = generateUniqueCode();
  
  if(sbClient) {
    try {
      const payload = {
        unique_code: state.uniqueCode,
        ip_address: state.clientIp || await ensureClientIp(),
        display_name: state.displayName || null,
        winner_display_mode: state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name',
        theme: state.theme,
        ui_mode: state.uiMode,
        accent_color: state.accentColor,
        gender: state.gender,
        interests: normalizeInterests(state.interests),
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      let { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users').update(payload).eq('phone', state.phone).select('phone').maybeSingle();
      while(error && isMissingColumnError(error) && (payload.ui_mode || payload.accent_color)) {
        if(payload.ui_mode && isMissingSpecificColumnError(error, 'ui_mode')) delete payload.ui_mode;
        else if(payload.accent_color && isMissingSpecificColumnError(error, 'accent_color')) delete payload.accent_color;
        else if(payload.ui_mode) delete payload.ui_mode;
        else delete payload.accent_color;
        const retry = await sbClient.from('users').update(payload).eq('phone', state.phone).select('phone').maybeSingle();
        data = retry.data;
        error = retry.error;
      }
      if(error) throw error;
      if(!data) {
        returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
        return;
      }
    } catch(e) { console.warn('Save error:', e); }
  }
  
  const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  session.uniqueCode = state.uniqueCode;
  session.displayName = state.displayName;
  session.winnerDisplayMode = state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
  session.theme = state.theme;
  session.uiMode = state.uiMode;
  session.accentColor = state.accentColor;
  session.gender = state.gender;
  session.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_theme_draft');
  
  hideLoading();
  if(!(await enforceUpdatingServerGate())) return;
  saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
  showPage('dashboard');
  await initDashboard();
  maybeStartMainTutorial();
}

function logout() {
  if(isPvpMatchLocked()) {
    showToast('Selesaikan PvP dulu sebelum keluar.', 'error');
    navigateTo('findpvp', { replaceRoute: true });
    return;
  }
  stopUserAutoRefresh();
  clearMemberEffect();
  if(state.memberEffectChannel && sbClient) {
    sbClient.removeChannel(state.memberEffectChannel);
    state.memberEffectChannel = null;
  }
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_session');
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_pending');
  localStorage.removeItem('ne_last_member_page');
  history.replaceState(null, '', `${window.location.pathname}${window.location.search}`);
  if(state.pollInterval) clearInterval(state.pollInterval);
  state.phone = '';
  state.verifyCode = '';
  state.uniqueCode = '';
  state.displayName = '';
  state.interests = [];
  state.gender = '';
  state.hasRated = false;
  materialState.completed = [];
  state.currentPage = 'beranda';
  showPage('phone');
  document.getElementById('phone-form-area').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('waitingState').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('phoneInput').value = '';
  const profileInput = document.getElementById('profileNameInput');
  if(profileInput) profileInput.value = '';
  document.getElementById('btnSend').textContent = 'Kirim Verifikasi';
  updateSignupJoinGate();
  showSignupStep('identity');
  showToast('Anda telah keluar');
}

// ========== NAVIGATION ==========
function setActiveNavigation(page) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item, .hotbar-item, .new-menu-item').forEach(item => {
    item.classList.toggle('active', item.dataset.page === page);
  });
}

function renderNewMenuItems() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('newMenuGrid');
  if(!grid) return;
  const items = [...document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item[data-page]')];
  const menuHtml = items.map(item => {
    const page = item.dataset.page || '';
    const icon = item.querySelector('.icon')?.innerHTML || '<i class="fas fa-circle"></i>';
    const label = [...item.childNodes]
      .filter(node => node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE)
      .map(node => node.textContent.trim())
      .join(' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim() || item.textContent.trim();
    return `
      <button class="new-menu-item ${page === state.currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" data-page="${escapeHtml(page)}" onclick="selectNewMenuItem('${escapeHtml(page)}')">
        <span class="new-menu-icon">${icon}</span>
        <span class="new-menu-label">${escapeHtml(label)}</span>
      </button>
    `;
  }).join('');
  grid.innerHTML = `${menuHtml}
    <button class="new-menu-item danger" type="button" onclick="closeNewMenu(); logout()">
      <span class="new-menu-icon"><i class="fas fa-right-from-bracket"></i></span>
      <span class="new-menu-label">Keluar</span>
    </button>
  `;
}

function openNewMenu() {
  renderNewMenuItems();
  document.getElementById('newMenuOverlay')?.classList.add('open');
  document.getElementById('newMenuPanel')?.classList.add('open');
}

function closeNewMenu() {
  document.getElementById('newMenuOverlay')?.classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('newMenuPanel')?.classList.remove('open');
}

function toggleNewMenu() {
  const panel = document.getElementById('newMenuPanel');
  if(panel?.classList.contains('open')) closeNewMenu();
  else openNewMenu();
}

async function selectNewMenuItem(page) {
  closeNewMenu();
  await navigateTo(page);
}

async function navigateTo(page, options = {}) {
  closeSidebar();
  closeNewMenu();

  if(state.phone && !(await enforceUpdatingServerGate())) {
    return;
  }

  if(state.phone && !['beranda', 'penyesuaian'].includes(page) && !ensureAccountTuningComplete()) {
    return;
  }

  if(page !== 'findpvp' && isPvpMatchLocked()) {
    showToast('Selesaikan PvP dulu. Halaman dikunci sampai kedua pihak acc.', 'error');
    page = 'findpvp';
    options.replaceRoute = true;
  }
  
  setActiveNavigation(page);
  
  state.currentPage = page;
  
  switch(page) {
    case 'beranda':
      showPage('dashboard');
      updateGreeting();
      break;
    case 'penyesuaian':
      if(isAccountTuningComplete()) {
        showPage('dashboard');
        state.currentPage = 'beranda';
        saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
        setActiveNavigation('beranda');
        updateGreeting();
        break;
      }
      showPage('account-tune');
      renderDashboardAccountTuning();
      break;
    case 'materi':
      showPage('materi');
      await initMaterials();
      break;
    case 'giveaway':
      showPage('giveaway');
      await initGiveaways();
      break;
    case 'needhelp':
      showPage('needhelp');
      await initNeedHelp();
      break;
    case 'findpvp':
      showPage('findpvp');
      await initFindPvp();
      break;
    case 'shop':
      if(!(await requestShopAccess())) {
        const berandaShopItem = document.querySelector('.sidebar-item[data-page="beranda"]');
        if(berandaShopItem) {
          document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
          berandaShopItem.classList.add('active');
        }
        state.currentPage = 'beranda';
        showPage('dashboard');
        updateGreeting();
        break;
      }
      showPage('shop');
      await initShop();
      break;
    case 'stock':
      showPage('stock');
      initFruitStock();
      break;
    case 'games':
      showPage('games');
      await initGames();
      break;
    case 'random':
      showPage('random');
      await initRandom();
      break;
    case 'pinterest':
      showPage('pinterest');
      initPinterestSearch();
      break;
    case 'youtube':
      showPage('youtube');
      initYoutubeSearch();
      break;
    case 'lahelu':
      showPage('lahelu');
      initLaheluSearch();
      break;
    case 'downloader':
      showPage('downloader');
      initDownloader();
      break;
    case 'brat':
      showPage('brat');
      initBrat();
      break;
    case 'whatsapp':
      window.open('https://chat.whatsapp.com/Hh9PJBefjnpC6ao7rt6bWI', '_blank');
      showToast('Membuka grup WhatsApp...');
      showPage('dashboard');
      const berandaItem = document.querySelector('.sidebar-item[data-page="beranda"]');
      if (berandaItem) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
        berandaItem.classList.add('active');
      }
      state.currentPage = 'beranda';
      break;
    case 'tele':
      window.open('https://t.me/ArcZNewEra', '_blank');
      showToast('Membuka Telegram ArcZ...');
      showPage('dashboard');
      const berandaItem2 = document.querySelector('.sidebar-item[data-page="beranda"]');
      if (berandaItem2) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
        berandaItem2.classList.add('active');
      }
      state.currentPage = 'beranda';
      break;
    case 'report':
      showPage('report');
      break;
    case 'combo':
      if(!(await requestComboAccess())) {
        const berandaComboItem = document.querySelector('.sidebar-item[data-page="beranda"]');
        if(berandaComboItem) {
          document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
          berandaComboItem.classList.add('active');
        }
        state.currentPage = 'beranda';
        showPage('dashboard');
        updateGreeting();
        break;
      }
      showPage('combo');
      setComboStep(state.comboRequestStep || 0);
      refreshComboCooldownUI();
      break;
    case 'pengaturan':
      showPage('settings');
      initSettings();
      break;
    case 'tampilan':
      showPage('appearance');
      initSettings();
      break;
  }
  if(MEMBER_NAV_PAGES.includes(state.currentPage)) {
    saveMemberRoute(state.currentPage, options.replaceRoute === true);
  }
}

function goToDashboardFromMateri() {
  showPage('dashboard');
  state.currentPage = 'beranda';
  saveMemberRoute('beranda');
  document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
  const berandaItem = document.querySelector('.sidebar-item[data-page="beranda"]');
  if (berandaItem) berandaItem.classList.add('active');
  updateGreeting();
}

    showToast('Match PvP masih aktif. Kedua pihak wajib acc selesai dulu.', 'error');
    showPage('findpvp');
    state.currentPage = 'findpvp';
    saveMemberRoute('findpvp', true);
    renderFindPvp();
    return;
  }
  showPage('dashboard');
  state.currentPage = 'beranda';
  saveMemberRoute('beranda');
  document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
  const berandaItem = document.querySelector('.sidebar-item[data-page="beranda"]');
  if (berandaItem) berandaItem.classList.add('active');
  updateGreeting();
}

async function requestShopAccess() {
  const code = await appPrompt('Shop masih coming soon / whitelist only. Masukkan kode admin:', 'Shop Whitelist', 'Kode admin');
  if(code === null) {
    showToast('Shop whitelist only');
    return false;
  }
  if(code.trim() !== '0z') {
    showToast('Kode shop salah', 'error');
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
