function setActiveSettingsButtons() {
  state.theme = normalizeTheme(state.theme);
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceThemeOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.theme === state.theme);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceUiModeOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.uiMode === state.uiMode);
  });
  const accentText = document.getElementById('appearanceAccentText');
  const accentPicker = document.getElementById('appearanceAccentPicker');
  if(accentText && document.activeElement !== accentText) accentText.value = normalizeAccentColor(state.accentColor);
  if(accentPicker) accentPicker.value = normalizeAccentColor(state.accentColor);
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceAccentPresets .appearance-swatch').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', normalizeAccentColor(btn.dataset.accent) === normalizeAccentColor(state.accentColor));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsTimezoneOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.tz === state.timezone);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceTimezoneOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.tz === state.timezone);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsGenderOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.gender === state.gender);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsWinnerDisplayOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.winnerMode === state.winnerDisplayMode);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsInterestOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', state.interests.includes(btn.dataset.interest));
  });
  markMissingSettings();
}

function markMissingSettings() {
  const displayName = document.getElementById('settingsDisplayName');
  const uniqueCode = document.getElementById('settingsUniqueCode');
  const saved = state.savedSettings || JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  displayName?.classList.toggle('settings-missing', displayName.value.trim() !== (saved.displayName || ''));
  uniqueCode?.classList.toggle('settings-missing', false);

  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsGenderOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', btn.dataset.gender === state.gender && state.gender !== (saved.gender || ''));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsWinnerDisplayOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', btn.dataset.winnerMode === state.winnerDisplayMode && state.winnerDisplayMode !== (saved.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name'));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsInterestOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    const savedSelected = (saved.interests || []).includes(btn.dataset.interest);
    const currentSelected = (state.interests || []).includes(btn.dataset.interest);
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', currentSelected !== savedSelected);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceThemeOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', btn.dataset.theme === state.theme && state.theme !== normalizeTheme(saved.theme || 'light'));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceUiModeOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', btn.dataset.uiMode === state.uiMode && state.uiMode !== normalizeUiMode(saved.uiMode || 'old'));
  });
  const accentChanged = normalizeAccentColor(state.accentColor) !== normalizeAccentColor(saved.accentColor || '#27a44f');
  document.getElementById('appearanceAccentText')?.classList.toggle('settings-missing', accentChanged);
  document.getElementById('appearanceAccentPicker')?.classList.toggle('settings-missing', accentChanged);
  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsTimezoneOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', btn.dataset.tz === state.timezone && state.timezone !== normalizeDisplayTimezone(saved.timezone || NEVERERA_TIMEZONE));
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#appearanceTimezoneOptions .settings-option').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('settings-missing', btn.dataset.tz === state.timezone && state.timezone !== normalizeDisplayTimezone(saved.timezone || NEVERERA_TIMEZONE));
  });
}

function initSettings() {
  const hasUnsaved = document.querySelector('.settings-missing');
  const active = document.activeElement;
  if(hasUnsaved || (active && active.closest && (active.closest('#page-settings') || active.closest('#page-appearance')))) {
    markMissingSettings();
    return;
  }
  const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  state.theme = session.theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';
  state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(session.uiMode || state.uiMode || 'old');
  state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(session.accentColor || state.accentColor || '#27a44f');
  state.gender = session.gender || state.gender || '';
  state.winnerDisplayMode = session.winnerDisplayMode || state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
  state.interests = normalizeInterests(session.interests).length ? normalizeInterests(session.interests) : normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  state.uniqueCode = session.uniqueCode || state.uniqueCode || '';
  state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(session.timezone || state.timezone);
  state.savedSettings = {
    theme: state.theme,
    uiMode: state.uiMode,
    accentColor: state.accentColor,
    gender: state.gender,
    interests: normalizeInterests(state.interests),
    uniqueCode: state.uniqueCode,
    displayName: session.displayName || '',
    winnerDisplayMode: state.winnerDisplayMode,
    timezone: state.timezone
  };
  document.getElementById('settingsDisplayName').value = session.displayName || '';
  if(document.getElementById('settingsUniqueCode')) document.getElementById('settingsUniqueCode').value = state.uniqueCode || '';
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
  ['settingsDisplayName', 'appearanceAccentText'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el && !el.dataset.missingListener) {
      el.addEventListener('input', markMissingSettings);
      el.dataset.missingListener = 'true';
    }
  });
}

function setSettingsTheme(theme) {
  state.theme = theme;
  applyTheme(theme);
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

function setSettingsUiMode(mode) {
  state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(mode);
  applyUiMode(state.uiMode);
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

function setSettingsAccentColor(color) {
  const raw = String(color || '').trim();
  const text = document.getElementById('appearanceAccentText');
  const picker = document.getElementById('appearanceAccentPicker');
  if(text && document.activeElement !== text) text.value = raw || normalizeAccentColor(state.accentColor);
  if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(raw)) {
    markMissingSettings();
    return;
  }
  state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(raw);
  if(text) text.value = state.accentColor;
  if(picker) picker.value = state.accentColor;
  applyAccentColor(state.accentColor);
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

function resetAccentColorDefault() {
  setSettingsAccentColor('#27a44f');
  markMissingSettings();
  showToast('Warna theme dikembalikan ke default');
}

function setSettingsTimezone(tz) {
  state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(tz);
  updateClock();
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

function setSettingsGender(gender) {
  state.gender = gender;
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

function setSettingsWinnerDisplayMode(mode) {
  state.winnerDisplayMode = mode;
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

function resetSettingsField(field) {
  const saved = state.savedSettings || JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  if(field === 'theme') {
    state.theme = saved.theme || 'light';
    applyTheme(state.theme);
  } else if(field === 'uiMode') {
    state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(saved.uiMode || 'old');
    applyUiMode(state.uiMode);
  } else if(field === 'accentColor') {
    state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(saved.accentColor || '#27a44f');
    applyAccentColor(state.accentColor);
  } else if(field === 'timezone') {
    state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(saved.timezone || NEVERERA_TIMEZONE);
    updateClock();
  } else if(field === 'winnerDisplayMode') {
    state.winnerDisplayMode = saved.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
  } else if(field === 'gender') {
    state.gender = saved.gender || '';
  } else if(field === 'interests') {
    state.interests = normalizeInterests(saved.interests);
  }
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
  markMissingSettings();
  showToast('Perubahan dikembalikan');
}

function toggleSettingsInterest(key) {
  state.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
  if(state.interests.includes(key)) {
    state.interests = state.interests.filter(item => item !== key);
  } else {
    state.interests.push(key);
  }
  setActiveSettingsButtons();
}

async function requestComboAccess() {
  let completedCount = normalizeMaterialIds(materialState.completed).length;
  if(completedCount < 5) {
    const localCount = normalizeMaterialIds(loadLocalMaterialProgress()).length;
    completedCount = Math.max(completedCount, localCount);
  }
  if(completedCount < 5 && sbClient && state.phone) {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await sbClient
        .from('users')
        .select('material_completed')
        .eq('phone', state.phone)
        .maybeSingle();
      if(!error) completedCount = Math.max(completedCount, normalizeMaterialIds(data?.material_completed || []).length);
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('Gagal cek progress materi untuk combo:', e);
    }
  }
  if(completedCount < 5) {
    showToast(`Request Combo terbuka setelah kamu menyelesaikan 5 materi. Progress kamu: ${completedCount}/5`, 'error');
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function setComboStep(step) {
  const maxStep = document.querySelectorAll('.combo-step').length - 1;
  state.comboRequestStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxStep, step));
  document.querySelectorAll('.combo-step').forEach(el => {
    el.classList.toggle('active', Number(el.dataset.comboStep) === state.comboRequestStep);
  });
  const progressText = document.getElementById('comboProgressText');
  const progressFill = document.getElementById('comboProgressFill');
  const totalQuestions = Math.max(1, maxStep);
  const label = state.comboRequestStep >= maxStep
    ? 'Review request'
    : `Pertanyaan ${state.comboRequestStep + 1} dari ${totalQuestions}`;
  if(progressText) progressText.textContent = label;
  if(progressFill) progressFill.style.width = `${Math.round((state.comboRequestStep / maxStep) * 100)}%`;
  if(state.comboRequestStep === maxStep) renderComboReview();
}

function nextComboStep() {
  setComboStep((state.comboRequestStep || 0) + 1);
}

function prevComboStep() {
  setComboStep((state.comboRequestStep || 0) - 1);
}

function editComboStep(step) {
  state.comboInlineEditStep = step;
  renderComboReview();
}

function getComboInput(id) {
  return document.getElementById(id)?.value.trim() || '';
}

function setComboInput(id, value) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if(el) el.value = value;
}

function getComboStat(id, label) {
  const raw = getComboInput(id);
  if(!raw) return null;
  const value = Number(raw);
  if(!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 1 || value > 2800) {
    throw new Error(`${label} harus angka 1-2800`);
  }
  return value;
}

function resetComboRequestForm() {
  [
    'comboFruit','comboFightingStyle','comboSword','comboGun','comboRace','comboLevel',
    'comboMainBuild',
    'comboDeviceBrand','comboDeviceModel','comboInternetLag','comboDeviceLag',
    'comboPlaystyle','comboExtraQuestions'
  ].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.value = '';
  });
  state.comboInlineEditStep = null;
  setComboStep(0);
}

async function confirmResetComboRequestForm() {
  if(!(await appConfirm('Reset semua jawaban request combo dan mulai dari awal?', 'Reset Form'))) return;
  resetComboRequestForm();
  showToast('Form request combo sudah direset');
}

function getComboReviewItems() {
  return [
    { step: 0, id: 'comboFruit', label: 'Fruit', value: getComboInput('comboFruit') },
    { step: 1, id: 'comboFightingStyle', label: 'Fighting style', value: getComboInput('comboFightingStyle') },
    { step: 2, id: 'comboSword', label: 'Sword', value: getComboInput('comboSword') },
    { step: 3, id: 'comboGun', label: 'Gun', value: getComboInput('comboGun') },
    { step: 4, id: 'comboRace', label: 'Race', value: getComboInput('comboRace') },
    { step: 5, id: 'comboLevel', label: 'Level / sea', value: getComboInput('comboLevel') },
    { step: 6, id: 'comboMainBuild', label: 'Main', value: getComboInput('comboMainBuild') },
    { step: 7, id: 'comboDeviceBrand', label: 'Merek device', value: getComboInput('comboDeviceBrand') },
    { step: 8, id: 'comboDeviceModel', label: 'Jenis device', value: getComboInput('comboDeviceModel') },
    { step: 9, id: 'comboInternetLag', label: 'Internet', value: getComboInput('comboInternetLag') },
    { step: 10, id: 'comboDeviceLag', label: 'Performa device', value: getComboInput('comboDeviceLag') },
    { step: 11, id: 'comboPlaystyle', label: 'Gaya combo', value: getComboInput('comboPlaystyle') },
    { step: 12, id: 'comboExtraQuestions', label: 'Pertanyaan tambahan', value: getComboInput('comboExtraQuestions') }
  ];
}

function getComboReviewEditControl(item) {
  const source = document.getElementById(item.id);
  const editId = `comboReviewEdit_${item.step}`;
  if(!source) return '';
  if(source.tagName === 'SELECT') {
    const options = [...source.options]
      .filter(option => !option.disabled || option.value)
      .map(option => `<option value="${escapeHtml(option.value)}" ${option.value === item.value ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(option.textContent)}</option>`)
      .join('');
    return `<select class="settings-input" id="${editId}">${options}</select>`;
  }
  if(source.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') {
    return `<textarea class="settings-input" id="${editId}" rows="4">${escapeHtml(item.value)}</textarea>`;
  }
  return `<input class="settings-input" id="${editId}" value="${escapeHtml(item.value)}">`;
}

function saveComboReviewEdit(step) {
  const item = getComboReviewItems().find(entry => entry.step === step);
  const editEl = document.getElementById(`comboReviewEdit_${step}`);
  if(!item || !editEl) return;
  setComboInput(item.id, editEl.value);
  state.comboInlineEditStep = null;
  renderComboReview();
}

function cancelComboReviewEdit() {
  state.comboInlineEditStep = null;
  renderComboReview();
}

function renderComboReview() {
  const el = document.getElementById('comboReviewList');
  if(!el) return;
  el.innerHTML = getComboReviewItems().map(item => {
    const editing = state.comboInlineEditStep === item.step;
    return `
    <div class="combo-review-row">
      <div>
        <div class="combo-review-label">${escapeHtml(item.label)}</div>
        ${editing ? `
          <div class="combo-review-edit">
            ${getComboReviewEditControl(item)}
            <button class="combo-edit-btn" onclick="saveComboReviewEdit(${item.step})" title="Simpan"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></button>
            <button class="combo-edit-btn" onclick="cancelComboReviewEdit()" title="Batal"><i class="fas fa-xmark"></i></button>
          </div>
        ` : `<div class="combo-review-value">${escapeHtml(item.value || '-')}</div>`}
      </div>
      ${editing ? '' : `<button class="combo-edit-btn" onclick="editComboStep(${item.step})" title="Edit"><i class="fas fa-pen"></i></button>`}
    </div>
  `;
  }).join('');
}

const COMBO_REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;

function getComboCooldownKey() {
  return `ne_combo_request_last_${state.phone || state.clientIp || 'guest'}`;
}

function getLocalComboCooldownRemaining() {
  const last = Number(localStorage.getItem(getComboCooldownKey()) || 0);
  if(!last) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, COMBO_REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS - (Date.now() - last));
}

function formatCooldown(ms) {
  const totalSeconds = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
  const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
  const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
  return `${minutes}:${String(seconds).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function updateComboCooldownDisplay() {
  const remaining = Math.max(0, state.comboCooldownUntil - Date.now());
  const card = document.getElementById('comboRequestCard');
  const timeEl = document.getElementById('comboCooldownTime');
  if(timeEl) timeEl.textContent = formatCooldown(remaining);
  if(card) card.classList.toggle('cooldown', remaining > 0);
  if(remaining <= 0) stopComboCooldown();
}

function startComboCooldown(ms) {
  stopComboCooldown();
  state.comboCooldownUntil = Date.now() + Math.max(0, ms);
  updateComboCooldownDisplay();
  if(ms > 0) {
    state.comboCooldownInterval = setInterval(updateComboCooldownDisplay, 1000);
  }
}

function stopComboCooldown() {
  if(state.comboCooldownInterval) {
    clearInterval(state.comboCooldownInterval);
    state.comboCooldownInterval = null;
  }
  state.comboCooldownUntil = 0;
  const card = document.getElementById('comboRequestCard');
  if(card) card.classList.remove('cooldown');
}

async function getRemoteComboCooldownRemaining() {
  if(!sbClient) return 0;
  try {
    const ipAddress = state.clientIp || await ensureClientIp();
    let resetAt = 0;
    if(state.phone) {
      const { data: userData } = await sbClient.from('users')
        .select('combo_cooldown_reset_at')
        .eq('phone', state.phone)
        .maybeSingle();
      resetAt = userData?.combo_cooldown_reset_at
        ? new Date(userData.combo_cooldown_reset_at).getTime()
        : 0;
    }
    let query = sbClient.from('combo_requests')
      .select('created_at')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(1);
    if(state.phone && ipAddress) {
      query = query.or(`user_phone.eq.${state.phone},client_ip.eq.${ipAddress}`);
    } else if(state.phone) {
      query = query.eq('user_phone', state.phone);
    } else if(ipAddress) {
      query = query.eq('client_ip', ipAddress);
    } else {
      return 0;
    }
    const { data, error } = await query;
    if(error || !data?.length) return 0;
    const last = new Date(data[0].created_at).getTime();
    if(!Number.isFinite(last)) return 0;
    if(resetAt && last <= resetAt) return 0;
    return Math.max(0, COMBO_REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS - (Date.now() - last));
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal cek cooldown combo:', e);
    return 0;
  }
}

async function getComboCooldownRemaining() {
  if(sbClient) return await getRemoteComboCooldownRemaining();
  return getLocalComboCooldownRemaining();
}

async function refreshComboCooldownUI() {
  const remaining = await getComboCooldownRemaining();
  if(remaining > 0) startComboCooldown(remaining);
  else stopComboCooldown();
}

function setComboSubmitBusy(isBusy) {
  state.comboSubmitting = isBusy;
  const btn = document.getElementById('comboSubmitBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  btn.disabled = isBusy;
  btn.innerHTML = isBusy
    ? '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Mengirim...'
    : '<i class="fas fa-paper-plane"></i> Kirim Request';
}

async function submitComboRequest() {
  if(state.comboSubmitting) return;
  setComboSubmitBusy(true);
  showLoading('Mengirim request combo...');
  try {
    if(!(await requestComboAccess())) return;
    const cooldownRemaining = await getComboCooldownRemaining();
    if(cooldownRemaining > 0) {
      startComboCooldown(cooldownRemaining);
      showToast(`Tunggu ${formatCooldown(cooldownRemaining)} sebelum request combo lagi`, 'error');
      return;
    }
    const comboMainBuild = getComboInput('comboMainBuild');
    const comboExtraQuestions = getComboInput('comboExtraQuestions');
    const comboNotes = [
      comboMainBuild ? `Main: ${comboMainBuild}` : '',
      comboExtraQuestions
    ].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    const payload = {
      user_phone: state.phone || null,
      display_name: state.displayName || null,
      fruit: getComboInput('comboFruit'),
      fighting_style: getComboInput('comboFightingStyle'),
      sword: getComboInput('comboSword'),
      gun: getComboInput('comboGun'),
      race: getComboInput('comboRace'),
      level_text: getComboInput('comboLevel'),
      melee_stat: null,
      defense_stat: null,
      sword_stat: null,
      gun_stat: null,
      fruit_stat: null,
      device_brand: getComboInput('comboDeviceBrand'),
      device_model: getComboInput('comboDeviceModel'),
      internet_lag: getComboInput('comboInternetLag'),
      device_lag: getComboInput('comboDeviceLag'),
      playstyle: getComboInput('comboPlaystyle'),
      extra_questions: comboNotes,
      client_ip: state.clientIp || await ensureClientIp(),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    if(!payload.fruit || !payload.fighting_style || !comboMainBuild || !payload.device_brand || !payload.device_model || !payload.playstyle) {
      showToast('Isi fruit, fighting style, main, merek device, jenis device, dan gaya main dulu', 'error');
      return;
    }
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('combo_requests').insert(payload);
    if(error) throw error;
    localStorage.setItem(getComboCooldownKey(), String(Date.now()));
    resetComboRequestForm();
    startComboCooldown(COMBO_REQUEST_COOLDOWN_MS);
    showToast('Request combo terkirim. Tunggu admin review.');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Combo request error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal mengirim request combo', 'error');
  } finally {
    setComboSubmitBusy(false);
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function submitBugReport() {
  const category = document.getElementById('bugCategory')?.value || 'lainnya';
  const message = document.getElementById('bugMessage')?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!message) {
    showToast('Isi laporan bug dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Mengirim report...');
  try {
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('bug_reports').insert({
      user_phone: state.phone || null,
      display_name: state.displayName || null,
      category,
      message,
      page: state.currentPage || null,
      client_ip: state.clientIp || null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString()
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    document.getElementById('bugMessage').value = '';
    showToast('Report terkirim. Makasih sudah bantu lapor.');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Bug report error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal mengirim report', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function formatRupiah(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'IDR',
    maximumFractionDigits: 0
  }).format(value);
}

async function loadShopProducts() {
  if(!sbClient) {
    state.shopProducts = SHOP_PRODUCTS;
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('shop_products')
      .select('*')
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .order('order_num', { ascending: true })
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    if(error) throw error;
    state.shopProducts = (data || []).map(item => ({
      id: String(item.id),
      name: item.name,
      category: item.category || 'Lainnya',
      price: Number(item.price || 0),
      rating: Number(item.rating || 5),
      sold: Number(item.sold || 0),
      icon: item.icon || 'fa-box',
      badge: item.badge || '',
      imageUrl: item.image_url || '',
      stock: item.stock,
      description: item.description || ''
    }));
    if(!state.shopProducts.length) state.shopProducts = SHOP_PRODUCTS;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal memuat produk shop:', e);
    state.shopProducts = SHOP_PRODUCTS;
  }
}

async function initShop() {
  loadShopCart();
  await loadShopProducts();
  renderShopCategories();
  renderShop();
}

function renderShopCategories() {
  const el = document.getElementById('shopCategories');
  if(!el) return;
  const products = state.shopProducts.length ? state.shopProducts : SHOP_PRODUCTS;
  const categories = ['Semua', ...new Set(products.map(item => item.category))];
  el.innerHTML = categories.map(category => `
    <button class="shop-chip ${state.shopCategory === category ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setShopCategory('${category}')">
      ${escapeHtml(category)}
    </button>
  `).join('');
}

function setShopCategory(category) {
  state.shopCategory = category;
  renderShopCategories();
  renderShop();
}

function getFilteredShopProducts() {
  const search = document.getElementById('shopSearchInput')?.value.trim().toLowerCase() || '';
  const sort = document.getElementById('shopSort')?.value || 'popular';
  const source = state.shopProducts.length ? state.shopProducts : SHOP_PRODUCTS;
  let products = source.filter(item => {
    const matchCategory = state.shopCategory === 'Semua' || item.category === state.shopCategory;
    const matchSearch = !search || `${item.name} ${item.category}`.toLowerCase().includes(search);
    return matchCategory && matchSearch;
  });
  products = [...products].sort((a, b) => {
    if(sort === 'low') return a.price - b.price;
    if(sort === 'high') return b.price - a.price;
    if(sort === 'rating') return b.rating - a.rating;
    return b.sold - a.sold;
  });
  return products;
}

function renderShop() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('shopGrid');
  const count = document.getElementById('shopResultCount');
  if(!grid) return;
  const products = getFilteredShopProducts();
  if(count) count.textContent = `${products.length} produk`;
  updateShopCartCount();
  if(!products.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div class="shop-empty"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass"></i><br>Produk tidak ditemukan.</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = products.map(item => `
    <div class="shop-product">
      <div class="shop-product-img">
        <span class="shop-badge">${escapeHtml(item.badge)}</span>
        ${item.imageUrl
          ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(item.imageUrl)}" alt="${escapeHtml(item.name)}">`
          : `<i class="fas ${escapeHtml(item.icon)}"></i>`}
      </div>
      <div class="shop-product-body">
        <div class="shop-product-name">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</div>
        <div class="shop-product-price">${formatRupiah(item.price)}</div>
        <div class="shop-product-meta">
          <span><i class="fas fa-star"></i> ${item.rating.toFixed(1)}</span>
          <span>${item.sold} terjual</span>
        </div>
        <button class="shop-buy-btn" onclick="addShopCart('${item.id}')">
          <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Keranjang
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function addShopCart(productId) {
  const products = state.shopProducts.length ? state.shopProducts : SHOP_PRODUCTS;
  const product = products.find(item => String(item.id) === String(productId));
  if(!product) return;
  state.shopCart.push(String(productId));
  saveShopCart();
  updateShopCartCount();
  renderShopCart();
  showToast(`${product.name} masuk keranjang`);
}

function getShopCartKey() {
  return `ne_shop_cart_${state.phone || 'guest'}`;
}

function loadShopCart() {
  try {
    state.shopCart = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(getShopCartKey()) || '[]');
  } catch(e) {
    state.shopCart = [];
  }
}

function saveShopCart() {
  localStorage.setItem(getShopCartKey(), JSON.stringify(state.shopCart));
}

function updateShopCartCount() {
  const count = document.getElementById('shopCartCount');
  if(count) count.textContent = state.shopCart.length;
}

function getShopProductSource() {
  return state.shopProducts.length ? state.shopProducts : SHOP_PRODUCTS;
}

function getShopCartItems() {
  const products = getShopProductSource();
  const qtyMap = state.shopCart.reduce((map, id) => {
    const key = String(id);
    map[key] = (map[key] || 0) + 1;
    return map;
  }, {});
  return Object.entries(qtyMap)
    .map(([id, qty]) => {
      const product = products.find(item => String(item.id) === String(id));
      return product ? { ...product, qty } : null;
    })
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function getShopCartTotal(items = getShopCartItems()) {
  return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (Number(item.price || 0) * item.qty), 0);
}

function showShopCartSummary() {
  openShopCart();
}

function openShopCart() {
  renderShopCart();
  document.getElementById('shopCartOverlay')?.classList.add('open');
}

function closeShopCart(event) {
  if(event && event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
  document.getElementById('shopCartOverlay')?.classList.remove('open');
}

function renderShopCart() {
  const body = document.getElementById('shopCartBody');
  const totalEl = document.getElementById('shopCartTotal');
  if(!body || !totalEl) return;
  const items = getShopCartItems();
  const total = getShopCartTotal(items);
  totalEl.textContent = formatRupiah(total);
  if(!items.length) {
    body.innerHTML = `
      <div class="shop-cart-empty-panel">
        <div>
          <div style="font-size:34px;margin-bottom:8px"><i class="fas fa-cart-shopping"></i></div>
          <div>Keranjang masih kosong.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    return;
  }
  body.innerHTML = items.map(item => `
    <div class="shop-cart-item">
      <div class="shop-cart-img">
        ${item.imageUrl
          ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(item.imageUrl)}" alt="${escapeHtml(item.name)}">`
          : `<i class="fas ${escapeHtml(item.icon || 'fa-box')}"></i>`}
      </div>
      <div class="shop-cart-info">
        <div class="shop-cart-name">${escapeHtml(item.name || 'Produk')}</div>
        <div class="shop-cart-price">${formatRupiah(item.price)}</div>
        <div class="shop-cart-row">
          <div class="shop-cart-qty">
            <button onclick="changeShopCartQty('${escapeHtml(String(item.id))}', -1)"><i class="fas fa-minus"></i></button>
            <span>${item.qty}</span>
            <button onclick="changeShopCartQty('${escapeHtml(String(item.id))}', 1)"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i></button>
          </div>
          <button class="shop-cart-remove" onclick="removeShopCartItem('${escapeHtml(String(item.id))}')">Hapus</button>
        </div>
        <div class="shop-cart-row">
          <span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted)">Subtotal</span>
          <strong style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text)">${formatRupiah(Number(item.price || 0) * item.qty)}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function changeShopCartQty(productId, delta) {
  const id = String(productId);
  if(delta > 0) {
    state.shopCart.push(id);
  } else {
    const idx = state.shopCart.findIndex(item => String(item) === id);
    if(idx >= 0) state.shopCart.splice(idx, 1);
  }
  saveShopCart();
  updateShopCartCount();
  renderShopCart();
}

function removeShopCartItem(productId) {
  const id = String(productId);
  state.shopCart = state.shopCart.filter(item => String(item) !== id);
  saveShopCart();
  updateShopCartCount();
  renderShopCart();
}

function clearShopCart() {
  if(!state.shopCart.length) return;
  state.shopCart = [];
  saveShopCart();
  updateShopCartCount();
  renderShopCart();
  showToast('Keranjang dikosongkan');
}

function buildShopCheckoutMessage() {
  const items = getShopCartItems();
  const note = document.getElementById('shopCartNote')?.value.trim() || '-';
  const lines = items.map((item, index) =>
    `${index + 1}. ${item.name} x${item.qty} = ${formatRupiah(Number(item.price || 0) * item.qty)}`
  );
  return [
    'Halo admin, saya mau checkout:',
    ...lines,
    `Total: ${formatRupiah(getShopCartTotal(items))}`,
    `Nama: ${state.displayName || 'Member'}`,
    `Nomor: ${state.phone ? formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone) : '-'}`,
    `Catatan: ${note}`
  ].join('\n');
}

async function checkoutShopCart() {
  if(!state.shopCart.length) {
    showToast('Keranjang masih kosong', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const message = buildShopCheckoutMessage();
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard?.writeText(message);
    showToast('Detail checkout disalin');
  } catch(e) {
    showToast('Checkout siap dikirim');
  }
  window.open(`https://t.me/ArcZNewEra?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`, '_blank');
}

let currentGameQuestion = null;

function setGameRoundState(mode = 'idle') {
  const playing = mode === 'playing';
  const actions = document.getElementById('gamesActions');
  const findBtn = document.getElementById('gameFindBtn');
  const submitBtn = document.getElementById('gameSubmitBtn');
  const surrenderBtn = document.getElementById('gameSurrenderBtn');
  const inputEl = document.getElementById('gamesUserAnswer');
  actions?.classList.toggle('playing', playing);
  if(findBtn) findBtn.style.display = playing ? 'none' : '';
  if(submitBtn) submitBtn.style.display = playing ? '' : 'none';
  if(surrenderBtn) surrenderBtn.style.display = playing ? '' : 'none';
  if(inputEl) {
    inputEl.disabled = !playing;
    inputEl.placeholder = playing ? 'Jawaban kamu' : 'Cari soal dulu';
  }
}

function resetGameRound(message = 'Pilih game lalu cari soal.') {
  currentGameQuestion = null;
  const questionEl = document.getElementById('gamesQuestionText');
  const answerEl = document.getElementById('gamesAnswerText');
  const inputEl = document.getElementById('gamesUserAnswer');
  if(questionEl) questionEl.textContent = message;
  if(answerEl) {
    answerEl.textContent = '';
    answerEl.classList.remove('show');
  }
  if(inputEl) inputEl.value = '';
  renderGameMedia('');
  setGameRoundState('idle');
}

function showGameResultAlert({ title = 'Selesai', text = '', prize = '', icon = 'fa-trophy', party = false } = {}) {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('winnerAlertOverlay');
  const titleEl = overlay?.querySelector('.winner-alert-title');
  const iconEl = overlay?.querySelector('.winner-alert-icon i');
  const textEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertText');
  const prizeEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertPrize');
  const hintEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertHint');
  if(!overlay || !titleEl || !iconEl || !textEl || !prizeEl) return;
  state.winnerAlertOpen = true;
  state.activeWinnerAlertKey = '';
  state.winnerAlertTapCount = 0;
  titleEl.textContent = title;
  iconEl.className = `fas ${icon}`;
  textEl.textContent = text;
  prizeEl.textContent = prize;
  prizeEl.style.display = prize ? '' : 'none';
  if(hintEl) hintEl.textContent = 'Ketuk 2x layar untuk lanjut';
  overlay.querySelectorAll('.winner-burst').forEach(item => item.remove());
  if(party) createWinnerBurst(overlay);
  overlay.classList.add('open');
}

function updateGameHint(slug = '') {
  const hint = document.getElementById('gamesHintText');
  if(!hint) return;
  if(slug === 'tebakangka') {
    hint.textContent = 'Petunjuk Tebak Angka: jawab pakai huruf, bukan angka. Contoh 74 ditulis: Tujuh puluh empat. Huruf pertama kapital.';
    hint.classList.add('show');
  } else {
    hint.textContent = '';
    hint.classList.remove('show');
  }
}

function initGames() {
  const select = document.getElementById('gamesCategory');
  if(!select) return;
  if(!select.dataset.ready) {
    select.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected hidden>Pilih game</option>' +
      GAMES_CATALOG.map(item => `<option value="${item.slug}">${escapeHtml(item.label)}</option>`).join('');
    select.addEventListener('change', () => {
      updateGameHint(select.value);
      resetGameRound('Pilih game lalu cari soal.');
    });
    select.dataset.ready = 'true';
  }
  updateGameHint(select.value);
  setGameRoundState(currentGameQuestion ? 'playing' : 'idle');
}

function getGamePayload(json) {
  let payload = json?.data || json?.game || json?.result || json;
  for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if(payload?.data && typeof payload.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.data)) {
      payload = payload.data;
    }
  }
  return payload;
}

function pickGameField(data, keys) {
  for(const key of keys) {
    const value = data?.[key];
    if(value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') return value;
  }
  return '';
}

function normalizeGameQuestion(json) {
  const data = getGamePayload(json);
  const question = pickGameField(data, ['soal','question','pertanyaan','deskripsi','desc','teks','text','str','clue','petunjuk','kalimat','lirik']);
  const answer = pickGameField(data, ['jawaban','answer','result','hasil','name','nama','judul']);
  const artist = pickGameField(data, ['artis','artist','penyanyi']);
  const hint = pickGameField(data, ['hint','tipe','type','kategori']);
  const media = pickGameField(data, ['image','img','gambar','url','logo','lagu','audio','music','mp3','video','mp4','media']);
  const options = pickGameField(data, ['pilihan','opsi','options','choices']);
  const lines = [];
  if(question) lines.push(String(question));
  if(hint) lines.push(`Hint: ${hint}`);
  if(!question && media) lines.push('Perhatikan media berikut, lalu jawab.');
  if(Array.isArray(options)) lines.push(`Pilihan: ${options.join(', ')}`);
  if(!lines.length) lines.push(JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
  const answerParts = [answer, artist].filter(Boolean);
  return {
    question: lines.join('\n'),
    answer: Array.isArray(answer) ? answer.join(', ') : String(answerParts.join(' - ') || '-'),
    media: String(media || '')
  };
}

function renderGameMedia(url = '') {
  const box = document.getElementById('gamesMediaBox');
  if(!box) return;
  box.classList.remove('show');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) return;
  const safeUrl = escapeHtml(url);
  const cleanUrl = url.split('?')[0].toLowerCase();
  if(/\.(mp3|wav|ogg|m4a)$/i.test(cleanUrl)) {
    box.innerHTML = `<audio controls src="${safeUrl}"></audio>`;
  } else if(/\.(mp4|webm|mov|m3u8)$/i.test(cleanUrl)) {
    box.innerHTML = `<video controls src="${safeUrl}"></video>`;
  } else {
    box.innerHTML = `<img src="${safeUrl}" alt="Media soal game" loading="lazy">`;
  }
  box.classList.add('show');
}

function normalizeGameAnswer(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

let gameBurstTimer = null;

function triggerGameCorrectBurst() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('gameBurstOverlay');
  if(!overlay) return;
  clearTimeout(gameBurstTimer);
  createWinnerBurst(overlay);
  overlay.classList.add('show');
  gameBurstTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    overlay.classList.remove('show');
    overlay.querySelectorAll('.winner-burst').forEach(item => item.remove());
  }, 2200);
}

async function loadGameQuestion() {
  initGames();
  const slug = document.getElementById('gamesCategory')?.value;
  if(!slug) {
    showToast('Pilih game dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  updateGameHint(slug);
  setGameRoundState('idle');
  const questionEl = document.getElementById('gamesQuestionText');
  const answerEl = document.getElementById('gamesAnswerText');
  const inputEl = document.getElementById('gamesUserAnswer');
  if(questionEl) questionEl.textContent = 'Mengambil soal...';
  renderGameMedia('');
  if(answerEl) {
    answerEl.classList.remove('show');
    answerEl.textContent = '';
  }
  if(inputEl) inputEl.value = '';
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${GAMES_API_BASE}/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`);
    if(!res.ok) {
      let message = `HTTP ${res.status}`;
      try {
        const errorData = await res.json();
        message = errorData?.error || errorData?.message || message;
      } catch(e) {}
      throw new Error(message);
    }
    currentGameQuestion = normalizeGameQuestion(await res.json());
    if(questionEl) questionEl.textContent = currentGameQuestion.question;
    renderGameMedia(currentGameQuestion.media);
    setGameRoundState('playing');
    inputEl?.focus();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Games API error:', e);
    currentGameQuestion = null;
    setGameRoundState('idle');
    if(questionEl) questionEl.textContent = slug === 'kabupaten'
      ? 'Game Tebak Kabupaten sedang error dari server. Coba lagi nanti atau pilih game lain.'
      : `Gagal mengambil soal. ${e.message || 'Coba kategori lain atau refresh.'}`;
    showToast(slug === 'kabupaten' ? 'Kabupaten sedang error dari API' : 'Gagal mengambil soal game', 'error');
  }
}

function checkGameAnswer() {
  if(!currentGameQuestion) {
    showToast('Ambil soal dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const rawAnswer = document.getElementById('gamesUserAnswer')?.value || '';
  if(!rawAnswer.trim()) {
    showToast('Isi jawaban dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const userAnswer = normalizeGameAnswer(rawAnswer);
  const correctAnswer = normalizeGameAnswer(currentGameQuestion.answer.split(' - ')[0] || currentGameQuestion.answer);
  if(userAnswer && correctAnswer && (userAnswer === correctAnswer || correctAnswer.includes(userAnswer) || userAnswer.includes(correctAnswer))) {
    showToast('Benar!');
    triggerGameCorrectBurst();
    const answer = currentGameQuestion.answer;
    resetGameRound('Kamu menang. Cari soal lagi kalau mau lanjut.');
    showGameResultAlert({
      title: 'Kamu Menang',
      text: 'Jawaban kamu benar. Ronde ini selesai dengan mulus.',
      prize: `Jawaban: ${answer}`,
      icon: 'fa-trophy',
      party: true
    });
  } else {
    showToast('Belum tepat, coba lagi', 'error');
  }
}

function handleGameAnswerKey(event) {
  if(event.key !== 'Enter') return;
  event.preventDefault();
  checkGameAnswer();
}

function showGameAnswer() {
  const answerEl = document.getElementById('gamesAnswerText');
  if(!currentGameQuestion) {
    showToast('Ambil soal dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(answerEl) {
    answerEl.textContent = `Jawaban: ${currentGameQuestion.answer}`;
    answerEl.classList.add('show');
  }
  const answer = currentGameQuestion.answer;
  resetGameRound('Kamu menyerah. Cari soal lagi kalau mau ulang.');
  showGameResultAlert({
    title: 'Ronde Selesai',
    text: 'Kamu menyerah di soal ini. Bisa langsung cari soal baru.',
    prize: `Jawaban: ${answer}`,
    icon: 'fa-flag',
    party: false
  });
}

function initRandom() {
  const select = document.getElementById('randomCategory');
  if(!select) return;
  if(!select.dataset.ready) {
    select.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected hidden>Pilih random</option>' +

function flattenRandomPayload(json) {
  if(Array.isArray(json?.images)) return json.images[Math.floor(Math.random() * json.images.length)] || {};
  if(Array.isArray(json?.results)) return json.results[Math.floor(Math.random() * json.results.length)] || {};
  let payload = json?.data || json?.result || json;
  if(Array.isArray(payload?.images)) payload = payload.images[Math.floor(Math.random() * payload.images.length)] || {};
  if(Array.isArray(payload?.results)) payload = payload.results[Math.floor(Math.random() * payload.results.length)] || {};
  if(Array.isArray(payload)) payload = payload[Math.floor(Math.random() * payload.length)] || {};
  for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if(payload?.data && typeof payload.data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload.data)) payload = payload.data;
    if(Array.isArray(payload?.images)) payload = payload.images[Math.floor(Math.random() * payload.images.length)] || {};
    if(Array.isArray(payload?.results)) payload = payload.results[Math.floor(Math.random() * payload.results.length)] || {};
    if(Array.isArray(payload)) payload = payload[Math.floor(Math.random() * payload.length)] || {};
  }
  return payload;
}

function findRandomMedia(data) {
  if(typeof data === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(data)) return data;
  if(!data || typeof data !== 'object') return '';
  const direct = pickGameField(data, ['url','image','img','gambar','media','video','mp4','audio','mp3','file','source']);
  if(direct) return String(direct);
  if(Array.isArray(data.results) && data.results[0]) return findRandomMedia(data.results[0]);
  if(Array.isArray(data.images) && data.images[0]) return findRandomMedia(data.images[0]);
  if(Array.isArray(data.content)) {
    const contentMedia = data.content.find(item => item?.value && /^https?:\/\//i.test(item.value));
    if(contentMedia) return contentMedia.value;
  }
  for(const value of Object.values(data)) {
    if(typeof value === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(value)) return value;
  }
  return '';
}

function summarizeRandomPayload(data, mediaUrl = '') {
  if(typeof data === 'string') return /^https?:\/\//i.test(data) ? '' : data;
  if(!data || typeof data !== 'object') return String(data || '');
  if(data.url && (data.artist_name || data.source_url || data.artist_href || data.image_id || data.anime_name || data.source_anime)) return '';
  if(data.url && !data.quote && !data.quotes && !data.title && !data.caption && !data.description && !data.text) return '';
  const preferred = [
    ['quote', 'Quote'],
    ['quotes', 'Quote'],
    ['title', 'Judul'],
    ['signature', 'Source'],
    ['character', 'Karakter'],
    ['karakter', 'Karakter'],
    ['anime', 'Anime'],
    ['author', 'Author'],
    ['episode', 'Episode'],
    ['caption', 'Caption'],
    ['description', 'Deskripsi'],
    ['text', 'Text'],
    ['name', 'Nama']
  ];
  const lines = preferred
    .map(([key, label]) => data[key] ? `${label}: ${data[key]}` : '')
    .filter(Boolean);
  if(lines.length) return lines.join('\n');
  const entries = Object.entries(data)
    .filter(([, value]) => typeof value !== 'object' && String(value) !== mediaUrl)
    .slice(0, 6)
    .map(([key, value]) => `${key}: ${value}`);
  return entries.join('\n') || 'Random berhasil dimuat.';
}

function renderRandomMedia(url = '', mimeType = '') {
  const box = document.getElementById('randomMediaBox');
  if(!box) return;
  box.classList.remove('show');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  if(!/^(https?:|blob:)/i.test(url)) return;
  const safeUrl = escapeHtml(url);
  const cleanUrl = url.split('?')[0].toLowerCase();
  if(mimeType.startsWith('audio/') || /\.(mp3|wav|ogg|m4a)$/i.test(cleanUrl)) {
    box.innerHTML = `<audio controls src="${safeUrl}"></audio>`;
  } else if(mimeType.startsWith('video/') || /\.(mp4|webm|mov|m3u8)$/i.test(cleanUrl)) {
    box.innerHTML = `<video controls src="${safeUrl}"></video>`;
  } else {
    box.innerHTML = `<img src="${safeUrl}" alt="Random media" loading="lazy">`;
  }
  box.classList.add('show');
}

async function renderRandomFallback(slug) {
  const fallback = RANDOM_FALLBACKS[slug];
  if(!fallback) return false;
  const textEl = document.getElementById('randomText');
  if(fallback.type === 'image') {
    const separator = fallback.url.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
    const url = `${fallback.url}${fallback.url.endsWith('=') ? Date.now() : `${separator}ts=${Date.now()}`}`;
    if(textEl) textEl.textContent = 'Random berhasil dimuat dari fallback.';
    renderRandomMedia(url, 'image/jpeg');
    return true;
  }
  if(fallback.type === 'json') {
    const res = await fetch(fallback.url);
    if(!res.ok) return false;
    const payload = flattenRandomPayload(await res.json());
    const mediaUrl = findRandomMedia(payload);
    const summary = summarizeRandomPayload(payload, mediaUrl);
    if(textEl) textEl.textContent = summary || 'Random berhasil dimuat dari fallback.';
    renderRandomMedia(mediaUrl);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

async function loadRandomItem() {
  initRandom();
  const slug = document.getElementById('randomCategory')?.value;
  if(!slug) {
    showToast('Pilih random dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const endpointSlug = slug === 'cecan'
    ? `cecan/${RANDOM_CECAN_COUNTRIES[Math.floor(Math.random() * RANDOM_CECAN_COUNTRIES.length)]}`
    : slug;
  const textEl = document.getElementById('randomText');
  if(textEl) textEl.textContent = 'Mengambil random...';
  renderRandomMedia('');
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${RANDOM_API_BASE}/${endpointSlug}`);
    if(!res.ok) {
      if(await renderRandomFallback(slug)) return;
      throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    }
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if(!contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      if(contentType.includes('text/') || contentType.includes('javascript')) {
        const text = await res.text();
        try {
          const payload = flattenRandomPayload(JSON.parse(text));
          const mediaUrl = findRandomMedia(payload);
          const summary = summarizeRandomPayload(payload, mediaUrl);
          if(textEl) textEl.textContent = summary || 'Random berhasil dimuat.';
          renderRandomMedia(mediaUrl);
          return;
        } catch(e) {
          if(textEl) textEl.textContent = text.slice(0, 800) || 'Random berhasil dimuat.';
          return;
        }
      }
      const blob = await res.blob();
      const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      if(textEl) textEl.textContent = 'Random berhasil dimuat.';
      renderRandomMedia(objectUrl, blob.type || contentType);
      return;
    }
    const payload = flattenRandomPayload(await res.json());
    const mediaUrl = findRandomMedia(payload);
    const summary = summarizeRandomPayload(payload, mediaUrl);
    if(textEl) textEl.textContent = summary || 'Random berhasil dimuat.';
    renderRandomMedia(mediaUrl);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Random API error:', e);
    if(await renderRandomFallback(slug)) {
      showToast('Endpoint utama error, pakai fallback');
      return;
    }
    if(textEl) textEl.textContent = `Gagal mengambil random. ${e.message || 'Coba kategori lain.'}`;
    showToast('Gagal mengambil random', 'error');
  }
}

const SEARCH_API_BASE = atob('aHR0cHM6Ly9hcGkuc2lwdXR6eC5teS5pZC9hcGkvcy');

function initPinterestSearch() {
  const input = document.getElementById('pinterestSearchInput');

function handlePinterestSearchKey(event) {

  event.preventDefault();
  searchPinterest();
}

function handleYoutubeSearchKey(event) {

function flattenSearchPayload(json) {
  let payload = json?.data || json?.result || json?.results || json;
  for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if(payload?.data) payload = payload.data;
    if(payload?.result) payload = payload.result;
    if(payload?.results) payload = payload.results;
  }
  return Array.isArray(payload) ? payload : [payload].filter(Boolean);
}

function getSearchItemTitle(item) {
  if(typeof item === 'string') return item;
  return item?.username || item?.name || item?.full_name || item?.title || item?.caption || item?.id || 'Hasil';
}

function isHttpUrl(value) {
  return typeof value === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(value.trim());
}

function isImageLikeUrl(value) {
  return isHttpUrl(value) && /\.(jpe?g|png|webp|gif)(\?|#|$)/i.test(value);
}

function findValueDeep(source, predicate, depth = 0) {
  if(depth > 4 || source === null || source === undefined) return '';
  if(predicate(source, '')) return source;
  if(Array.isArray(source)) {
    for(const item of source) {
      const found = findValueDeep(item, predicate, depth + 1);
      if(found) return found;
    }
    return '';
  }
  if(typeof source !== 'object') return '';
  for(const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if(predicate(value, key)) return value;
    const found = findValueDeep(value, predicate, depth + 1);
    if(found) return found;
  }
  return '';
}

function getPinterestImageUrl(item) {
  return findValueDeep(item, (value, key) => {
    const lowerKey = String(key || '').toLowerCase();
    if(!isHttpUrl(value)) return false;
    return /(image|img|thumb|photo|media|src)/.test(lowerKey) || isImageLikeUrl(value);
  });
}

function getPinterestLinkUrl(item) {
  if(typeof item === 'string') return isHttpUrl(item) ? item : '';
  return item?.link || item?.url || item?.source || item?.source_url || item?.pin || item?.href || findValueDeep(item, value => isHttpUrl(value));
}

function getYoutubeThumbnailUrl(item) {
  return findValueDeep(item, (value, key) => {
    const lowerKey = String(key || '').toLowerCase();
    if(!isHttpUrl(value)) return false;
    return /(thumbnail|thumb|image|img|photo|cover|poster)/.test(lowerKey) || isImageLikeUrl(value);
  });
}

function getYoutubeLinkUrl(item) {
  if(typeof item === 'string') return isHttpUrl(item) ? item : '';
  const direct = item?.url || item?.link || item?.videoUrl || item?.video_url || item?.href || item?.watchUrl || item?.watch_url;
  if(isHttpUrl(direct)) return direct;
  const id = item?.videoId || item?.video_id || item?.id;
  if(id && /^[\w-]{8,}$/.test(String(id))) return `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${encodeURIComponent(String(id))}`;
  return findValueDeep(item, value => isHttpUrl(value));
}

function getLaheluMediaUrl(item) {
  return findValueDeep(item, (value, key) => {
    const lowerKey = String(key || '').toLowerCase();
    if(!isHttpUrl(value)) return false;
    return /(image|img|thumb|photo|media|src|post|meme|url)/.test(lowerKey) || isImageLikeUrl(value);
  });
}

function getLaheluLinkUrl(item) {
  if(typeof item === 'string') return isHttpUrl(item) ? item : '';
  return item?.link || item?.url || item?.post_url || item?.postUrl || item?.href || item?.source || findValueDeep(item, value => isHttpUrl(value));
}

function getSearchItemMeta(item) {
  if(typeof item === 'string') return '';
  const fields = ['bio','description','followers','follower','following','posts'];
  return fields
    .filter(key => item?.[key] !== undefined && item?.[key] !== null && item?.[key] !== '')
    .map(key => `${escapeHtml(key)}: ${escapeHtml(String(item[key]))}`)
    .join('<br>');
}

function renderPinterestResults(items) {
  const list = document.getElementById('pinterestSearchResults');
  if(!list) return;
  if(!items.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="combo-review-row"><div><div class="combo-review-value">Tidak ada hasil.</div></div></div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = items.slice(0, 12).map(item => {
    const imageUrl = getPinterestImageUrl(item);
    const linkUrl = getPinterestLinkUrl(item) || imageUrl;
    const meta = getSearchItemMeta(item);
    const fallback = imageUrl ? '' : escapeHtml(typeof item === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(item) : String(item));
    return `
      <div class="combo-review-row">
        <div>
          <div class="combo-review-label">${escapeHtml(String(getSearchItemTitle(item)))}</div>
          <div class="combo-review-value">${meta || fallback || 'Gambar Pinterest ditemukan.'}</div>
          ${linkUrl ? `<a class="pinterest-result-link" href="${escapeHtml(linkUrl)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fas fa-up-right-from-square"></i> Buka hasil</a>` : ''}
          ${imageUrl ? `<img class="pinterest-result-thumb" src="${escapeHtml(imageUrl)}" alt="${escapeHtml(String(getSearchItemTitle(item)))}" loading="lazy">` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

  if(!list) return;
  if(!items.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="combo-review-row"><div><div class="combo-review-value">Tidak ada hasil.</div></div></div>';

  }

    const imageUrl = getYoutubeThumbnailUrl(item);
    const linkUrl = getYoutubeLinkUrl(item) || imageUrl;
    const meta = getSearchItemMeta(item);
    const fallback = imageUrl ? '' : escapeHtml(typeof item === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(item) : String(item));
    return `
      <div class="combo-review-row">
        <div>
          <div class="combo-review-label">${escapeHtml(String(getSearchItemTitle(item)))}</div>
          <div class="combo-review-value">${meta || fallback || 'Video YouTube ditemukan.'}</div>
          ${linkUrl ? `<a class="youtube-result-link" href="${escapeHtml(linkUrl)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fas fa-up-right-from-square"></i> Buka video</a>` : ''}
          ${imageUrl ? `<img class="youtube-result-thumb" src="${escapeHtml(imageUrl)}" alt="${escapeHtml(String(getSearchItemTitle(item)))}" loading="lazy">` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderLaheluResults(items) {
  const list = document.getElementById('laheluSearchResults');
  if(!list) return;
  if(!items.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="combo-review-row"><div><div class="combo-review-value">Tidak ada hasil.</div></div></div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = items.slice(0, 12).map(item => {
    const mediaUrl = getLaheluMediaUrl(item);
    const linkUrl = getLaheluLinkUrl(item) || mediaUrl;
    const meta = getSearchItemMeta(item);
    const fallback = mediaUrl ? '' : escapeHtml(typeof item === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(item) : String(item));
    return `
      <div class="combo-review-row">
        <div>
          <div class="combo-review-label">${escapeHtml(String(getSearchItemTitle(item)))}</div>
          <div class="combo-review-value">${meta || fallback || 'Post Lahelu ditemukan.'}</div>
          ${linkUrl ? `<a class="lahelu-result-link" href="${escapeHtml(linkUrl)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fas fa-up-right-from-square"></i> Buka hasil</a>` : ''}
          ${mediaUrl ? `<img class="lahelu-result-thumb" src="${escapeHtml(mediaUrl)}" alt="${escapeHtml(String(getSearchItemTitle(item)))}" loading="lazy">` : ''}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

async function fetchSearchEndpoint(endpoint, term, signal, label = 'Search') {
  const params = ['query', 'q', 'keyword', 'search'];
  let lastError = null;
  for(const param of params) {
    const url = `${SEARCH_API_BASE}/${endpoint}?${param}=${encodeURIComponent(term)}`;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(url, { signal });
      const text = await res.text();
      if(!res.ok) {
        lastError = new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}${text ? ` - ${text.slice(0, 120)}` : ''}`);
        if(res.status === 400) continue;
        throw lastError;
      }
      return JSON.parse(text);
    } catch(e) {
      if(e.name === 'AbortError') throw e;
      lastError = e;
    }
  }
  throw lastError || new Error(`Endpoint ${label} tidak merespons.`);
}

async function fetchPinterestSearch(term, signal) {
  return fetchSearchEndpoint('pinterest', term, signal, 'Pinterest');
}

async function searchPinterest() {
  const input = document.getElementById('pinterestSearchInput');
  const status = document.getElementById('pinterestSearchStatus');
  const list = document.getElementById('pinterestSearchResults');
  const term = input?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!term) {
    showToast('Isi keyword dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(status) status.textContent = 'Mencari...';
  if(list) list.innerHTML = '';
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 12000);
  try {
    const json = await fetchPinterestSearch(term, controller.signal);
    const items = flattenSearchPayload(json);
    if(status) status.textContent = `${items.length} hasil ditemukan.`;
    renderPinterestResults(items);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Pinterest search error:', e);
    if(status) status.textContent = e.name === 'AbortError'
      ? 'Search terlalu lama. Coba keyword lain.'
      : `Gagal mencari. ${e.message || 'Coba lagi.'}`;
    showToast('Search gagal', 'error');
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

async function searchYoutube() {
  const input = document.getElementById('youtubeSearchInput');
  const status = document.getElementById('youtubeSearchStatus');
  const list = document.getElementById('youtubeSearchResults');
  const term = input?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!term) {
    showToast('Isi keyword dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }

  if(list) list.innerHTML = '';

  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 12000);
  try {
    const json = await fetchSearchEndpoint('youtube', term, controller.signal, 'YouTube');
    const items = flattenSearchPayload(json);
    if(status) status.textContent = `${items.length} hasil ditemukan.`;
    renderYoutubeResults(items);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('YouTube search error:', e);
    if(status) status.textContent = e.name === 'AbortError'
      ? 'Search terlalu lama. Coba keyword lain.'
      : `Gagal mencari. ${e.message || 'Coba lagi.'}`;
    showToast('Search gagal', 'error');
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}
