const DOWNLOADER_API_BASE = atob('aHR0cHM6Ly9hcGkuc2lwdXR6eC5teS5pZC9hcGkvZA==');
const DOWNLOADER_CATALOG = [
  { slug: 'capcut', label: 'CapCut' },
  { slug: 'gdrive', label: 'Google Drive' },
  { slug: 'spotify', label: 'Spotify' },
  { slug: 'savefrom', label: 'SaveFrom' },
  { slug: 'github', label: 'GitHub' },
  { slug: 'douyin', label: 'Douyin' },
  { slug: 'lahelu', label: 'Lahelu' },
  { slug: 'soundcloud', label: 'SoundCloud' },
  { slug: 'snackvideo', label: 'Snack Video' },
  { slug: 'spotifyv2', label: 'Spotify V2' },
  { slug: 'tiktok', label: 'TikTok' },
  { slug: 'fastdl', label: 'Instagram FastDL' },
  { slug: 'igram', label: 'iGram' },
  { slug: 'tiktok/v2', label: 'TikTok V2' },
  { slug: 'twitter', label: 'Twitter' },
  { slug: 'ssstwiter', label: 'SSSTwitter' },
  { slug: 'facebook', label: 'Facebook' },
  { slug: 'ytpost', label: 'YouTube Community' },
  { slug: 'sssinstagram', label: 'SSSInstagram' },
  { slug: 'ummy', label: 'Ummy' },
  { slug: 'rednote', label: 'RedNote' }
];
let selectedDownloaderSlug = '';

function initDownloader() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('downloaderGrid');
  if(grid && !grid.dataset.ready) {
    grid.innerHTML = DOWNLOADER_CATALOG.map(item => `
      <button class="downloader-tile" type="button" data-downloader="${escapeHtml(item.slug)}" onclick="selectDownloader('${escapeHtml(item.slug)}')">
        <i class="fas fa-download"></i>
        <span>${escapeHtml(item.label)}</span>
      </button>
    `).join('');
    grid.dataset.ready = 'true';
  }
  if(!selectedDownloaderSlug && DOWNLOADER_CATALOG[0]) {
    selectedDownloaderSlug = DOWNLOADER_CATALOG[0].slug;
  }
  renderDownloaderSelection();
  const status = document.getElementById('downloaderStatus');
  const activeItem = DOWNLOADER_CATALOG.find(item => item.slug === selectedDownloaderSlug);
  if(status && activeItem && status.textContent === 'Pilih downloader lalu tempel link.') {
    status.textContent = `${activeItem.label} dipilih. Tempel link lalu ambil.`;
  }
  document.getElementById('downloaderUrlInput')?.focus();
}

function selectDownloader(slug) {
  selectedDownloaderSlug = slug;
  renderDownloaderSelection();
  const item = DOWNLOADER_CATALOG.find(entry => entry.slug === slug);
  const status = document.getElementById('downloaderStatus');
  if(status) status.textContent = `${item?.label || 'Downloader'} dipilih. Tempel link lalu ambil.`;
  document.getElementById('downloaderUrlInput')?.focus();
}

function renderDownloaderSelection() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.downloader-tile').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.downloader === selectedDownloaderSlug);
  });
}

function handleDownloaderKey(event) {
  if(event.key !== 'Enter') return;
  event.preventDefault();
  runDownloader();
}

function collectUrlsDeep(source, urls = new Set(), depth = 0) {
  if(depth > 5 || source === null || source === undefined) return urls;
  if(typeof source === 'string') {
    const matches = source.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/gi) || [];
    matches.forEach(url => urls.add(url.replace(/[),.]+$/g, '')));
    return urls;
  }
  if(Array.isArray(source)) {
    source.forEach(item => collectUrlsDeep(item, urls, depth + 1));
    return urls;
  }
  if(typeof source === 'object') {
    Object.values(source).forEach(value => collectUrlsDeep(value, urls, depth + 1));
  }
  return urls;
}

function isVideoLikeUrl(url) {
  return /\.(mp4|webm|mov|m3u8)(\?|#|$)/i.test(url);
}

function isAudioLikeUrl(url) {
  return /\.(mp3|wav|ogg|m4a|flac)(\?|#|$)/i.test(url);
}

function summarizeDownloaderPayload(data, urls = []) {
  if(typeof data === 'string') return urls.length ? '' : data.slice(0, 500);
  if(!data || typeof data !== 'object') return String(data || '');
  const preferred = ['title','name','author','username','artist','album','duration','quality','type','mime','caption','description'];
  const lines = preferred
    .filter(key => data[key] !== undefined && data[key] !== null && data[key] !== '')
    .map(key => `${escapeHtml(key)}: ${escapeHtml(String(data[key]))}`);
  if(lines.length) return lines.join('<br>');
  const urlSet = new Set(urls);
  return Object.entries(data)
    .filter(([, value]) => typeof value !== 'object' && !urlSet.has(String(value)))
    .slice(0, 6)
    .map(([key, value]) => `${escapeHtml(key)}: ${escapeHtml(String(value))}`)
    .join('<br>');
}

function renderDownloaderResults(payload) {
  const list = document.getElementById('downloaderResults');
  if(!list) return;
  const urls = [...collectUrlsDeep(payload)];
  const summary = summarizeDownloaderPayload(payload, urls);
  if(!urls.length && !summary) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="combo-review-row"><div><div class="combo-review-value">Tidak ada link download ditemukan.</div></div></div>';
    return;
  }
  const media = urls.find(url => isVideoLikeUrl(url) || isAudioLikeUrl(url) || isImageLikeUrl(url));
  const linkRows = urls.slice(0, 12).map((url, index) => `
    <a class="downloader-result-link" href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
      <i class="fas fa-download"></i> Download ${index + 1}
    </a>
  `).join('');
  const preview = media
    ? (isVideoLikeUrl(media)
      ? `<video class="downloader-result-video" src="${escapeHtml(media)}" controls></video>`
      : isAudioLikeUrl(media)
        ? `<audio controls src="${escapeHtml(media)}" style="width:100%;margin-top:8px"></audio>`
        : `<img class="downloader-result-thumb" src="${escapeHtml(media)}" alt="" loading="lazy">`)
    : '';
  list.innerHTML = `
    <div class="combo-review-row downloader-result-card">
      <div class="downloader-result-title">
        <div class="combo-review-label">Hasil Downloader</div>
        <div class="combo-review-value">${summary || `${urls.length} link ditemukan.`}</div>
      </div>
      ${linkRows ? `<div class="downloader-result-actions">${linkRows}</div>` : ''}
      ${preview ? `<div class="downloader-result-preview">${preview}</div>` : ''}
    </div>
  `;
}

async function fetchDownloaderEndpoint(slug, targetUrl, signal) {
  const params = ['url', 'link', 'query', 'q'];
  let lastError = null;
  for(const param of params) {
    const url = `${DOWNLOADER_API_BASE}/${slug}?${param}=${encodeURIComponent(targetUrl)}`;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(url, { signal });
      const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      if(!res.ok) {
        const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
        lastError = new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}${text ? ` - ${text.slice(0, 120)}` : ''}`);
        if(res.status === 400) continue;
        throw lastError;
      }
      if(contentType.includes('application/json')) return await res.json();
      const text = await res.text();
      try { return JSON.parse(text); } catch(e) { return text; }
    } catch(e) {
      if(e.name === 'AbortError') throw e;
      lastError = e;
    }
  }
  throw lastError || new Error('Endpoint downloader tidak merespons.');
}

async function runDownloader() {
  initDownloader();
  const slug = selectedDownloaderSlug || '';
  const targetUrl = document.getElementById('downloaderUrlInput')?.value.trim() || '';
  const status = document.getElementById('downloaderStatus');
  const list = document.getElementById('downloaderResults');
  if(!slug) {
    showToast('Pilih downloader dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(targetUrl)) {
    showToast('Tempel link yang valid dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(status) status.textContent = 'Mengambil link download...';
  if(list) list.innerHTML = '';
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 20000);
  try {
    const payload = await fetchDownloaderEndpoint(slug, targetUrl, controller.signal);
    renderDownloaderResults(payload?.data || payload?.result || payload);
    if(status) status.textContent = 'Downloader selesai.';
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Downloader error:', e);
    if(status) status.textContent = e.name === 'AbortError'
      ? 'Downloader terlalu lama. Coba endpoint lain.'
      : `Gagal mengambil download. ${e.message || 'Coba lagi.'}`;
    showToast('Downloader gagal', 'error');
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

const BRAT_API_URL = 'https://api.siputzx.my.id/api/m/brat';
let selectedBratMode = 'image';
let currentBratResult = null;

function initBrat() {
  renderBratMode();
  document.getElementById('bratTextInput')?.focus();
}

function selectBratMode(mode) {
  selectedBratMode = mode === 'video' ? 'video' : 'image';
  renderBratMode();
}

function renderBratMode() {
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-brat-mode]').forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.bratMode === selectedBratMode);
  });
}

function getBratExtension(mime = '', mode = selectedBratMode) {
  if(mime.includes('webm')) return 'webm';
  if(mime.includes('mp4') || mode === 'video') return 'mp4';
  if(mime.includes('webp')) return 'webp';
  if(mime.includes('jpeg')) return 'jpg';
  return 'png';
}

function revokeBratObjectUrl() {
  if(currentBratResult?.url) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentBratResult.url);
  currentBratResult = null;
}

function dataUrlToBlob(dataUrl) {
  const [meta, body] = String(dataUrl || '').split(',');
  const mime = meta.match(/data:(.*?);base64/i)?.[1] || 'application/octet-stream';
  const binary = atob(body || '');
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for(let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  return new Blob([bytes], { type: mime });
}

async function fetchBlobFromBratPayload(payload, signal) {
  const raw = payload?.data || payload?.result || payload;
  const media = typeof raw === 'string'
    ? raw
    : getNestedValue(raw || {}, ['url','image','img','video','file','base64','data.url','data.image','data.video']);
  if(!media) throw new Error('API Brat tidak mengirim media');
  if(String(media).startsWith('data:')) return dataUrlToBlob(media);
  if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(media))) {
    const res = await fetch(media, { signal });
    if(!res.ok) throw new Error(`Media HTTP ${res.status}`);
    return await res.blob();
  }
  return new Blob([dataUrlToBlob(`data:image/png;base64,${media}`)], { type: 'image/png' });
}

async function fetchBratBlob(text, mode, signal) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    text,
    isVideo: String(mode === 'video'),
    delay: '500'
  });
  const res = await fetch(`${BRAT_API_URL}?${params.toString()}`, { signal, cache: 'no-store' });
  if(!res.ok) {
    const errText = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}${errText ? ` - ${errText.slice(0, 90)}` : ''}`);
  }
  const type = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  if(type.includes('application/json')) {
    const payload = await res.json();
    return await fetchBlobFromBratPayload(payload, signal);
  }
  return await res.blob();
}

function renderBratResult() {
  const list = document.getElementById('bratResults');
  if(!list) return;
  if(!currentBratResult) {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }
  const isVideo = currentBratResult.type === 'video' || currentBratResult.mime.startsWith('video/');
  const preview = isVideo
    ? `<video class="downloader-result-video" src="${escapeHtml(currentBratResult.url)}" controls></video>`
    : `<img class="downloader-result-thumb" src="${escapeHtml(currentBratResult.url)}" alt="Brat" loading="lazy">`;
  list.innerHTML = `
    <div class="combo-review-row downloader-result-card">
      <div class="downloader-result-title">
        <div class="combo-review-label">Hasil Brat</div>
        <div class="combo-review-value">${escapeHtml(currentBratResult.fileName)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="downloader-result-actions">
        <button class="downloader-result-link" type="button" onclick="downloadBratResult()"><i class="fas fa-download"></i> Download ${isVideo ? 'Video' : 'Gambar'}</button>
        <button class="downloader-result-link" type="button" onclick="shareBratToWhatsApp()"><i class="fab fa-whatsapp"></i> Jadi Sticker WA</button>
      </div>
      <div class="downloader-result-preview">${preview}</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

async function generateBrat() {
  initBrat();
  const text = document.getElementById('bratTextInput')?.value.trim() || '';
  const status = document.getElementById('bratStatus');
  if(!text) {
    showToast('Tulis teks brat dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(status) status.textContent = 'Membuat brat...';
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), selectedBratMode === 'video' ? 30000 : 18000);
  try {
    const blob = await fetchBratBlob(text, selectedBratMode, controller.signal);
    revokeBratObjectUrl();
    const mime = blob.type || (selectedBratMode === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/png');
    const ext = getBratExtension(mime, selectedBratMode);
    const fileName = `neverera-brat-${Date.now()}.${ext}`;
    currentBratResult = {
      blob,
      mime,
      type: selectedBratMode,
      text,
      fileName,
      url: URL.createObjectURL(blob)
    };
    renderBratResult();
    if(status) status.textContent = 'Brat berhasil dibuat.';
    showToast('Brat berhasil dibuat');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Brat error:', e);
    if(status) status.textContent = e.name === 'AbortError'
      ? 'Brat terlalu lama dibuat. Coba teks lebih pendek.'
      : `Gagal membuat brat. ${e.message || 'Coba lagi.'}`;
    showToast('Brat gagal', 'error');
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function clearBratResult() {
  revokeBratObjectUrl();
  const input = document.getElementById('bratTextInput');
  const status = document.getElementById('bratStatus');
  const list = document.getElementById('bratResults');
  if(input) input.value = '';
  if(status) status.textContent = 'Tulis teks lalu buat brat.';
  if(list) list.innerHTML = '';
  selectedBratMode = 'image';
  renderBratMode();
}

function downloadBratResult() {
  if(!currentBratResult) {
    showToast('Buat brat dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = currentBratResult.url;
  a.download = currentBratResult.fileName;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
}

async function makeBratShareFile() {
  if(!currentBratResult) return null;
  if(currentBratResult.type === 'video') {
    return new File([currentBratResult.blob], currentBratResult.fileName, { type: currentBratResult.mime || 'video/mp4' });
  }
  try {
    const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(currentBratResult.blob);
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = bitmap.width;
    canvas.height = bitmap.height;
    canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
    const webpBlob = await new Promise(resolve => canvas.toBlob(resolve, 'image/webp', 0.92));
    bitmap.close?.();
    if(webpBlob) return new File([webpBlob], 'neverera-brat-sticker.webp', { type: 'image/webp' });
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Brat webp convert fallback:', e);
  }
  return new File([currentBratResult.blob], currentBratResult.fileName, { type: currentBratResult.mime || 'image/png' });
}

async function shareBratToWhatsApp() {
  if(!currentBratResult) {
    showToast('Buat brat dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  try {
    const file = await makeBratShareFile();
    if(file && navigator.canShare?.({ files: [file] })) {
      await navigator.share({
        files: [file],
        title: 'NeverEra Brat',
        text: currentBratResult.type === 'video' ? 'Video brat NeverEra' : 'Sticker brat NeverEra'
      });
      showToast('Pilih WhatsApp untuk kirim sticker');
      return;
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Brat share error:', e);
  }
  downloadBratResult();
  window.open(`https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent('Brat sudah didownload. Kirim file ini ke WhatsApp untuk dijadikan sticker.')}`, '_blank');
  showToast('Browser ini belum bisa share file langsung, file didownload dulu');
}

async function saveSettings() {
  const displayName = document.getElementById('settingsDisplayName').value.trim();
  const uniqueCode = document.getElementById('settingsUniqueCode')?.value.trim().toUpperCase() || '';
  const accentInput = document.getElementById('appearanceAccentText')?.value.trim() || state.accentColor;
  if(uniqueCode && uniqueCode.length < 4) {
    showToast('Kode unik minimal 4 karakter', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(accentInput && !/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(accentInput)) {
    showToast('Warna theme harus format hex, contoh #27a44f', 'error');
    return;
  }
  markMissingSettings();
  if(uniqueCode) state.uniqueCode = uniqueCode;
  state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(state.uiMode);
  state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(accentInput);
  applyAppearancePreferences();

  showLoading('Menyimpan pengaturan...');
  try {
    if(sbClient && state.phone) {
      const payload = {
        phone: state.phone,
        unique_code: state.uniqueCode,
        theme: state.theme,
        ui_mode: state.uiMode,
        accent_color: state.accentColor,
        gender: state.gender,
        interests: normalizeInterests(state.interests),
        display_name: displayName || null,
        winner_display_mode: state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name',
        timezone: state.timezone,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      let { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users')
        .update(payload)
        .eq('phone', state.phone)
        .select('phone')
        .maybeSingle();
      while(error && isMissingColumnError(error) && (payload.ui_mode || payload.accent_color)) {
        if(payload.ui_mode && isMissingSpecificColumnError(error, 'ui_mode')) delete payload.ui_mode;
        else if(payload.accent_color && isMissingSpecificColumnError(error, 'accent_color')) delete payload.accent_color;
        else if(payload.ui_mode) delete payload.ui_mode;
        else delete payload.accent_color;
        const retry = await sbClient.from('users')
          .update(payload)
          .eq('phone', state.phone)
          .select('phone')
          .maybeSingle();
        data = retry.data;
        error = retry.error;
      }
      if(error) throw error;
      if(!data) {
        returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
        return;
      }
    }

    const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
    session.theme = state.theme;
    session.uiMode = state.uiMode;
    session.accentColor = state.accentColor;
    session.gender = state.gender;
    session.interests = normalizeInterests(state.interests);
    session.uniqueCode = state.uniqueCode;
    session.displayName = displayName;
    session.winnerDisplayMode = state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
    session.timezone = state.timezone;
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
    updateGreeting();
    state.savedSettings = {
      theme: state.theme,
      uiMode: state.uiMode,
      accentColor: state.accentColor,
      gender: state.gender,
      interests: normalizeInterests(state.interests),
      uniqueCode: state.uniqueCode,
      displayName,
      winnerDisplayMode: state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name',
      timezone: state.timezone
    };
    markMissingSettings();

    showToast('Pengaturan tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal simpan pengaturan:', e);
    showToast('Gagal menyimpan pengaturan', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

let profileCropState = {
  image: null,
  zoom: 1,
  x: 0,
  y: 0
};

function getProfileInitial() {
  const name = state.displayName || JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}').displayName || state.phone || 'N';
  return String(name).trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'N';
}

function normalizeProfilePictureUrl(url = '') {
  const value = String(url || '').trim();
  if(value.startsWith('data:image/')) return value.replace(/\s+/g, '');
  return value.replace(/^https?:\/\/i\.ibb\.co\//i, match =>
    match.replace('i.ibb.co', 'i.ibb.co.com')
  );
}

function isProfilePictureSource(value = '') {
  const src = normalizeProfilePictureUrl(value);
  return /^https?:\/\/.+\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif)(\?.*)?$/i.test(src)
    || /^data:image\/(png|jpe?g|webp|gif);base64,[a-z0-9+/=]+$/i.test(src);
}

async function loadAvatarProfilesForPhones(phones = []) {
  if(!sbClient) return {};
  const uniquePhones = [...new Set((phones || []).map(phone => String(phone || '').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  if(!uniquePhones.length) return {};
  try {
    let { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('users')
      .select('phone, avatar_border, profile_picture_url')
      .in('phone', uniquePhones);
    if(error && isMissingColumnError(error)) {
      const retry = await sbClient
        .from('users')
        .select('phone, avatar_border')
        .in('phone', uniquePhones);
      data = retry.data;
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(error) throw error;
    return Object.fromEntries((data || []).map(user => [
      String(user.phone || ''),
      {
        avatarBorder: normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(user.avatar_border || 'white'),
        profilePictureUrl: normalizeProfilePictureUrl(user.profile_picture_url || '')
      }
    ]));
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Avatar profile load error:', e);
    return {};
  }
}

function getCurrentProfilePictureUrl() {
  return normalizeProfilePictureUrl(state.profilePictureUrl || '');
}

function renderProfileAvatar() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('profileAvatarButton');
  if(!btn) return;
  const url = getCurrentProfilePictureUrl();
  const border = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(state.avatarBorder || JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}').avatarBorder || 'white');
  const purpleClass = border === 'purple' ? ' needhelp-avatar--purple' : '';
  const borderEffects = border === 'purple'
    ? '<span class="needhelp-avatar-orbit"></span><span class="needhelp-avatar-sparkles"></span><span class="needhelp-avatar-triangles"></span>'
    : '';
  if(border === 'purple') {
    const loopDelay = duration => `-${((Date.now() % duration) / 1000).toFixed(2)}s`;
    btn.style.setProperty('--avatar-particle-delay', loopDelay(14000));
    btn.style.setProperty('--avatar-triangle-delay', loopDelay(3000));
    btn.style.setProperty('--avatar-orbit-delay', loopDelay(5600));
    btn.style.setProperty('--avatar-orbit-delay-2', loopDelay(6400));
  } else {
    ['--avatar-particle-delay','--avatar-triangle-delay','--avatar-orbit-delay','--avatar-orbit-delay-2'].forEach(prop => btn.style.removeProperty(prop));
  }
  btn.className = `profile-avatar-button ${border}${purpleClass}`;
  btn.innerHTML = `${borderEffects}<span class="profile-avatar-face">${
    url
    ? `<img class="profile-avatar-img" src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt="Foto profil" onerror="handleProfileAvatarImageError(this)">`
    : `<span class="profile-avatar-fallback">${escapeHtml(getProfileInitial())}</span>`
  }</span>`;
}

function handleProfileAvatarImageError(img) {
  console.warn('Foto profil gagal dimuat:', getCurrentProfilePictureUrl());
  const face = img?.closest?.('.profile-avatar-face');
  if(face) {
    face.innerHTML = `<span class="profile-avatar-fallback">${escapeHtml(getProfileInitial())}</span>`;
  }
}

function openProfilePicturePicker() {
  openProfileEditModal();
}

function renderProfileEditCurrentAvatar() {
  const el = document.getElementById('profileEditCurrentAvatar');
  if(!el) return;
  const url = getCurrentProfilePictureUrl();
  el.innerHTML = url
    ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt="Foto profil" onerror="handleProfileEditAvatarImageError()">`
    : escapeHtml(getProfileInitial());
}

function handleProfileEditAvatarImageError() {
  const el = document.getElementById('profileEditCurrentAvatar');
  if(el) el.textContent = getProfileInitial();
}

function openProfileEditModal() {
  renderProfileEditCurrentAvatar();
  const input = document.getElementById('profilePictureUrlInput');
  if(input) input.value = getCurrentProfilePictureUrl();
  document.getElementById('profileEditModal')?.classList.add('open');
}

function closeProfileEditModal() {
  document.getElementById('profileEditModal')?.classList.remove('open');
}

function resetProfileCropControls() {
  profileCropState.zoom = 1;
  profileCropState.x = 0;
  profileCropState.y = 0;
  const zoom = document.getElementById('profileCropZoom');
  const x = document.getElementById('profileCropX');
  const y = document.getElementById('profileCropY');
  if(zoom) zoom.value = '1';
  if(x) x.value = '0';
  if(y) y.value = '0';
}

function updateProfileCropFromControls() {
  profileCropState.zoom = Number(document.getElementById('profileCropZoom')?.value || 1);
  profileCropState.x = Number(document.getElementById('profileCropX')?.value || 0);
  profileCropState.y = Number(document.getElementById('profileCropY')?.value || 0);
  drawProfileCrop();
}

function drawProfileCrop() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('profileCropCanvas');
  const img = profileCropState.image;
  if(!canvas || !img) return;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const size = canvas.width;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d1f14';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
  const baseScale = Math.max(size / img.naturalWidth, size / img.naturalHeight);
  const scale = baseScale * profileCropState.zoom;
  const drawW = img.naturalWidth * scale;
  const drawH = img.naturalHeight * scale;
  const maxOffsetX = Math.max(0, (drawW - size) / 2);
  const maxOffsetY = Math.max(0, (drawH - size) / 2);
  const offsetX = (profileCropState.x / 100) * maxOffsetX;
  const offsetY = (profileCropState.y / 100) * maxOffsetY;
  const dx = (size - drawW) / 2 + offsetX;
  const dy = (size - drawH) / 2 + offsetY;
  ctx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, drawW, drawH);
}

async function uploadProfilePicture(input) {
  const file = input?.files?.[0];
  if(!file) return;
  if(!file.type?.startsWith('image/')) {
    showToast('Pilih file gambar', 'error');
    input.value = '';
    return;
  }
  if(file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
    showToast('Ukuran gambar maksimal 5MB', 'error');
    input.value = '';
    return;
  }
  try {
    const dataUrl = await fileToDataUrl(file);
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => {
      profileCropState.image = img;
      resetProfileCropControls();
      document.getElementById('profileCropCanvas').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('profileCropEmpty').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('profileCropControls').style.display = 'grid';
      document.getElementById('profileCropSaveBtn').disabled = false;
      drawProfileCrop();
      openProfileEditModal();
    };
    img.onerror = () => showToast('Gagal membaca foto', 'error');
    img.src = dataUrl;
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Profile crop load error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal membaca foto', 'error');
  } finally {
    input.value = '';
  }
}

async function saveCroppedProfilePicture() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('profileCropCanvas');
  if(!canvas || !profileCropState.image) {
    showToast('Pilih foto dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone) {
    showToast('Login dulu untuk ganti foto profil', 'error');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Upload foto profil...');
  try {
    const blob = await new Promise(resolve => canvas.toBlob(resolve, 'image/jpeg', 0.92));
    if(!blob) throw new Error('Gagal membuat crop foto');
    const imageUrls = await uploadImageToImgBBDetails(blob);
    const imageUrl = normalizeProfilePictureUrl(imageUrls.directUrl);
    if(!imageUrl) throw new Error('URL gambar kosong');
    let { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .update({
        profile_picture_url: imageUrl,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('phone', state.phone)
      .select('phone, profile_picture_url')
      .maybeSingle();
    if(error && isMissingColumnError(error)) {
      const retry = await sbClient.from('users')
        .update({ profile_picture_url: imageUrl, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
        .eq('phone', state.phone)
        .select('phone, profile_picture_url')
        .maybeSingle();
      data = retry.data;
      error = retry.error;
    }
    if(error) throw error;
    if(!data) {
      returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
      return;
    }
    state.profilePictureUrl = imageUrl;
    const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
    delete session.profilePictureUrl;
    session.timestamp = Date.now();
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
    renderProfileAvatar();
    renderProfileEditCurrentAvatar();
    closeProfileEditModal();
    showToast('Foto profil tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Upload profile picture error:', e);
    if(isMissingColumnError(e)) {
      showToast('Kolom profile_picture_url belum ada. Jalankan SQL terbaru dulu.', 'error');
    } else {
      showToast('Gagal upload foto profil', 'error');
    }
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function saveProfilePictureUrl() {
  const input = document.getElementById('profilePictureUrlInput');
  const imageUrl = normalizeProfilePictureUrl(input?.value || '');
  if(!imageUrl) {
    showToast('Masukkan URL foto dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!isProfilePictureSource(imageUrl)) {
    showToast('Masukkan link gambar langsung atau base64 data:image', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone) {
    showToast('Login dulu untuk ganti foto profil', 'error');
    return;
  }

  showLoading('Simpan URL foto...');
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users')
      .update({
        profile_picture_url: imageUrl,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('phone', state.phone)
      .select('phone, profile_picture_url')
      .maybeSingle();
    if(error) throw error;
    if(!data) {
      returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
      return;
    }

    state.profilePictureUrl = imageUrl;
    const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
    delete session.profilePictureUrl;
    session.timestamp = Date.now();
    localStorage.setItem('ne_session', JSON.stringify(session));
    renderProfileAvatar();
    renderProfileEditCurrentAvatar();
    showToast('URL foto profil tersimpan');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Simpan URL foto profil error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal menyimpan URL foto', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

// ========== DASHBOARD ==========
async function initDashboard(options = {}) {
  applyFontAwesomeIcons();
  updateClientIpUI();
  updateGreeting();
  startClock();
  initCarousel();
  await loadRatings();
  startRatingCycle();
  loadCarouselImages();
  const profile = await loadUserProfile();
  if(!profile && sbClient && state.phone && state.profileLoadStatus === 'missing') {
    returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
    return;
  }
  if(!profile && sbClient && state.phone) {
    returnToHomeBecauseProfileBlocked();
    return;
  }
  await initMaterials();
  
  const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  const fmt = formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone || session.phone || '');
  
  document.getElementById('dashPhone').textContent = fmt;
  document.getElementById('sbUserPhone').textContent = fmt;
  document.getElementById('sbUserName').textContent = session.displayName || 'Member';
  if(session.theme) state.theme = session.theme;
  if(session.uiMode) state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(session.uiMode);
  if(session.accentColor) state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(session.accentColor);
  applyAppearancePreferences();
  if(session.gender) state.gender = session.gender;
  if(session.interests) state.interests = session.interests;
  if(session.uniqueCode) state.uniqueCode = session.uniqueCode;
  if(session.avatarBorder) state.avatarBorder = session.avatarBorder;
  renderProfileAvatar();
  renderDashboardAccountTuning();
  renderNewMenuItems();
  
  document.getElementById('codeValue').textContent = '••••••';
  state.codeVisible = false;
  
  if(Date.now() - state.hasRatedLoadedAt > 30000) {
    state.hasRated = await checkUserHasRated(state.phone);
    state.hasRatedLoadedAt = Date.now();
  }
  const ratingBtn = document.getElementById('addRatingBtn');
  if(ratingBtn) {
    ratingBtn.disabled = state.hasRated;
    ratingBtn.innerHTML = state.hasRated ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Anda sudah memberi ulasan' : '+ Tambah Ulasan Kamu';
  }
  
  if(options.resetPage !== false) {
    setActiveNavigation('beranda');
    state.currentPage = 'beranda';
    saveMemberRoute('beranda', true);
  }
}

function updateGreeting() {
  const h = getNeverEraDate().getUTCHours();
  let g = h<11?'Selamat pagi,':(h<15?'Selamat siang,':(h<18?'Selamat sore,':'Selamat malam,'));
  const session = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ne_session') || '{}');
  const displayMode = session.winnerDisplayMode || state.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
  const name = displayMode === 'phone'
    ? formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone || session.phone || '')
    : (session.displayName || state.displayName || '');
  document.getElementById('dashGreeting').textContent = name ? `${g} ${name}` : g;
}

// ---- CLOCK ----
const NEVERERA_TIMEZONE = 'WIB';
const NEVERERA_OFFSET = 7;
const DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_OFFSETS = { WIB: 7, WITA: 8, WIT: 9 };
const DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_CYCLE = ['WIB', 'WITA', 'WIT'];

function normalizeDisplayTimezone(tz = NEVERERA_TIMEZONE) {
  return DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_CYCLE.includes(tz) ? tz : NEVERERA_TIMEZONE;
}

function getOffsetDate(value = Date.now(), offset = NEVERERA_OFFSET) {
  const source = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return new Date(source.getTime() + offset * 3600000);
}

function getNeverEraDate(value = Date.now()) {
  return getOffsetDate(value, NEVERERA_OFFSET);
}

function getDisplayDate(value = Date.now(), zone = state.timezone) {
  const normalized = normalizeDisplayTimezone(zone);
  return getOffsetDate(value, DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_OFFSETS[normalized]);
}

function formatOffsetDateTime(value, { withSeconds = false, zone = state.timezone } = {}) {
  if(!value) return '';
  try {
    const normalized = normalizeDisplayTimezone(zone);
    const local = getDisplayDate(value, normalized);
    const datePart = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
      timeZone: 'UTC',
      day: '2-digit',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric'
    }).format(local);
    const hh = String(local.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const mm = String(local.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const ss = String(local.getUTCSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${datePart}, ${hh}.${mm}${withSeconds ? `.${ss}` : ''} ${normalized}`;
  } catch(e) {
    return value;
  }
}

function startClock() {
  clearInterval(state.clockInterval);
  state.clockInterval = setInterval(updateClock, 1000);
  updateClock();
}

function updateClock() {
  state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(state.timezone);
  const local = getDisplayDate(Date.now(), state.timezone);
  const hh = String(local.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0');
  const mm = String(local.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
  const ss = String(local.getUTCSeconds()).padStart(2,'0');
  const display = document.getElementById('clockDisplay');
  const zone = document.getElementById('clockZone');
  if(display) display.textContent = `${hh}:${mm}:${ss}`;
  if(zone) zone.textContent = state.timezone;
}

function cycleTimezone() {
  state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(state.timezone);
  const idx = DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_CYCLE.indexOf(state.timezone);
  state.timezone = DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_CYCLE[(idx + 1) % DISPLAY_TIMEZONE_CYCLE.length];
  updateClock();
  showToast(`Zona waktu: ${state.timezone}`);
}

// ---- SIDEBAR ----
function toggleSidebar() {
  const sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  const ov = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
  const btn = document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn');
  sb.classList.toggle('open');
  ov.classList.toggle('open');
  btn.classList.toggle('open');
  
  if (sb.classList.contains('open')) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(item => {
      item.classList.remove('active');
    });
    const currentItem = document.querySelector(`.sidebar-item[data-page="${state.currentPage}"]`);
    if (currentItem) {
      currentItem.classList.add('active');
    }
  }
}
function closeSidebar() {
  document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay').classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('hamburgerBtn').classList.remove('open');
}

// ---- CAROUSEL ----
function initCarousel() {
  state.currentSlide = 0;
  initCarouselDrag();
  restartCarouselAuto();
}

function restartCarouselAuto() {
  clearInterval(state.carouselInterval);
  state.carouselInterval = setInterval(() => slideDir(1), 4000);
}

function getCarouselParts() {
  return {
    wrap: document.querySelector('.carousel-wrap'),
    track: document.getElementById('carouselTrack'),
    slides: document.querySelectorAll('.carousel-slide')
  };
}

function setCarouselTransform(percent, animated = true) {
  const track = document.getElementById('carouselTrack');
  if(!track) return;
  track.style.transition = animated ? 'transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)' : 'none';
  track.style.transform = `translateX(${percent}%)`;
}

function initCarouselDrag() {
  const { wrap, track } = getCarouselParts();
  if(!wrap || !track || wrap.dataset.dragReady === '1') return;
  wrap.dataset.dragReady = '1';

  const endDrag = (clientX) => {
    if(!state.carouselDrag?.active) return;
    const drag = state.carouselDrag;
    const delta = clientX - drag.startX;
    const threshold = Math.max(44, drag.width * 0.14);
    wrap.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    state.carouselDrag.active = false;

    if(Math.abs(delta) > threshold) {
      goSlide(drag.index + (delta < 0 ? 1 : -1));
    } else {
      goSlide(drag.index);
    }
    restartCarouselAuto();
  };

  wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
    if(event.button !== undefined && event.button !== 0) return;
    if(event.target?.closest?.('.carousel-btn, .carousel-dot')) return;
    const { slides } = getCarouselParts();
    if(slides.length <= 1) return;
    clearInterval(state.carouselInterval);
    state.carouselDrag = {
      active: true,
      startX: event.clientX,
      width: Math.max(1, wrap.clientWidth),
      index: state.currentSlide
    };
    wrap.classList.add('is-dragging');
    wrap.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
    setCarouselTransform(-state.currentSlide * 100, false);
  });

  wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', (event) => {
    const drag = state.carouselDrag;
    if(!drag?.active) return;
    const delta = event.clientX - drag.startX;
    const percent = -drag.index * 100 + (delta / drag.width) * 100;
    setCarouselTransform(percent, false);
  });

  wrap.addEventListener('pointerup', (event) => endDrag(event.clientX));
  wrap.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
    if(!state.carouselDrag?.active) return;
    wrap.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    state.carouselDrag.active = false;
    goSlide(state.carouselDrag.index);
    restartCarouselAuto();
  });
}

async function loadCarouselImages() {
  if(!sbClient) return;
  try {
    const {data} = await sbClient.from('carousel_images').select('url,title').order('order_num');
    if(!data || data.length===0) return;
    const track = document.getElementById('carouselTrack');
    const dotsEl = document.getElementById('carouselDots');
    track.innerHTML = ''; dotsEl.innerHTML = '';
    data.forEach((item, i) => {
      const slide = document.createElement('div');
      slide.className = 'carousel-slide';
      if(item.url) {
        const img = document.createElement('img');
        img.src = item.url; img.alt = item.title||'';
        slide.appendChild(img);
      } else {
        slide.innerHTML = `<div class="slide-placeholder"><div style="font-size:40px"><i class="fas fa-image"></i></div><div>${item.title||'Banner'}</div></div>`;
      }
      track.appendChild(slide);
      const dot = document.createElement('div');
      dot.className = 'carousel-dot'+(i===0?' active':'');
      dot.onclick = (event)=>{
        event.stopPropagation();
        goSlide(i);
        restartCarouselAuto();
      };
      dotsEl.appendChild(dot);
    });
    applyFontAwesomeIcons();
    initCarouselDrag();
    goSlide(0);
  } catch(e) {}
}

function goSlide(n) {
  const slides = document.querySelectorAll('.carousel-slide');
  const dots = document.querySelectorAll('.carousel-dot');
  if(!slides.length) return;
  state.currentSlide = (n + slides.length) % slides.length;
  setCarouselTransform(-state.currentSlide * 100, true);
  dots.forEach((d,i)=>d.classList.toggle('active', i===state.currentSlide));
}
function slideDir(d) { goSlide(state.currentSlide+d); }

// ---- RATINGS ----
const DUMMY_RATINGS = [
  { stars:5, text:'NeverEra Academy terbaik! Belajar PvP jadi lebih mudah.', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-gear' },
  { stars:5, text:'Materi grinding sangat lengkap dan mudah dipahami!', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-gamepad' },
  { stars:4, text:'Komunitas nya aktif dan selalu bantu satu sama lain.', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-trophy' },
  { stars:5, text:'Device setup guide nya keren banget, HP ku jadi lebih smooth!', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-mobile-screen' },
  { stars:5, text:'Admin responsif dan materi selalu up-to-date!', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-bolt' },
];

DUMMY_RATINGS.splice(0, DUMMY_RATINGS.length,
  { stars:5, text:'NeverEra Academy terbaik! Belajar PvP jadi lebih mudah.', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-gear' },
  { stars:5, text:'Materi grinding sangat lengkap dan mudah dipahami!', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-gamepad' },
  { stars:4, text:'Komunitas nya aktif dan selalu bantu satu sama lain.', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-trophy' },
  { stars:5, text:'Device setup guide nya keren banget, HP ku jadi lebih smooth!', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-mobile-screen' },
  { stars:5, text:'Admin responsif dan materi selalu up-to-date!', who:'Member', avatarIcon:'fa-bolt' }
);

async function loadRatings(force = false) {
  if(!force && state.allRatings.length && Date.now() - state.ratingsLoadedAt < 30000) {
    return;
  }
  let ratings = [...DUMMY_RATINGS];
  if(sbClient) {
    try {
      const {data} = await sbClient.from('ratings').select('*').order('created_at',{ascending:false}).limit(20);
      if(data && data.length>0) {
        const phones = [...new Set(data.map(r => r.phone).filter(Boolean))];
        const userMap = {};
        if(phones.length) {
          const { data: users } = await sbClient
            .from('users')
            .select('phone, display_name, winner_display_mode')
            .in('phone', phones);
          (users || []).forEach(user => {
            userMap[user.phone] = user;
          });
        }
        ratings = data.map(r => ({
            stars: r.stars,
            text: r.text,
            who: getMemberDisplayName(r.phone, userMap[r.phone]),
            avatarIcon: 'fa-user'
          }));
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }
  state.allRatings = ratings;
  state.ratingsLoadedAt = Date.now();
  renderRatings();
}

function getMemberDisplayName(phone, user = null) {
  if(user?.winner_display_mode === 'phone') return phone ? formatPhoneDisplay(phone) : 'Member';
  return user?.display_name || 'Member';
}

function renderRatings() {
  const list = document.getElementById('ratingsList');
  if(!list) return;
  const shuffled = [...state.allRatings].sort(()=>Math.random()-0.5).slice(0,5);
  list.innerHTML = '';
  const isNewUi = state.uiMode === 'new' || document.body.getAttribute('data-ui-mode') === 'new';
  const renderedRatings = shuffled;
  list.style.animation = 'none';
  renderedRatings.forEach(r => {
    const stars = '<i class="fas fa-star"></i>'.repeat(Number(r.stars) || 0);
    const avatarIcon = r.avatarIcon || 'fa-user';
    const item = document.createElement('div');
    item.className = 'rating-item';
    item.innerHTML = `
      <div class="rating-avatar"><i class="fas ${avatarIcon}"></i></div>
      <div><div class="rating-stars">${stars}</div>
      <div class="rating-text">${escapeHtml(r.text || '')}</div>
      <div class="rating-who">${escapeHtml(r.who || 'Member')}</div></div>`;
    list.appendChild(item);
  });
  if(isNewUi) requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    list.style.animation = '';
    startRatingFocusEffect();
  });
}

function startRatingFocusEffect() {
  if(state.ratingFocusFrame) return;
  const tick = () => {
    const isNewUi = state.uiMode === 'new' || document.body.getAttribute('data-ui-mode') === 'new';
    const section = document.querySelector('.rating-section');
    const items = [...document.querySelectorAll('#ratingsList .rating-item')];
    if(!isNewUi || !section || !items.length) {
      state.ratingFocusFrame = null;
      return;
    }
    const sectionRect = section.getBoundingClientRect();
    const center = sectionRect.left + sectionRect.width / 2;
    const maxDistance = Math.max(1, sectionRect.width / 2);
    let focusedItem = null;
    let closestDistance = Infinity;
    items.forEach(item => {
      const rect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
      const itemCenter = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
      const distance = Math.abs(center - itemCenter);
      const closeness = Math.max(0, 1 - Math.min(distance / maxDistance, 1));
      const eased = closeness * closeness * (3 - 2 * closeness);
      const scale = 0.7 + eased * 0.4;
      const opacity = 0.32 + eased * 0.68;
      const blur = (1 - eased) * 4;
      item.style.setProperty('--rating-scale', scale.toFixed(3));
      item.style.setProperty('--rating-opacity', opacity.toFixed(3));
      item.style.setProperty('--rating-blur', `${blur.toFixed(2)}px`);
      item.style.setProperty('--rating-z', String(Math.round(1 + eased * 10)));
      if(distance < closestDistance) {
        closestDistance = distance;
        focusedItem = item;
      }
    });
    items.forEach(item => item.classList.toggle('rating-focus', item === focusedItem));
    state.ratingFocusFrame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  };
  state.ratingFocusFrame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

function startRatingCycle() {
  if(state.ratingInterval) return;
  state.ratingTimerSec = 180;
  state.ratingInterval = setInterval(()=>{
    state.ratingTimerSec--;
    const m = Math.floor(state.ratingTimerSec/60);
    const s = state.ratingTimerSec%60;
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('ratingTimer');
    if(timerEl) timerEl.textContent = `Berganti dalam ${m}:${String(s).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    if(state.ratingTimerSec<=0) { state.ratingTimerSec=180; renderRatings(); }
  },1000);
}

// ---- RATING MODAL ----
function openRatingModal() {
  if(state.hasRated) {
    showToast('Anda sudah pernah memberi ulasan!', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const modal = document.getElementById('ratingModal');
  if(modal) modal.classList.add('open');
  state.selectedStars = 0;
  const ratingText = document.getElementById('ratingText');
  if(ratingText) ratingText.value = '';
  document.querySelectorAll('.star-btn').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('lit'));
}
function closeRatingModal() { 
  const modal = document.getElementById('ratingModal');
  if(modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
}

function setStar(n) {
  state.selectedStars = n;
  document.querySelectorAll('.star-btn').forEach((s,i)=>s.classList.toggle('lit',i<n));
}

async function submitRating() {
  if(!state.selectedStars){ showToast('Pilih jumlah bintang!'); return; }
  const text = document.getElementById('ratingText').value.trim();
  if(!text){ showToast('Tulis ulasan dulu!'); return; }
  if(state.hasRated){ showToast('Anda sudah memberi ulasan!', 'error'); return; }

  showLoading('Menyimpan ulasan...');
  
  if(sbClient) {
    try {
      await sbClient.from('ratings').insert({
        phone: state.phone, stars: state.selectedStars, text,
        created_at: new Date().toISOString()
      });
      state.hasRated = true;
      state.hasRatedLoadedAt = Date.now();
      const ratingBtn = document.getElementById('addRatingBtn');
      if(ratingBtn) {
        ratingBtn.disabled = true;
        ratingBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Anda sudah memberi ulasan';
      }
    } catch(e) { console.warn('Rating save error', e); showToast('Gagal menyimpan', 'error'); hideLoading(); return; }
  }
  const localWho = state.winnerDisplayMode === 'phone'
    ? (state.phone ? formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone) : 'Member')
    : (state.displayName || 'Member');
  state.allRatings.unshift({stars:state.selectedStars, text, who:localWho, avatarIcon:'fa-user'});
  state.ratingsLoadedAt = Date.now();
  renderRatings();
  closeRatingModal();
  hideLoading();
  showToast('Ulasan berhasil dikirim! Terima kasih!');
}

// ========== GIVEAWAY FUNCTIONS ==========
async function loadGiveaways() {
  if(!sbClient) {
    state.giveaways = [];
    state.giveawayEntries = [];
    state.giveawayWinners = [];
    return;
  }

  try {
    const { data: giveaways, error: giveawayError } = await sbClient
      .from('giveaways')
      .select('id,title,description,prize,is_active,starts_at,ends_at,created_at')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    if(giveawayError) throw giveawayError;

    state.giveaways = giveaways || [];
    const giveawayIds = state.giveaways.map(g => g.id);

    if(giveawayIds.length === 0) {
      state.giveawayEntries = [];
      state.giveawayWinners = [];
      return;
    }

    const { data: entries } = await sbClient
      .from('giveaway_entries')
      .select('giveaway_id,user_phone,joined_at')
      .in('giveaway_id', giveawayIds);

    const { data: winners } = await sbClient
      .from('giveaway_winners')
      .select('giveaway_id,user_phone,winner_name,prize_note,announced_at')
      .in('giveaway_id', giveawayIds)
      .order('announced_at', { ascending: true });

    state.giveawayEntries = entries || [];
    state.giveawayWinners = winners || [];
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal memuat giveaway:', e);
    state.giveaways = [];
    state.giveawayEntries = [];
    state.giveawayWinners = [];
    showToast('Gagal memuat giveaway', 'error');
  }
}

function formatDateTime(value) {
  if(!value) return '';
  return formatOffsetDateTime(value);
}

function formatChatDateTime(value) {
  if(!value) return '';
  return formatOffsetDateTime(value, { withSeconds: true });
}

function renderGiveaways() {
  const list = document.getElementById('giveawayList');
  if(!list) return;

  if(!state.giveaways.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="giveaway-empty"><i class="fas fa-gift"></i><br>Belum ada giveaway.</div>';
    return;
  }

  list.innerHTML = state.giveaways.map(giveaway => {
    const isActive = giveaway.is_active === true;
    const joined = state.giveawayEntries.some(entry => entry.giveaway_id === giveaway.id && entry.user_phone === state.phone);
    const winners = state.giveawayWinners.filter(winner => winner.giveaway_id === giveaway.id && isCurrentGiveawayWinner(winner, giveaway));
    const statusClass = isActive ? 'active' : 'done';
    const statusText = isActive ? 'Berlangsung' : 'Selesai';
    const dateParts = [
      giveaway.starts_at ? `Mulai: ${formatDateTime(giveaway.starts_at)}` : '',
      giveaway.ends_at ? `Akhir: ${formatDateTime(giveaway.ends_at)}` : ''
    ].filter(Boolean).join(' • ');

    let body = '';
    if(isActive) {
      body = joined
        ? `<div class="giveaway-waiting"><i class="fas fa-hourglass-half"></i> Kamu sudah join. Tunggu hasil giveaway diumumkan.</div>`
        : `<div class="giveaway-actions">
            <button class="giveaway-join-btn" onclick="joinGiveaway('${giveaway.id}')">
              <i class="fas fa-ticket"></i> Join Giveaway
            </button>
          </div>`;
    } else {
      body = renderGiveawayWinners(winners);
    }

    return `
      <div class="giveaway-card">
        <div class="giveaway-card-head">
          <div class="giveaway-card-title">${escapeHtml(giveaway.title || 'Giveaway')}</div>
          <span class="giveaway-status ${statusClass}">${statusText}</span>
        </div>
        ${giveaway.description ? `<div class="giveaway-desc">${escapeHtml(giveaway.description)}</div>` : ''}
        <div class="giveaway-prize"><i class="fas fa-award"></i> ${escapeHtml(giveaway.prize || 'Lampu neon')}</div>
        ${dateParts ? `<div class="giveaway-meta">${escapeHtml(dateParts)}</div>` : ''}
        ${body}
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function renderGiveawayWinners(winners) {
  if(!winners.length) {
    return `
      <div class="giveaway-winners">
        <div class="giveaway-winners-title"><i class="fas fa-trophy"></i> Pemenang</div>
        <div class="giveaway-waiting">Pemenang belum diumumkan.</div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  const ownWinner = winners.find(winner => winner.user_phone === state.phone);
  const visibleWinner = ownWinner || winners[0];
  const hiddenCount = Math.max(0, winners.length - 1);
  const name = ownWinner
    ? (visibleWinner.winner_name || state.displayName || 'Kamu')
    : `${winners.length}+ lainnya`;
  const prize = ownWinner
    ? (visibleWinner.prize_note ? ` - ${visibleWinner.prize_note}` : ' - Lampu neon')
    : '';

  return `
    <div class="giveaway-winners">
      <div class="giveaway-winners-title"><i class="fas fa-trophy"></i> Pemenang</div>
      <div class="giveaway-winner-item ${ownWinner ? 'neon' : ''}">
        <i class="fas ${ownWinner ? 'fa-bolt' : 'fa-crown'}"></i>
        <span>${escapeHtml(name + prize)}</span>
      </div>
      ${ownWinner && hiddenCount ? `<div class="giveaway-waiting">${hiddenCount}+ lainnya</div>` : ''}
    </div>
  `;
}

async function joinGiveaway(giveawayId) {
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone) {
    showToast('Login dulu sebelum join giveaway', 'error');
    return;
  }

  showLoading('Join giveaway...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('giveaway_entries').upsert({
      giveaway_id: giveawayId,
      user_phone: state.phone,
      joined_at: new Date().toISOString()
    }, { onConflict: 'giveaway_id,user_phone' });

    if(error) throw error;
    showToast('Berhasil join giveaway. Tunggu hasilnya ya.');
    await initGiveaways();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal join giveaway:', e);
    showToast('Gagal join giveaway', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function initGiveaways() {
  await loadGiveaways();
  renderGiveaways();
  await checkWinnerNotification();
}

const PVP_CHAT_LIMIT_PER_PLAYER = 15;
const PVP_REPORT_WAIT_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;

function getPvpDisplayName() {
  return state.displayName || (state.phone ? formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone) : 'Member');
}

function getPvpInitial(name = '') {
  return String(name || 'P').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'P';
}

function getPvpAvatarProfile(phone = '') {
  return state.pvpAvatarProfiles?.[String(phone || '')] || {};
}

function getPvpAvatarUrl(phone = '') {
  return normalizeProfilePictureUrl(getPvpAvatarProfile(phone).profilePictureUrl || '');
}

function getPvpAvatarBorder(phone = '') {
  return normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(getPvpAvatarProfile(phone).avatarBorder || 'white');
}

function renderPvpAvatar(name = '', phone = '', extraClass = '') {
  return renderNeedHelpAvatar(name, getPvpAvatarBorder(phone), getPvpAvatarUrl(phone), `pvp-avatar-frame ${extraClass}`.trim());
}

async function loadPvpAvatarProfiles(extraPhones = []) {
  const match = state.pvpActiveMatch || {};
  const phones = [
    ...extraPhones,
    ...(state.pvpQueue || []).map(entry => entry.user_phone),
    state.pvpActiveEntry?.user_phone,
    match.requester_phone,
    match.opponent_phone,
    ...(state.pvpMatchMessages || []).map(message => message.user_phone)
  ];
  state.pvpAvatarProfiles = await loadAvatarProfilesForPhones(phones);
}

function getCurrentPvpRole(match = state.pvpActiveMatch) {
  if(!match || !state.phone) return '';
  if(String(match.requester_phone) === String(state.phone)) return 'requester';
  if(String(match.opponent_phone) === String(state.phone)) return 'opponent';
  return '';
}

function isPvpMatchComplete(match = state.pvpActiveMatch) {
  return !!match && (match.status === 'completed' || (match.requester_accepted && match.opponent_accepted));
}

function isPvpMatchLocked() {
  return !!state.pvpActiveMatch && state.pvpActiveMatch.status === 'active' && !isPvpMatchComplete();
}

function getPvpElapsedMs(match = state.pvpActiveMatch) {
  if(!match?.started_at) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, Date.now() - new Date(match.started_at).getTime());
}

function formatPvpElapsed(ms) {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  const minutes = String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
  const seconds = String(total % 60).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${minutes}:${seconds}`;
}

function formatPvpClock(value, zone = state.timezone) {
  if(!value) return '-';
  try {
    return formatOffsetDateTime(value, { withSeconds: true, zone });
  } catch(e) {
    return formatDateTime(value) || '-';
  }
}

async function deletePvpMatchCompletely(matchId) {
  if(!sbClient || !matchId) return;
  const messageResult = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_messages').delete().eq('match_id', matchId);
  if(messageResult.error) throw messageResult.error;
  const reportResult = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_reports').delete().eq('match_id', matchId);
  if(reportResult.error) throw reportResult.error;
  const queueResult = await sbClient.from('pvp_queue').delete().eq('matched_id', matchId);
  if(queueResult.error) throw queueResult.error;
  const matchResult = await sbClient.from('pvp_matches').delete().eq('id', matchId);
  if(matchResult.error) throw matchResult.error;
}

async function cleanupStalePvpRecords() {
  if(!sbClient || state.pvpCleanupInFlight) return;
  state.pvpCleanupInFlight = true;
  try {
    const staleQueue = await sbClient.from('pvp_queue').delete().neq('status', 'waiting');
    if(staleQueue.error) console.warn('Cleanup stale PvP queue error:', staleQueue.error);
    const { data: completedMatches, error } = await sbClient.from('pvp_matches')
      .select('id')
      .neq('status', 'active');
    if(error) throw error;
    for(const match of completedMatches || []) {
      await deletePvpMatchCompletely(match.id);
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Cleanup stale PvP records error:', e);
  } finally {
    state.pvpCleanupInFlight = false;
  }
}

async function initFindPvp() {
  showPage('findpvp');
  state.currentPage = 'findpvp';
  saveMemberRoute('findpvp', true);
  await loadPvpState();
  startPvpTimer();
}

async function loadPvpState() {
  const status = document.getElementById('pvpStatusText');
  if(!sbClient) {
    if(status) status.textContent = 'Supabase belum aktif.';
    return;
  }
  if(!state.phone) {
    if(status) status.textContent = 'Login dulu sebelum cari PvP.';
    return;
  }
  try {
    await cleanupStalePvpRecords();
    const [queueRes, ownQueueRes, matchRes] = await Promise.all([
      sbClient.from('pvp_queue')
        .select('id,user_phone,display_name,status,created_at,updated_at')
        .eq('status', 'waiting')
        .neq('user_phone', state.phone)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true }),
      sbClient.from('pvp_queue')
        .select('id,user_phone,display_name,status,created_at,updated_at')
        .eq('status', 'waiting')
        .eq('user_phone', state.phone)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle(),
      sbClient.from('pvp_matches')
        .select('*')
        .eq('status', 'active')
        .or(`requester_phone.eq.${state.phone},opponent_phone.eq.${state.phone}`)
        .order('started_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle()
    ]);
    if(queueRes.error) throw queueRes.error;
    if(ownQueueRes.error) throw ownQueueRes.error;
    if(matchRes.error) throw matchRes.error;
    state.pvpQueue = queueRes.data || [];
    state.pvpActiveEntry = ownQueueRes.data || null;
    state.pvpActiveMatch = matchRes.data || null;
    if(state.pvpActiveMatch?.id) {
      await Promise.all([
        loadPvpMatchMessages(false),
        loadPvpMatchReports(false)
      ]);
    } else {
      state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
      state.pvpMatchReports = [];
    }
    await loadPvpAvatarProfiles();
    if(isPvpMatchLocked()) {
      state.currentPage = 'findpvp';
      showPage('findpvp');
      saveMemberRoute('findpvp', true);
    }
    renderFindPvp();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('PvP state error:', e);
    if(status) status.textContent = 'Gagal memuat Find PvP. Pastikan tabel Supabase sudah dibuat.';
  }
}

function renderFindPvp() {
  const card = document.getElementById('pvpMainCard');
  if(!card) return;

  if(state.pvpActiveMatch && state.pvpActiveMatch.status === 'active') {
    renderPvpMatch(card);
    return;
  }

  const ownEntry = state.pvpActiveEntry;
  const queueActionLabel = ownEntry ? 'Batal Cari' : 'Cari PvP';
  const queueActionIcon = ownEntry ? 'fa-xmark' : 'fa-magnifying-glass';
  const queueActionClass = ownEntry ? 'settings-save secondary pvp-action-btn' : 'settings-save pvp-action-btn';
  card.innerHTML = `
    <div class="pvp-hero">
      <div class="pvp-title">Cari lawan PvP</div>
      <div class="pvp-subtitle">Masuk antrean, tunggu orang lain klik nama kamu, atau tantang member lain yang sedang cari PvP.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="pvp-actions">
      <button class="${queueActionClass}" type="button" onclick="togglePvpQueue()"><i class="fas ${queueActionIcon}"></i> ${queueActionLabel}</button>
      <button class="settings-save secondary pvp-action-btn pvp-refresh-inline" type="button" onclick="refreshFindPvp(event)"><i class="fas fa-rotate"></i> Refresh</button>
    </div>
    <div class="games-question">
      <div class="games-question-title">Status</div>
      <div class="games-question-text" id="pvpStatusText"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="settings-card-title">Orang yang sedang cari PvP</div>
    <div class="pvp-list" id="pvpQueueList"></div>
    <button class="settings-save secondary pvp-action-btn pvp-refresh-bottom" type="button" onclick="refreshFindPvp(event)"><i class="fas fa-rotate"></i> Refresh</button>
  `;
  const status = document.getElementById('pvpStatusText');
  const list = document.getElementById('pvpQueueList');
  status.innerHTML = ownEntry
    ? `Kamu sedang masuk antrean PvP sejak ${escapeHtml(formatRelativeTime(ownEntry.created_at))}. Tunggu orang lain klik nama kamu.`
    : 'Kamu belum masuk antrean. Tekan Cari PvP untuk muncul di list orang lain.';

  if(!state.pvpQueue.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Belum ada orang lain yang cari PvP.</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = state.pvpQueue.map(entry => {
    const name = entry.display_name || 'Member';
    return `
      <button class="pvp-player-row" onclick="challengePvpQueue('${escapeHtml(String(entry.id))}')">
        ${renderPvpAvatar(name, entry.user_phone)}
        <div>
          <div class="pvp-player-name">${escapeHtml(name)}</div>
          <div class="pvp-player-meta">Cari PvP · ${escapeHtml(formatRelativeTime(entry.created_at))}</div>
        </div>
        <i class="fas fa-crosshairs"></i>
      </button>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function getPvpMessageCountForCurrentUser() {
  return state.pvpMatchMessages.filter(message => String(message.user_phone || '') === String(state.phone || '')).length;
}

function getPvpOtherAccepted(match = state.pvpActiveMatch) {
  const role = getCurrentPvpRole(match);
  if(role === 'requester') return !!match?.opponent_accepted;
  if(role === 'opponent') return !!match?.requester_accepted;
  return false;
}

function getPvpOwnAccepted(match = state.pvpActiveMatch) {
  const role = getCurrentPvpRole(match);
  if(role === 'requester') return !!match?.requester_accepted;
  if(role === 'opponent') return !!match?.opponent_accepted;
  return false;
}

function hasReportedCurrentPvpMatch() {
  const matchId = state.pvpActiveMatch?.id;
  if(!matchId || !state.phone) return false;
  return state.pvpMatchReports.some(report =>
    String(report.match_id || '') === String(matchId) &&
    String(report.reporter_phone || '') === String(state.phone)
  );
}

function canReportCurrentPvpMatch() {
  const match = state.pvpActiveMatch;
  if(!match || match.status !== 'active') return false;
  if(getPvpOtherAccepted(match)) return false;
  return getPvpElapsedMs(match) >= PVP_REPORT_WAIT_MS;
}

function getPvpReportButtonText() {
  if(hasReportedCurrentPvpMatch()) return 'Report sudah dikirim';
  if(getPvpOtherAccepted()) return 'Lawan sudah acc';
  const remaining = PVP_REPORT_WAIT_MS - getPvpElapsedMs();
  if(remaining > 0) return `Report aktif dalam ${formatPvpElapsed(remaining)}`;
  return 'Report lawan belum acc';
}

async function loadPvpMatchMessages(render = true) {
  const matchId = state.pvpActiveMatch?.id;
  if(!sbClient || !matchId) {
    state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
    if(render) renderPvpMatchChat();
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('pvp_match_messages')
      .select('id,match_id,user_phone,display_name,body,created_at')
      .eq('match_id', matchId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
      .order('id', { ascending: true });
    if(error) throw error;
    state.pvpMatchMessages = data || [];
    await loadPvpAvatarProfiles();
    if(render) renderPvpMatchChat();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('PvP match messages error:', e);
    state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
    if(render) renderPvpMatchChat('Gagal memuat chat match. Pastikan SQL PvP chat sudah dijalankan.');
  }
}

async function loadPvpMatchReports(render = true) {
  const matchId = state.pvpActiveMatch?.id;
  if(!sbClient || !matchId) {
    state.pvpMatchReports = [];
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('pvp_match_reports')
      .select('id,match_id,reporter_phone,reported_phone,reason,created_at')
      .eq('match_id', matchId);
    if(error) throw error;
    state.pvpMatchReports = data || [];
    if(render) renderPvpReportButton();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('PvP match reports error:', e);
    state.pvpMatchReports = [];
    if(render) renderPvpReportButton();
  }
}

function renderPvpMatchChat(errorText = '') {
  const list = document.getElementById('pvpMatchChatList');
  const count = document.getElementById('pvpMatchChatCount');
  const input = document.getElementById('pvpMatchChatInput');
  const sendBtn = document.getElementById('pvpMatchChatSend');
  if(!list) return;
  const ownCount = getPvpMessageCountForCurrentUser();
  const limitReached = ownCount >= PVP_CHAT_LIMIT_PER_PLAYER;
  if(count) count.textContent = `${ownCount}/${PVP_CHAT_LIMIT_PER_PLAYER} chat kamu`;
  if(input) {
    input.disabled = limitReached;
    input.placeholder = limitReached ? 'Limit 15 chat kamu sudah habis' : 'Chat untuk janjian ketemu di game';
  }
  if(sendBtn) sendBtn.disabled = limitReached;
  if(errorText) {
    list.innerHTML = `<div class="needhelp-empty">${escapeHtml(errorText)}</div>`;
    return;
  }
  if(!state.pvpMatchMessages.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Belum ada chat. Pakai ini untuk janjian server/tempat ketemu.</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = state.pvpMatchMessages.map(message => {
    const own = String(message.user_phone || '') === String(state.phone || '');
    const name = message.display_name || 'Member';
    return `
      <div class="pvp-chat-message ${own ? 'own' : 'other'}">
        ${renderPvpAvatar(name, message.user_phone, 'pvp-chat-avatar')}
        <div class="pvp-chat-body">
          <div class="pvp-chat-meta">${escapeHtml(name)} · ${escapeHtml(formatChatDateTime(message.created_at) || '')}</div>
          <div class="pvp-chat-bubble">${escapeHtml(message.body || '')}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
  list.scrollTop = list.scrollHeight;
}

function renderPvpReportButton() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('pvpReportBtn');
  if(!btn) return;
  const disabled = !canReportCurrentPvpMatch() || hasReportedCurrentPvpMatch();
  btn.disabled = disabled;
  btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-flag"></i> ${escapeHtml(getPvpReportButtonText())}`;
}

function handlePvpChatKey(event) {
  if(event.key !== 'Enter' || event.shiftKey) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  sendPvpMatchMessage();
}

async function sendPvpMatchMessage() {
  const match = state.pvpActiveMatch;
  const input = document.getElementById('pvpMatchChatInput');
  const body = input?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!match || match.status !== 'active') return;
  if(!body) return;
  if(getPvpMessageCountForCurrentUser() >= PVP_CHAT_LIMIT_PER_PLAYER) {
    showToast('Limit chat PvP kamu sudah 15 pesan', 'error');
    renderPvpMatchChat();
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_messages').insert({
      match_id: match.id,
      user_phone: state.phone,
      display_name: getPvpDisplayName(),
      body
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    input.value = '';
    await loadPvpMatchMessages();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Send PvP chat error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal kirim chat PvP', 'error');
  }
}

async function reportPvpNoAcc() {
  const match = state.pvpActiveMatch;
  if(!match || match.status !== 'active') return;
  if(hasReportedCurrentPvpMatch()) {
    showToast('Report match ini sudah dikirim');
    return;
  }
  if(!canReportCurrentPvpMatch()) {
    showToast(getPvpReportButtonText(), 'error');
    return;
  }
  const role = getCurrentPvpRole(match);
  const reportedPhone = role === 'requester' ? match.opponent_phone : match.requester_phone;
  const reportedName = role === 'requester' ? match.opponent_name : match.requester_name;
  showLoading('Mengirim report PvP...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pvp_match_reports').insert({
      match_id: match.id,
      reporter_phone: state.phone,
      reporter_name: getPvpDisplayName(),
      reported_phone: reportedPhone,
      reported_name: reportedName || 'Member',
      reason: 'Tidak acc lebih dari 10 menit'
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    await loadPvpMatchReports();
    showToast('Report PvP dikirim');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('PvP report error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal kirim report PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function renderPvpMatch(card) {
  const match = state.pvpActiveMatch;
  const elapsed = getPvpElapsedMs(match);
  const role = getCurrentPvpRole(match);
  const ownAccepted = getPvpOwnAccepted(match);
  const requesterName = match.requester_name || 'Member 1';
  const opponentName = match.opponent_name || 'Member 2';
  card.innerHTML = `
    <div class="pvp-hero">
      <div class="pvp-title"><i class="fas fa-lock"></i> Match PvP Aktif</div>
      <div class="pvp-subtitle">Gunakan chat match untuk janjian server/tempat ketemu di game. Halaman dikunci sampai kedua pihak menekan acc.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="pvp-match-grid">
      <div class="pvp-match-player">
        ${renderPvpAvatar(requesterName, match.requester_phone)}
        <div class="pvp-match-name">${escapeHtml(requesterName)}</div>
        <div class="pvp-match-phone"><i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ${escapeHtml(formatPhoneDisplay(match.requester_phone || '-'))}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="pvp-match-player">
        ${renderPvpAvatar(opponentName, match.opponent_phone)}
        <div class="pvp-match-name">${escapeHtml(opponentName)}</div>
        <div class="pvp-match-phone"><i class="fas fa-phone"></i> ${escapeHtml(formatPhoneDisplay(match.opponent_phone || '-'))}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pvp-time-grid">
      <div class="pvp-time-box">
        <div class="pvp-time-label">WIB</div>
        <div class="pvp-time-value">${escapeHtml(formatPvpClock(match.started_at, 'WIB'))}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="pvp-time-box">
        <div class="pvp-time-label">WITA</div>
        <div class="pvp-time-value">${escapeHtml(formatPvpClock(match.started_at, 'WITA'))}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="pvp-time-box">
        <div class="pvp-time-label">WIT</div>
        <div class="pvp-time-value">${escapeHtml(formatPvpClock(match.started_at, 'WIT'))}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="games-question">
      <div class="games-question-title">Durasi Match</div>
      <div class="games-question-text" id="pvpElapsedText">${escapeHtml(formatPvpElapsed(elapsed))}</div>
      <div class="pvp-small">ACC bisa ditekan kapan saja setelah match ditemukan.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="pvp-accept-grid">
      <div class="pvp-accept-box ${match.requester_accepted ? 'done' : ''}">
        <div class="pvp-accept-label">${escapeHtml(requesterName)}</div>
        <div class="pvp-accept-value">${match.requester_accepted ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Sudah acc' : '<i class="fas fa-hourglass-half"></i> Belum acc'}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="pvp-accept-box ${match.opponent_accepted ? 'done' : ''}">
        <div class="pvp-accept-label">${escapeHtml(opponentName)}</div>
        <div class="pvp-accept-value">${match.opponent_accepted ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Sudah acc' : '<i class="fas fa-hourglass-half"></i> Belum acc'}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pvp-lock-note"><i class="fas fa-triangle-exclamation"></i> Kamu tidak bisa keluar dari halaman ini sebelum match selesai dan dua-duanya acc.</div>
    <button class="settings-save pvp-action-btn" type="button" onclick="acceptPvpComplete()" ${!ownAccepted ? '' : 'disabled'}>
      <i class="fas fa-check-double"></i> ${ownAccepted ? 'Kamu sudah acc, tunggu pihak kedua' : 'Acc PvP Selesai'}
    </button>
    <div class="pvp-match-chat">
      <div class="pvp-chat-head">
        <span><i class="fas fa-comments"></i> Chat Match</span>
        <span class="pvp-chat-count" id="pvpMatchChatCount">0/${PVP_CHAT_LIMIT_PER_PLAYER} chat kamu</span>
      </div>
      <div class="pvp-chat-list" id="pvpMatchChatList"></div>
      <div class="pvp-chat-form">
        <input class="settings-input" id="pvpMatchChatInput" placeholder="Chat untuk janjian ketemu di game" maxlength="240" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" spellcheck="false" onkeydown="handlePvpChatKey(event)">
        <button class="settings-save" id="pvpMatchChatSend" type="button" onclick="sendPvpMatchMessage()"><i class="fas fa-paper-plane"></i></button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <button class="settings-save secondary pvp-action-btn pvp-report-btn" id="pvpReportBtn" type="button" onclick="reportPvpNoAcc()"><i class="fas fa-flag"></i> ${escapeHtml(getPvpReportButtonText())}</button>
    <button class="settings-save secondary pvp-action-btn pvp-refresh-bottom" type="button" onclick="refreshFindPvp(event)"><i class="fas fa-rotate"></i> Refresh</button>
  `;
  renderPvpMatchChat();
  renderPvpReportButton();
}

function startPvpTimer() {
  if(state.pvpTimerInterval) clearInterval(state.pvpTimerInterval);
  state.pvpTimerInterval = setInterval(() => {
    if(state.currentPage !== 'findpvp' || !state.pvpActiveMatch) return;
    const elapsedText = document.getElementById('pvpElapsedText');
    if(elapsedText) elapsedText.textContent = formatPvpElapsed(getPvpElapsedMs());
    renderPvpReportButton();
  }, 1000);
}

async function refreshFindPvp(event) {
  event?.preventDefault?.();
  event?.stopPropagation?.();
  if(state.pvpRefreshing) return;
  if(!canRunDataRequest('findpvp_refresh')) return;
  const btn = event?.currentTarget;
  if(btn?.disabled) return;
  state.pvpRefreshing = true;
  btn?.classList.add('refreshing');
  if(btn) btn.disabled = true;
  try {
    state.currentPage = 'findpvp';
    const profile = await loadUserProfile();
    if(!profile && sbClient && state.phone && state.profileLoadStatus === 'missing') {
      returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
      return;
    }
    if(!profile && sbClient && state.phone) {
      returnToHomeBecauseProfileBlocked();
      return;
    }
    renderProfileAvatar();
    renderProfileEditCurrentAvatar();
    updateGreeting();
    await loadPvpState();
    startPvpTimer();
    showToast('Find PvP diperbarui');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Refresh Find PvP error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal refresh Find PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    state.pvpRefreshing = false;
    btn?.classList.remove('refreshing');
    if(btn) btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

async function joinPvpQueue() {
  if(state.pvpLoading) return;
  if(isPvpMatchLocked()) {
    renderFindPvp();
    return;
  }
  state.pvpLoading = true;
  showLoading('Masuk antrean PvP...');
  try {
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    if(!state.phone) throw new Error('Login dulu sebelum cari PvP');
    if(state.pvpActiveEntry) {
      showToast('Kamu sudah masuk antrean PvP');
      return;
    }
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pvp_queue').insert({
      user_phone: state.phone,
      display_name: getPvpDisplayName(),
      status: 'waiting',
      created_at: now,
      updated_at: now
    });
    if(error) throw error;
    showToast('Kamu masuk antrean PvP');
    await loadPvpState();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Join PvP error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal masuk antrean PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    state.pvpLoading = false;
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function togglePvpQueue() {
  if(state.pvpActiveEntry) {
    cancelPvpQueue();
    return;
  }
  joinPvpQueue();
}

async function cancelPvpQueue() {
  if(state.pvpLoading) return;
  if(isPvpMatchLocked()) {
    showToast('Match sudah aktif, tidak bisa batal antrean.', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!state.pvpActiveEntry) {
    showToast('Kamu belum masuk antrean PvP');
    return;
  }
  state.pvpLoading = true;
  showLoading('Membatalkan antrean...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pvp_queue')
      .delete()
