      .eq('id', state.pvpActiveEntry.id)
      .eq('user_phone', state.phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    state.pvpActiveEntry = null;
    showToast('Antrean PvP dibatalkan');
    await loadPvpState();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Cancel PvP error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal batal antrean PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    state.pvpLoading = false;
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function challengePvpQueue(entryId) {
  if(state.pvpLoading) return;
  if(isPvpMatchLocked()) {
    renderFindPvp();
    return;
  }
  state.pvpLoading = true;
  showLoading('Membuat match PvP...');
  try {
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    if(!state.phone) throw new Error('Login dulu sebelum match PvP');
    const { data: entry, error: entryError } = await sbClient.from('pvp_queue')
      .select('id,user_phone,display_name,status')
      .eq('id', entryId)
      .eq('status', 'waiting')
      .maybeSingle();
    if(entryError) throw entryError;
    if(!entry) throw new Error('Orang ini sudah tidak tersedia');
    if(String(entry.user_phone) === String(state.phone)) throw new Error('Tidak bisa match dengan diri sendiri');
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const { data: match, error: matchError } = await sbClient.from('pvp_matches').insert({
      requester_phone: entry.user_phone,
      requester_name: entry.display_name || 'Member',
      opponent_phone: state.phone,
      opponent_name: getPvpDisplayName(),
      status: 'active',
      requester_accepted: false,
      opponent_accepted: false,
      started_at: now,
      updated_at: now
    }).select('*').single();
    if(matchError) throw matchError;
    await Promise.all([
      sbClient.from('pvp_queue').delete().eq('id', entry.id),
      sbClient.from('pvp_queue').delete().eq('user_phone', state.phone).eq('status', 'waiting')
    ]);
    state.pvpActiveMatch = match;
    showToast('Match PvP dibuat. Halaman dikunci.');
    await initFindPvp();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Challenge PvP error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal membuat match PvP', 'error');
  } finally {
    state.pvpLoading = false;
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function acceptPvpComplete() {
  const match = state.pvpActiveMatch;
  if(!match || match.status !== 'active') return;
  const role = getCurrentPvpRole(match);
  if(!role) {
    showToast('Kamu bukan bagian dari match ini', 'error');
    return;
  }
  const update = {
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  if(role === 'requester') update.requester_accepted = true;
  if(role === 'opponent') update.opponent_accepted = true;
  const otherAccepted = role === 'requester' ? match.opponent_accepted : match.requester_accepted;
  if(otherAccepted) update.status = 'completed';
  showLoading('Menyimpan acc...');
  try {
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('pvp_matches')
      .update(update)
      .eq('id', match.id);
    if(error) throw error;
    if(update.status === 'completed') {
      await deletePvpMatchCompletely(match.id);
      state.pvpActiveMatch = null;
      state.pvpMatchMessages = [];
      state.pvpMatchReports = [];
      await loadPvpState();
      showToast('PvP selesai. Kedua pihak sudah acc.');
      goToDashboardFromFindPvp();
    } else {
      await loadPvpState();
      showToast('Acc tersimpan. Tunggu pihak kedua.');
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Accept PvP error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal acc PvP selesai', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

function subscribePvpRealtime() {
}

function getNeedHelpDisplayName() {
  return state.displayName || (state.phone ? formatPhoneDisplay(state.phone) : 'Member');
}

function needHelpInitial(name = '') {
  return String(name || 'M').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || 'M';
}

function normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(value = 'white') {
  const normalized = String(value || '').toLowerCase();
  if(normalized === 'black') return 'purple';
  return ['white','bronze','silver','purple'].includes(normalized) ? normalized : 'white';
}

function getNeedHelpAvatarBorderLabel(value = 'white') {
  const labels = {
    white: 'Member biasa',
    bronze: 'Bronze',
    silver: 'Silver + cahaya',
    purple: 'Ungu space'
  };
  return labels[normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(value)] || labels.white;
}

function getNeedHelpMessageAvatarBorder(message = {}) {
  const phone = String(message.user_phone || '');
  return normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(state.needHelpAvatarProfiles?.[phone]?.avatarBorder || state.needHelpAvatarBorders?.[phone] || 'white');
}

function getNeedHelpMessageAvatarUrl(message = {}) {
  const phone = String(message.user_phone || '');
  return normalizeProfilePictureUrl(state.needHelpAvatarProfiles?.[phone]?.profilePictureUrl || '');
}

function isOwnNeedHelpMessage(message = {}) {
  const messagePhone = parsePhoneRaw(String(message.user_phone || ''));
  const ownPhone = parsePhoneRaw(String(state.phone || ''));
  return !!messagePhone && !!ownPhone && messagePhone === ownPhone;
}

function compareNeedHelpMessages(a = {}, b = {}) {
  const aId = Number(a.id || 0);
  const bId = Number(b.id || 0);
  if(aId !== bId) return aId - bId;
  const aTime = new Date(a.created_at || 0).getTime();
  const bTime = new Date(b.created_at || 0).getTime();
  if(aTime !== bTime) return aTime - bTime;
  return 0;
}

function orderNeedHelpMessages(messages = []) {
  return [...messages].sort(compareNeedHelpMessages);
}

const NEED_HELP_RESOLVE_COMMAND = 'form telah terbantu';
const NEED_HELP_CHAT_DELETE_DELAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function normalizeNeedHelpCommand(text = '') {
  return String(text || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

function isNeedHelpResolved(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost()) {
  return (post?.status || '').toLowerCase() === 'resolved';
}

function isNeedHelpOwner(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost()) {
  const postPhone = parsePhoneRaw(String(post?.user_phone || ''));
  const ownPhone = parsePhoneRaw(String(state.phone || ''));
  return !!postPhone && !!ownPhone && postPhone === ownPhone;
}

function getNeedHelpResolvedAt(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost()) {
  return post?.resolved_at || (isNeedHelpResolved(post) ? post?.updated_at : null);
}

function getNeedHelpCleanupRemainingMs(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost()) {
  const resolvedAt = getNeedHelpResolvedAt(post);
  if(!resolvedAt) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, new Date(resolvedAt).getTime() + NEED_HELP_CHAT_DELETE_DELAY_MS - Date.now());
}

function formatNeedHelpCountdown(ms) {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(ms / 1000));
  const hours = String(Math.floor(total / 3600)).padStart(2, '0');
  const minutes = String(Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
  const seconds = String(total % 60).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${hours}:${minutes}:${seconds}`;
}

function getNeedHelpResolutionText(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost()) {
  if(post?.chat_deleted_at) return 'Chat sudah dibersihkan.';
  const remaining = getNeedHelpCleanupRemainingMs(post);
  if(remaining <= 0) return 'Chat akan dibersihkan saat data diperbarui.';
  return `Chat dihapus otomatis dalam ${formatNeedHelpCountdown(remaining)}.`;
}

function renderNeedHelpResolutionNotice(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost(), options = {}) {
  const extraClass = options.thread ? ' thread-notice' : '';
  return `
    <div class="needhelp-resolution${extraClass}">
      <div class="needhelp-resolution-icon"><i class="fas fa-circle-check"></i></div>
      <div class="needhelp-resolution-title">Form selesai</div>
      <div class="needhelp-resolution-subtitle">Urusan selesai, bantuan sudah ditandai berhasil.</div>
      <div class="needhelp-resolution-countdown" data-needhelp-countdown="true">${escapeHtml(getNeedHelpResolutionText(post))}</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function updateNeedHelpResolutionCountdown() {
  const post = getActiveNeedHelpPost();
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-needhelp-countdown="true"]').forEach(el => {
    el.textContent = getNeedHelpResolutionText(post);
  });
}

function startNeedHelpResolutionCountdown() {
  if(state.needHelpResolutionInterval) clearInterval(state.needHelpResolutionInterval);
  const post = getActiveNeedHelpPost();
  if(!isNeedHelpResolved(post)) {
    state.needHelpResolutionInterval = null;
    return;
  }
  updateNeedHelpResolutionCountdown();
  state.needHelpResolutionInterval = setInterval(updateNeedHelpResolutionCountdown, 1000);
}

async function cleanupExpiredNeedHelpChats(posts = []) {
  if(!sbClient) return;
  const duePosts = (posts || []).filter(post => {
    if(!isNeedHelpResolved(post) || post.chat_deleted_at) return false;
    return getNeedHelpCleanupRemainingMs(post) <= 0;
  });
  for(const post of duePosts) {
    try {
      const messageResult = await sbClient.from('help_messages').delete().eq('post_id', post.id);
      if(messageResult.error) throw messageResult.error;
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      await sbClient.from('help_posts')
        .update({ chat_deleted_at: now, updated_at: now })
        .eq('id', post.id);
      post.chat_deleted_at = now;
      post.updated_at = now;
      if(String(state.activeNeedHelpPostId) === String(post.id)) state.needHelpMessages = [];
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('Need Help resolved cleanup error:', e);
    }
  }
}

function renderNeedHelpAvatar(name = '', border = 'white', imageUrl = '', extraClass = '') {
  const frame = normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(border);
  const url = normalizeProfilePictureUrl(imageUrl);
  const loopDelay = duration => `-${((Date.now() % duration) / 1000).toFixed(2)}s`;
  const animationStyle = frame === 'purple'
    ? ` style="--avatar-particle-delay:${loopDelay(14000)};--avatar-triangle-delay:${loopDelay(3000)};--avatar-orbit-delay:${loopDelay(5600)};--avatar-orbit-delay-2:${loopDelay(6400)}"`
    : '';
  const core = url
    ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt="${escapeHtml(name || 'Member')}" onerror="this.replaceWith(document.createTextNode('${escapeHtml(needHelpInitial(name))}'))">`
    : escapeHtml(needHelpInitial(name));
  return `
    <div class="needhelp-avatar needhelp-avatar--${escapeHtml(frame)} ${escapeHtml(extraClass)}" title="${escapeHtml(frame)}"${animationStyle}>
      <span class="needhelp-avatar-orbit"></span>
      <span class="needhelp-avatar-sparkles"></span>
      <span class="needhelp-avatar-triangles"></span>
      <span class="needhelp-avatar-core">${core}</span>
    </div>
  `;
}

async function loadNeedHelpAvatarBorders(messages = []) {
  state.needHelpAvatarBorders = {};
  state.needHelpAvatarProfiles = {};
  if(!sbClient) return;
  const phones = [...new Set((messages || []).map(message => message.user_phone).filter(Boolean))];
  if(!phones.length) return;
  state.needHelpAvatarProfiles = await loadAvatarProfilesForPhones(phones);
  state.needHelpAvatarBorders = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(state.needHelpAvatarProfiles).map(([phone, profile]) => [
    phone,
    normalizeNeedHelpAvatarBorder(profile.avatarBorder || 'white')
  ]));
}

function formatRelativeTime(value) {
  if(!value) return '';
  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(value).getTime();
  if(!Number.isFinite(diff)) return '';
  const minutes = Math.max(0, Math.floor(diff / 60000));
  if(minutes < 1) return 'baru saja';
  if(minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m lalu`;
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  if(hours < 24) return `${hours}j lalu`;
  return `${Math.floor(hours / 24)}h lalu`;
}

function toggleNeedHelpComposer(force) {
  const box = document.getElementById('needHelpComposer');
  if(!box) return;
  const show = typeof force === 'boolean' ? force : !box.classList.contains('show');
  box.classList.toggle('show', show);
  if(show) document.getElementById('needHelpTitle')?.focus();
}

function setNeedHelpThreadPage(open) {
  document.getElementById('needHelpLayout')?.classList.toggle('thread-open', !!open);
}

async function initNeedHelp() {
  setNeedHelpThreadPage(!!state.activeNeedHelpPostId);
  await loadNeedHelpPosts();
  if(state.activeNeedHelpPostId) {
    renderNeedHelpThreadHead();
    await loadNeedHelpMessages();
  } else {
    state.needHelpAvatarBorders = {};
    state.needHelpAvatarProfiles = {};
  }
  subscribeNeedHelpList();
}

async function loadNeedHelpPosts() {
  const list = document.getElementById('needHelpPostList');
  if(!sbClient) {
    if(list) list.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Supabase belum aktif.</div>';
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('help_posts')
      .select('id,user_phone,display_name,title,body,image_url,status,created_at,updated_at,last_message_at,resolved_at,chat_deleted_at')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true });
    if(error) throw error;
    const posts = data || [];
    await cleanupExpiredNeedHelpChats(posts);
    state.needHelpPosts = posts.sort((a, b) => {
      const aTime = new Date(a.last_message_at || a.updated_at || a.created_at || 0).getTime();
      const bTime = new Date(b.last_message_at || b.updated_at || b.created_at || 0).getTime();
      return bTime - aTime;
    });
    renderNeedHelpPosts();
    if(state.activeNeedHelpPostId && !state.needHelpPosts.some(post => post.id === state.activeNeedHelpPostId)) {
      closeNeedHelpThread();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Need Help posts error:', e);
    if(list) list.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Gagal memuat Need & Help. Pastikan tabel Supabase sudah dibuat.</div>';
  }
}

function renderNeedHelpPosts() {
  const list = document.getElementById('needHelpPostList');
  if(!list) return;
  const query = (document.getElementById('needHelpSearch')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const posts = state.needHelpPosts.filter(post => {
    if(!query) return true;
    return `${post.title || ''} ${post.body || ''} ${post.display_name || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(query);
  });
  if(!posts.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Belum ada post yang cocok.</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = posts.map(post => `
    <button class="needhelp-post ${post.id === state.activeNeedHelpPostId ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="openNeedHelpPost('${escapeHtml(String(post.id))}')">
      <div class="needhelp-post-title">${escapeHtml(post.title || 'Tanpa judul')}${isNeedHelpResolved(post) ? ' · Selesai' : ''}</div>
      <div class="needhelp-post-body">${escapeHtml((post.body || '').slice(0, 130))}${(post.body || '').length > 130 ? '...' : ''}</div>
      <div class="needhelp-post-meta"><i class="fas fa-comment"></i> ${escapeHtml(post.display_name || 'Member')} · ${escapeHtml(formatRelativeTime(post.created_at))}</div>
    </button>
  `).join('');
}

async function submitNeedHelpPost() {
  const title = document.getElementById('needHelpTitle')?.value.trim() || '';
  const body = document.getElementById('needHelpBody')?.value.trim() || '';
  const imageUrl = document.getElementById('needHelpImageUrl')?.value.trim() || '';
  if(!title || !body) {
    showToast('Judul dan isi pertanyaan wajib diisi', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(imageUrl && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(imageUrl)) {
    showToast('Link gambar harus berupa URL', 'error');
    return;
  }
  try {
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    if(!state.phone) throw new Error('Login dulu sebelum membuat post');
    const dayAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    const weekAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    const [dailyCheck, weeklyCheck] = await Promise.all([
      sbClient
        .from('help_posts')
        .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
        .eq('user_phone', state.phone)
        .gte('created_at', dayAgo),
      sbClient
        .from('help_posts')
        .select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
        .eq('user_phone', state.phone)
        .gte('created_at', weekAgo)
    ]);
    if(dailyCheck.error) throw dailyCheck.error;
    if(weeklyCheck.error) throw weeklyCheck.error;
    if((dailyCheck.count || 0) >= 1) {
      showToast('Kamu cuma bisa bikin 1 post Need & Help tiap 24 jam', 'error');
      return;
    }
    if((weeklyCheck.count || 0) >= 3) {
      showToast('Limit 3 post Need & Help per minggu sudah habis', 'error');
      return;
    }
    const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('help_posts').insert({
      user_phone: state.phone || null,
      display_name: getNeedHelpDisplayName(),
      title,
      body,
      image_url: imageUrl || null,
      status: 'open'
    }).select('id,created_at,last_message_at').single();
    if(error) throw error;
    ['needHelpTitle','needHelpBody','needHelpImageUrl'].forEach(id => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if(el) el.value = '';
    });
    toggleNeedHelpComposer(false);
    await loadNeedHelpPosts();
    if(data?.id) await openNeedHelpPost(data.id);
    showToast('Post Need & Help dibuat');
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Need Help post submit error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal membuat post', 'error');
  }
}

async function openNeedHelpPost(postId) {
  state.activeNeedHelpPostId = String(postId || '');
  setNeedHelpThreadPage(true);
  renderNeedHelpPosts();
  renderNeedHelpThreadHead();
  await loadNeedHelpMessages();
  document.getElementById('needHelpMessageInput')?.focus();
}

function closeNeedHelpThread() {
  state.activeNeedHelpPostId = '';
  state.needHelpMessages = [];
  setNeedHelpThreadPage(false);
  renderNeedHelpPosts();
  renderNeedHelpThreadHead();
  renderNeedHelpMessages();
}

function getActiveNeedHelpPost() {
  return state.needHelpPosts.find(post => String(post.id) === String(state.activeNeedHelpPostId));
}

function renderNeedHelpThreadHead() {
  const head = document.getElementById('needHelpThreadHead');
  if(!head) return;
  const post = getActiveNeedHelpPost();
  if(!post) {
    head.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Pilih post untuk masuk ke obrolan.</div>';
    return;
  }
  const ownerHint = !isNeedHelpResolved(post) && isNeedHelpOwner(post)
    ? `<div class="needhelp-thread-meta">Ketik "${NEED_HELP_RESOLVE_COMMAND}" untuk menandai urusan selesai.</div>`
    : '';
  head.innerHTML = `
    <div class="needhelp-thread-meta">${escapeHtml(post.display_name || 'Member')} · ${escapeHtml(formatRelativeTime(post.created_at))}</div>
    <div class="needhelp-post-title">${escapeHtml(post.title || 'Tanpa judul')}</div>
    <div class="needhelp-thread-body">${escapeHtml(post.body || '')}</div>
    ${post.image_url ? `<a class="downloader-result-link" href="${escapeHtml(post.image_url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fas fa-image"></i> Buka gambar</a>` : ''}
    ${ownerHint}
  `;
  startNeedHelpResolutionCountdown();
}

async function loadNeedHelpMessages() {
  const box = document.getElementById('needHelpMessages');
  if(!state.activeNeedHelpPostId) {
    renderNeedHelpMessages();
    return;
  }
  if(!sbClient) {
    if(box) box.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Supabase belum aktif.</div>';
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { data, error } = await sbClient
      .from('help_messages')
      .select('id,post_id,user_phone,display_name,body,created_at')
      .eq('post_id', state.activeNeedHelpPostId)
      .order('id', { ascending: true });
    if(error) throw error;
    state.needHelpMessages = orderNeedHelpMessages(data || []);
    await loadNeedHelpAvatarBorders(state.needHelpMessages);
    renderNeedHelpMessages();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Need Help messages error:', e);
    if(box) box.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Gagal memuat obrolan.</div>';
  }
}

function renderNeedHelpMessages() {
  const box = document.getElementById('needHelpMessages');
  if(!box) return;
  const post = getActiveNeedHelpPost();
  const input = document.getElementById('needHelpMessageInput');
  const form = document.querySelector('.needhelp-message-form');
  const sendBtn = document.querySelector('.needhelp-message-form .settings-save');
  const resolved = isNeedHelpResolved(post);
  form?.classList.toggle('resolved', resolved);
  if(input) {
    input.disabled = resolved;
    input.placeholder = resolved ? 'Form selesai - chat sudah dikunci' : 'Kirim pesan di obrolan ini';
  }
  if(sendBtn) sendBtn.disabled = resolved;
  if(!state.activeNeedHelpPostId) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Belum ada obrolan dibuka.</div>';
    return;
  }
  if(!state.needHelpMessages.length && !resolved) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="needhelp-empty">Belum ada pesan. Jadilah yang pertama bantu.</div>';
    return;
  }
  const messages = state.needHelpMessages;
  const messageHtml = messages.map(message => {
    const name = message.display_name || 'Member';
    const own = isOwnNeedHelpMessage(message);
    return `
      <div class="needhelp-message ${own ? 'own' : 'other'}">
        ${renderNeedHelpAvatar(name, getNeedHelpMessageAvatarBorder(message), getNeedHelpMessageAvatarUrl(message))}
        <div class="needhelp-bubble">
          <div class="needhelp-message-head">
            <span class="needhelp-message-name">${escapeHtml(name)}</span>
            <span class="needhelp-message-time">${escapeHtml(formatChatDateTime(message.created_at) || '')}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="needhelp-message-text">${escapeHtml(message.body || '')}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
  box.innerHTML = `${resolved ? renderNeedHelpResolutionNotice(post) : ''}${messageHtml}`;
  box.scrollTop = resolved ? 0 : box.scrollHeight;
  startNeedHelpResolutionCountdown();
}

function handleNeedHelpMessageKey(event) {
  if(event.key !== 'Enter' || event.shiftKey) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  sendNeedHelpMessage();
}

async function resolveNeedHelpPost(post = getActiveNeedHelpPost()) {
  if(!post) return;
  if(!isNeedHelpOwner(post)) {
    showToast('Hanya pembuat form yang bisa menutup urusan ini', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(isNeedHelpResolved(post)) {
    showToast('Urusan ini sudah selesai');
    return;
  }
  showLoading('Menandai urusan selesai...');
  try {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const { error } = await sbClient.from('help_posts')
      .update({ status: 'resolved', resolved_at: now, updated_at: now })
      .eq('id', post.id)
      .eq('user_phone', state.phone);
    if(error) throw error;
    showToast('Urusan selesai. Chat akan dibersihkan dalam 1 hari.');
    await loadNeedHelpPosts();
    await loadNeedHelpMessages();
    renderNeedHelpThreadHead();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Resolve Need Help error:', e);
    showToast('Gagal menandai urusan selesai', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function sendNeedHelpMessage() {
  const input = document.getElementById('needHelpMessageInput');
  const body = input?.value.trim() || '';
  const post = getActiveNeedHelpPost();
  if(!state.activeNeedHelpPostId) {
    showToast('Pilih post dulu', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(!body) return;
  if(normalizeNeedHelpCommand(body) === NEED_HELP_RESOLVE_COMMAND) {
    if(input) input.value = '';
    await resolveNeedHelpPost(post);
    return;
  }
  if(isNeedHelpResolved(post)) {
    showToast('Urusan ini sudah selesai', 'error');
    return;
  }
  try {
    if(!sbClient) throw new Error('Supabase belum aktif');
    const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('help_messages').insert({
      post_id: state.activeNeedHelpPostId,
      user_phone: state.phone || null,
      display_name: getNeedHelpDisplayName(),
      body
    }).select('id,created_at').single();
    if(error) throw error;
    input.value = '';
    const serverTime = data?.created_at || new Date().toISOString();
    await sbClient.from('help_posts')
      .update({ last_message_at: serverTime, updated_at: serverTime })
      .eq('id', state.activeNeedHelpPostId);
    await loadNeedHelpMessages();
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Need Help send error:', e);
    showToast(e.message || 'Gagal mengirim pesan', 'error');
  }
}

function subscribeNeedHelpList() {
}

function subscribeNeedHelpThread() {
}

function getSeenWinnerKeys() {
  if(!state.phone) return [];
  try {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(`ne_seen_winners_${state.phone}`) || '[]');
  } catch(e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function saveSeenWinnerKey(key) {
  if(!state.phone || !key) return;
  const seen = new Set(getSeenWinnerKeys());
  seen.add(key);
  localStorage.setItem(`ne_seen_winners_${state.phone}`, JSON.stringify([...seen]));
}

function normalizeMemberEffect(effect) {
  return ['none','neon','confetti','spotlight','warning'].includes(effect) ? effect : 'none';
}

function isMemberEffectActive(effect, until) {
  if(normalizeMemberEffect(effect) === 'none') return false;
  if(!until) return true;
  return new Date(until).getTime() > Date.now();
}

function clearMemberEffect() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('memberEffectOverlay');
  if(state.memberEffectInterval) {
    clearInterval(state.memberEffectInterval);
    state.memberEffectInterval = null;
  }
  if(state.memberEffectTimeout) {
    clearTimeout(state.memberEffectTimeout);
    state.memberEffectTimeout = null;
  }
  if(overlay) {
    overlay.className = 'member-effect-overlay';
    overlay.querySelectorAll('.member-effect-piece').forEach(item => item.remove());
  }
}

function spawnMemberConfetti() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('memberEffectOverlay');
  if(!overlay) return;
  const colors = ['#4fd97b', '#ffffff', '#f7d764', '#28a8ff', '#ff5b8a'];
  for(let i = 0; i < 28; i++) {
    const piece = document.createElement('span');
    piece.className = 'member-effect-piece';
    piece.style.left = `${Math.random() * 100}%`;
    piece.style.background = colors[i % colors.length];
    piece.style.animationDelay = `${Math.random() * 0.8}s`;
    piece.style.setProperty('--drift', `${Math.round((Math.random() - 0.5) * 160)}px`);
    overlay.appendChild(piece);
    setTimeout(() => piece.remove(), 3600);
  }
}

function applyMemberEffect(effect = state.adminEffect, until = state.adminEffectUntil) {
  effect = normalizeMemberEffect(effect);
  state.adminEffect = effect;
  state.adminEffectUntil = until || null;
  clearMemberEffect();
  if(!isMemberEffectActive(effect, until)) return;

  const overlay = document.getElementById('memberEffectOverlay');
  const label = document.getElementById('memberEffectLabel');
  if(!overlay || !label) return;
  label.textContent = effect === 'warning' ? 'ADMIN EFFECT' : '@ArcZNewEra';
  overlay.classList.add('active', effect);

  if(effect === 'confetti') {
    spawnMemberConfetti();
    state.memberEffectInterval = setInterval(spawnMemberConfetti, 4200);
  }
  if(until) {
    const ms = new Date(until).getTime() - Date.now();
    if(ms > 0) state.memberEffectTimeout = setTimeout(clearMemberEffect, ms);
  }
}

function applyMemberEffectFromProfile(profile) {
  if(!profile) {
    clearMemberEffect();
    return;
  }
  applyMemberEffect(profile.admin_effect || 'none', profile.admin_effect_until || null);
}

function subscribeMemberEffect() {
}

function createWinnerBurst(overlay) {
  overlay.querySelectorAll('.winner-burst').forEach(item => item.remove());
  const colors = ['#4fd97b', '#f7d764', '#ffffff', '#2fa85a', '#9dffbd'];
  for(let i = 0; i < 46; i++) {
    const piece = document.createElement('span');
    piece.className = 'winner-burst';
    piece.style.left = `${Math.random() * 100}%`;
    piece.style.background = colors[i % colors.length];
    piece.style.animationDelay = `${Math.random() * 1.4}s`;
    piece.style.animationDuration = `${2.2 + Math.random() * 1.3}s`;
    piece.style.setProperty('--drift', `${Math.round((Math.random() - 0.5) * 180)}px`);
    overlay.appendChild(piece);
  }
}

function showWinnerAlert(winner, giveaway) {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('winnerAlertOverlay');
  const titleEl = overlay?.querySelector('.winner-alert-title');
  const iconEl = overlay?.querySelector('.winner-alert-icon i');
  const textEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertText');
  const prizeEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertPrize');
  const hintEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertHint');
  if(!overlay || !textEl || !prizeEl) return;
  const title = giveaway?.title || 'giveaway';
  const prize = winner.prize_note || giveaway?.prize || 'Lampu neon';
  const winnerName = winner.winner_name || state.displayName || 'Member';
  state.winnerAlertOpen = true;
  state.activeWinnerAlertKey = `${winner.giveaway_id}|${winner.announced_at || winner.user_phone || ''}`;
  state.winnerAlertTapCount = 0;
  if(titleEl) titleEl.textContent = 'Selamat!';
  if(iconEl) iconEl.className = 'fas fa-trophy';
  textEl.textContent = `Selamat, ${winnerName}. Kamu menang di ${title} dan akan dikasih lampu neon.`;
  prizeEl.textContent = `Hadiah: ${prize}`;
  prizeEl.style.display = '';
  if(hintEl) hintEl.textContent = 'Ketuk 2x layar untuk lanjut';
  createWinnerBurst(overlay);
  overlay.classList.add('open');
}

function dismissWinnerAlert() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('winnerAlertOverlay');
  const hintEl = document.getElementById('winnerAlertHint');
  state.winnerAlertTapCount += 1;
  if(state.winnerAlertTapCount < 2) {
    if(hintEl) hintEl.textContent = 'Ketuk sekali lagi untuk menutup';
    return;
  }
  if(state.activeWinnerAlertKey) saveSeenWinnerKey(state.activeWinnerAlertKey);
  state.winnerAlertOpen = false;
  state.activeWinnerAlertKey = '';
  state.winnerAlertTapCount = 0;
  overlay?.classList.remove('open');
}

async function checkWinnerNotification() {
  if(!sbClient || !state.phone || state.winnerAlertOpen) return;
  const activePage = document.querySelector('.page.active')?.id?.replace('page-', '') || '';
  if(['phone','verified','onboarding','admin','banned'].includes(activePage)) return;
  try {
    const { data: winners, error } = await sbClient
      .from('giveaway_winners')
      .select('giveaway_id,user_phone,winner_name,prize_note,announced_at')
      .eq('user_phone', state.phone)
      .order('announced_at', { ascending: true });
    if(error) throw error;
    const seen = new Set(getSeenWinnerKeys());
    const unseen = (winners || []).find(winner => {
      const key = `${winner.giveaway_id}|${winner.announced_at || winner.user_phone || ''}`;
      return key !== state.activeWinnerAlertKey && !seen.has(key);
    });
    if(!unseen) return;

    let giveaway = state.giveaways.find(item => item.id === unseen.giveaway_id);
    if(!giveaway) {
      const { data: giveawayData } = await sbClient
        .from('giveaways')
        .select('id,title,prize')
        .eq('id', unseen.giveaway_id)
        .maybeSingle();
      giveaway = giveawayData;
    }
    showWinnerAlert(unseen, giveaway);
  } catch(e) {
    console.warn('Gagal cek pemenang giveaway:', e);
  }
}

// ========== MATERI FUNCTIONS (FIXED) ==========
async function loadMaterialProgress() {
    const localCompleted = loadLocalMaterialProgress();
    materialState.completed = localCompleted;
    materialState.synced = false;

    if (!sbClient || !state.phone) return;
    try {
        const { data: userSummary, error } = await sbClient
            .from('users')
            .select('material_completed')
            .eq('phone', state.phone)
            .maybeSingle();

        if (error) throw error;

        const summaryCompleted = normalizeMaterialIds(userSummary?.material_completed || []);
        materialState.completed = normalizeMaterialIds([...localCompleted, ...summaryCompleted]);
        materialState.synced = true;
        saveLocalMaterialProgress();
        if(summaryCompleted.length !== materialState.completed.length) {
            await syncUserLearningSummary();
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Gagal memuat progress materi:', e);
    }
}

async function saveMaterialProgress(materialId) {
    materialId = Number(materialId);
    if (!Number.isInteger(materialId)) return false;

    const previousCompleted = [...materialState.completed];
    if (!materialState.completed.includes(materialId)) {
        materialState.completed = normalizeMaterialIds([...materialState.completed, materialId]);
    }
    saveLocalMaterialProgress();

    if (!sbClient || !state.phone) return true;

    try {
        if(!(await ensureCurrentUserStillExists())) return false;
        const synced = await syncUserLearningSummary();
        if(!synced) throw new Error('Gagal menyimpan progress ke user');
        materialState.synced = true;
        return true;
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Gagal menyimpan progress materi:', e);
        materialState.completed = previousCompleted;
        saveLocalMaterialProgress();
        return false;
    }
}

async function syncUserLearningSummary() {
    if (!sbClient || !state.phone) return false;

    const completed = normalizeMaterialIds(materialState.completed);
    const totalMaterials = MATERI_DATA.length || DEFAULT_MATERI_DATA.length || completed.length || 1;
    const progressPercent = Math.min(100, Math.round((completed.length / totalMaterials) * 100));
    const lastMaterialId = completed.length ? completed[completed.length - 1] : null;

    try {
        const { data, error } = await sbClient.from('users').update({
            material_completed: completed,
            material_progress_percent: progressPercent,
            last_material_id: lastMaterialId,
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }).eq('phone', state.phone).select('phone').maybeSingle();

        if (error) throw error;
        if(!data) {
            returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Gagal sync ringkasan progress materi:', e);
        return false;
    }
}

function renderMaterialsPath() {
    const container = document.getElementById('materiPathContainer');
    if (!container) return;

    const completedIds = materialState.completed || [];
    
    let html = '';
    MATERI_DATA.forEach((m, index) => {
        const isCompleted = completedIds.includes(m.id);
        const prevCompleted = index === 0 || completedIds.includes(MATERI_DATA[index - 1].id);
        const isUnlocked = index === 0 || prevCompleted;
        
        let dotClass = 'locked';
        let lineClass = 'locked';
        let cardClass = 'locked';
        let lockIcon = '<i class="fas fa-lock"></i>';
        
        if (isCompleted) {
            dotClass = 'completed';
            lineClass = 'completed';
            cardClass = 'completed';
            lockIcon = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>';
        } else if (isUnlocked) {
            dotClass = 'unlocked';
            lineClass = 'unlocked';
            cardClass = '';
            lockIcon = '';
        }
        
        const numberContent = isCompleted
            ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>'
            : m.id;

        html += `
            <div class="materi-path-item">
                <div class="materi-path-connector">
                    <div class="materi-path-dot ${dotClass}"></div>
                    ${index < MATERI_DATA.length - 1 ? `<div class="materi-path-line ${lineClass}"></div>` : ''}
                </div>
                <div class="materi-path-card ${cardClass}" data-id="${m.id}" data-unlocked="${!isCompleted && isUnlocked}" data-completed="${isCompleted}">
                    <div class="materi-path-number">${numberContent}</div>
                    <div class="materi-path-info">
                        <div class="materi-path-card-title">${escapeHtml(m.title)}</div>
                        <div class="materi-path-card-desc">${escapeHtml(m.desc)}</div>
                    </div>
                    ${lockIcon ? `<span class="materi-path-lock-icon" id="pathLock-${m.id}">${lockIcon}</span>` : ''}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });
    container.innerHTML = html;
    
    // Attach event listeners setelah DOM diupdate
    document.querySelectorAll('.materi-path-card').forEach(card => {
        const isUnlocked = card.getAttribute('data-unlocked') === 'true';
        const isCompleted = card.getAttribute('data-completed') === 'true';
        const materialId = parseInt(card.getAttribute('data-id'));
        
        if (!isCompleted && isUnlocked) {
            card.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openReadingModal(materialId);
            };
        } else if (isCompleted) {
            card.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.title = 'Baca ulang materi';
            card.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openReadingModal(materialId, { reread: true });
            };
        } else {
            card.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            card.onclick = () => showToast('Selesaikan materi sebelumnya terlebih dahulu!');
        }
    });

    const total = MATERI_DATA.length;
    const completed = completedIds.length;
    const progress = total > 0 ? (completed / total) * 100 : 0;
    const progressBar = document.getElementById('materiProgressFill');
    if (progressBar) progressBar.style.width = `${progress}%`;
    
    const completedText = document.getElementById('materiCompletedText');
    if (completedText) completedText.textContent = `${completed} / ${total} selesai`;
    
    const badge = document.getElementById('materiBadge');
    if (badge) badge.textContent = total - completed;
}

function renderMaterialsPath() {
    const container = document.getElementById('materiPathContainer');
    if (!container) return;

    const completedIds = materialState.completed || [];
    let html = '';

    MATERI_DATA.forEach((m, index) => {
        const isCompleted = completedIds.includes(m.id);
        const prevCompleted = index === 0 || completedIds.includes(MATERI_DATA[index - 1].id);
        const isUnlocked = index === 0 || prevCompleted;

        let dotClass = 'locked';
        let lineClass = 'locked';
        let cardClass = 'locked';
        let lockIcon = '<i class="fas fa-lock"></i>';

        if (isCompleted) {
            dotClass = 'completed';
            lineClass = 'completed';
            cardClass = 'completed';
            lockIcon = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>';
        } else if (isUnlocked) {
            dotClass = 'unlocked';
            lineClass = 'unlocked';
            cardClass = '';
            lockIcon = '';
        }

        const numberContent = isCompleted
            ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>'
            : m.id;

        html += `
            <div class="materi-path-item">
                <div class="materi-path-connector">
                    <div class="materi-path-dot ${dotClass}"></div>
                    ${index < MATERI_DATA.length - 1 ? `<div class="materi-path-line ${lineClass}"></div>` : ''}
                </div>
                <div class="materi-path-card ${cardClass}" data-id="${m.id}" data-unlocked="${!isCompleted && isUnlocked}" data-completed="${isCompleted}">
                    <div class="materi-path-number">${numberContent}</div>
                    <div class="materi-path-info">
                        <div class="materi-path-card-title">${escapeHtml(m.title)}</div>
                        <div class="materi-path-card-desc">${escapeHtml(m.desc)}</div>
                    </div>
                    ${lockIcon ? `<span class="materi-path-lock-icon" id="pathLock-${m.id}">${lockIcon}</span>` : ''}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    });

    container.innerHTML = html;

    document.querySelectorAll('.materi-path-card').forEach(card => {
        const isUnlocked = card.getAttribute('data-unlocked') === 'true';
        const isCompleted = card.getAttribute('data-completed') === 'true';
        const materialId = parseInt(card.getAttribute('data-id'));

        if (!isCompleted && isUnlocked) {
            card.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openReadingModal(materialId);
            };
        } else if (isCompleted) {
            card.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.title = 'Baca ulang materi';
            card.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openReadingModal(materialId, { reread: true });
            };
        } else {
            card.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            card.onclick = () => showToast('Selesaikan materi sebelumnya terlebih dahulu!');
        }
    });

    const total = MATERI_DATA.length;
    const completed = completedIds.length;
    const progress = total > 0 ? (completed / total) * 100 : 0;
    const progressBar = document.getElementById('materiProgressFill');
    if (progressBar) progressBar.style.width = `${progress}%`;

    const completedText = document.getElementById('materiCompletedText');
    if (completedText) completedText.textContent = `${completed} / ${total} selesai`;

    const badge = document.getElementById('materiBadge');
    if (badge) badge.textContent = total - completed;
}

function getMaterialChapterGroups() {
    const chapterMap = new Map((MATERIAL_CHAPTERS || []).map(chapter => [Number(chapter.id), {
        id: Number(chapter.id),
        key: `chapter-${chapter.id}`,
        title: chapter.title || `Bab ${chapter.id}`,
        order_num: Number(chapter.order_num || chapter.id || 0),
        materials: []
    }]));
    const groups = [...chapterMap.values()];
    const ungrouped = {
        id: 0,
        key: 'chapter-none',
        title: 'Materi Tanpa Bab',
        order_num: 999999,
        materials: []
    };
    (MATERI_DATA || []).forEach(material => {
        const chapterId = Number(material.chapter_id || 0);
        const target = chapterMap.get(chapterId) || ungrouped;
        target.materials.push(material);
    });
    if(ungrouped.materials.length) groups.push(ungrouped);
    if(!groups.some(group => group.materials.length)) {
        return [{
            id: 0,
            key: 'chapter-all',
            title: 'Materi',
            order_num: 0,
            materials: [...(MATERI_DATA || [])]
        }];
    }
    return groups
        .filter(group => group.materials.length)
        .map(group => ({
            ...group,
            materials: group.materials.sort((a, b) =>
                (Number(a.chapter_order || a.order_num || a.id || 0) - Number(b.chapter_order || b.order_num || b.id || 0))
            )
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => (a.order_num || 0) - (b.order_num || 0));
}

function toggleMaterialChapterGroup(key) {
    const group = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-material-chapter]')]
        .find(item => item.getAttribute('data-material-chapter') === String(key));
    group?.classList.toggle('open');
}

function renderMaterialPathCard(material, flatIndex, flatMaterials, completedIds) {
    const isCompleted = completedIds.includes(material.id);
    const prevCompleted = flatIndex === 0 || completedIds.includes(flatMaterials[flatIndex - 1]?.id);
    const isUnlocked = flatIndex === 0 || prevCompleted;
    let dotClass = 'locked';
    let lineClass = 'locked';
    let cardClass = 'locked';
    let lockIcon = '<i class="fas fa-lock"></i>';

    if (isCompleted) {
        dotClass = 'completed';
        lineClass = 'completed';
        cardClass = 'completed';
        lockIcon = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>';
    } else if (isUnlocked) {
        dotClass = 'unlocked';
        lineClass = 'unlocked';
        cardClass = '';
        lockIcon = '';
    }

    const numberContent = isCompleted
        ? '<i class="fas fa-check"></i>'
        : flatIndex + 1;

    return `
        <div class="materi-path-item">
            <div class="materi-path-connector">
                <div class="materi-path-dot ${dotClass}"></div>
                ${flatIndex < flatMaterials.length - 1 ? `<div class="materi-path-line ${lineClass}"></div>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="materi-path-card ${cardClass}" data-id="${material.id}" data-unlocked="${!isCompleted && isUnlocked}" data-completed="${isCompleted}">
                <div class="materi-path-number">${numberContent}</div>
                <div class="materi-path-info">
                    <div class="materi-path-card-title">${escapeHtml(material.title)}</div>
                    <div class="materi-path-card-desc">${escapeHtml(material.desc)}</div>
                </div>
                ${lockIcon ? `<span class="materi-path-lock-icon" id="pathLock-${material.id}">${lockIcon}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}

function bindMaterialPathCards() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.materi-path-card').forEach(card => {
        const isUnlocked = card.getAttribute('data-unlocked') === 'true';
        const isCompleted = card.getAttribute('data-completed') === 'true';
        const materialId = parseInt(card.getAttribute('data-id'));

        if (!isCompleted && isUnlocked) {
            card.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openReadingModal(materialId);
            };
        } else if (isCompleted) {
            card.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.title = 'Baca ulang materi';
            card.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                openReadingModal(materialId, { reread: true });
            };
        } else {
            card.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            card.onclick = () => showToast('Selesaikan materi sebelumnya terlebih dahulu!');
        }
    });
}

function renderMaterialsPath() {
    const container = document.getElementById('materiPathContainer');
    if (!container) return;

    const completedIds = materialState.completed || [];
    const groups = getMaterialChapterGroups();
    const flatMaterials = groups.flatMap(group => group.materials);
    let flatIndex = 0;
    container.innerHTML = groups.map((group, groupIndex) => {
        const groupStartIndex = flatIndex;
        const groupMaterialIds = group.materials.map(material => material.id);
        const groupDone = groupMaterialIds.filter(id => completedIds.includes(id)).length;
        const shouldOpen = groupIndex === 0
            || groupMaterialIds.some(id => !completedIds.includes(id))
            || groupDone > 0;
        const cards = group.materials.map(material => {
            const card = renderMaterialPathCard(material, flatIndex, flatMaterials, completedIds);
            flatIndex += 1;
            return card;
        }).join('');
        return `
            <section class="materi-chapter-group ${shouldOpen ? 'open' : ''}" data-material-chapter="${escapeHtml(group.key)}">
                <button class="materi-chapter-toggle" type="button" onclick="toggleMaterialChapterGroup('${escapeHtml(group.key)}')">
                    <span>
                        <div class="materi-chapter-title">${escapeHtml(group.title)}</div>
                        <div class="materi-chapter-meta">${groupDone} / ${group.materials.length} materi selesai · mulai dari urutan ${groupStartIndex + 1}</div>
                    </span>
                    <i class="fas fa-chevron-down materi-chapter-chevron"></i>
                </button>
                <div class="materi-chapter-body">${cards}</div>
            </section>
        `;
    }).join('');

    bindMaterialPathCards();

    const total = flatMaterials.length;
    const completed = completedIds.filter(id => flatMaterials.some(material => material.id === id)).length;
    const progress = total > 0 ? (completed / total) * 100 : 0;
    const progressBar = document.getElementById('materiProgressFill');
    if (progressBar) progressBar.style.width = `${progress}%`;

    const completedText = document.getElementById('materiCompletedText');
    if (completedText) completedText.textContent = `${completed} / ${total} selesai`;

    const badge = document.getElementById('materiBadge');
    if (badge) badge.textContent = total - completed;
}

function escapeHtml(text) {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.textContent = text;
    return div.innerHTML;
}

function renderExpandableText(text, limit = 200) {
    const value = String(text || '');
    if(value.length <= limit) return escapeHtml(value);
    const shortText = `${value.slice(0, limit)}...`;
    return `
      <span class="expandable-text">
        <span class="expandable-text-short">${escapeHtml(shortText)}</span>
        <span class="expandable-text-full">${escapeHtml(value)}</span>
        <button class="expandable-text-link" type="button" onclick="toggleExpandableText(this)">lihat selengkapnya</button>
      </span>
    `;
}

function toggleExpandableText(button) {
    const wrap = button?.closest?.('.expandable-text');
    if(!wrap) return;
    const expanded = wrap.classList.toggle('expanded');
    button.textContent = expanded ? 'lihat lebih sedikit' : 'lihat selengkapnya';
}

function openReadingModal(materialId, options = {}) {
    const material = MATERI_DATA.find(m => m.id === materialId);
    if (!material) return;
    const isReread = options.reread === true || materialState.completed.includes(materialId);

    const orderedMaterials = getMaterialChapterGroups().flatMap(group => group.materials);
    const index = orderedMaterials.findIndex(m => m.id === materialId);
    if (!isReread && index > 0 && !materialState.completed.includes(orderedMaterials[index - 1].id)) {
        showToast('Selesaikan materi sebelumnya dulu!');
        return;
    }

    materialState.currentlyReading = materialId;
    materialState.secondsLeft = isReread ? 0 : 3;

    document.getElementById('readingTitle').textContent = material.title;
    const readingContent = document.getElementById('readingContent');
    if(readingContent) {
        readingContent.innerHTML = escapeHtml(material.content || 'Materi belum punya isi.');
    }

    const modal = document.getElementById('readingModal');
    modal.classList.add('open');

    if (materialState.readTimer) clearInterval(materialState.readTimer);
    if (isReread) {
        materialState.readTimer = null;
        renderReadingChapterModal(true);
        return;
    }

    renderReadingChapterModal(false);
    startReadingChapterTimer();
}

function renderReadingChapterModal(isReread = false) {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnFinishReading');
    if(!btn) return;

    if(isReread) {
      document.getElementById('readingTimerText').textContent = 'Mode baca ulang';
      btn.classList.add('active');
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.innerHTML = 'Tutup <i class="fas fa-xmark"></i>';
      btn.onclick = closeReadingModal;
      return;
    }

    document.getElementById('readingTimerText').textContent = `Selesaikan membaca (${materialState.secondsLeft}s)`;
    btn.classList.remove('active');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = `Baca ${materialState.secondsLeft} detik lagi...`;
    btn.onclick = null;
}

function selectReadingChapter(index) {
    const chapters = materialState.currentChapters || [];
    if(!chapters[index]) return;
    const completed = materialState.chapterCompleted || [];
    const isReread = materialState.completed.includes(materialState.currentlyReading);
    const unlocked = isReread || index === 0 || completed.includes(index - 1);
    if(!unlocked) {
        showToast('Baca bab sebelumnya dulu!', 'error');
        return;
    }
    if(materialState.readTimer) {
        clearInterval(materialState.readTimer);
        materialState.readTimer = null;
    }
    materialState.activeChapterIndex = index;
    materialState.secondsLeft = completed.includes(index) || isReread ? 0 : 3;
    renderReadingChapterModal(isReread);
    if(!isReread && !completed.includes(index)) startReadingChapterTimer();
}

function startReadingChapterTimer() {
    if(materialState.readTimer) clearInterval(materialState.readTimer);
    materialState.readTimer = setInterval(() => {
        materialState.secondsLeft--;
        document.getElementById('readingTimerText').textContent = `Selesaikan membaca (${materialState.secondsLeft}s)`;
        const btn = document.getElementById('btnFinishReading');
        btn.textContent = `Baca ${materialState.secondsLeft} detik lagi...`;

        if(materialState.secondsLeft <= 0) {
            clearInterval(materialState.readTimer);
            materialState.readTimer = null;
            document.getElementById('readingTimerText').textContent = 'Materi siap diselesaikan.';
            btn.classList.add('active');
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = 'Selesaikan Materi <i class="fas fa-check"></i>';
            btn.onclick = () => finishReading(materialState.currentlyReading);
        }
    }, 1000);
}

function finishReadingChapter(index) {
    if(!materialState.chapterCompleted.includes(index)) {
        materialState.chapterCompleted.push(index);
        materialState.chapterCompleted.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    }
    const allDone = materialState.chapterCompleted.length >= materialState.currentChapters.length;
    document.getElementById('readingTimerText').textContent = allDone ? 'Semua bab selesai dibaca.' : 'Bab selesai dibaca.';
    renderReadingChapterModal(false);
}

function goToNextReadingChapter() {
    const chapters = materialState.currentChapters || [];
    const activeIndex = materialState.activeChapterIndex || 0;
    if(materialState.chapterCompleted.length >= chapters.length) {
        finishReading(materialState.currentlyReading);
        return;
    }
    const nextIndex = Math.min(activeIndex + 1, chapters.length - 1);
    selectReadingChapter(nextIndex);
}

async function finishReading(materialId) {
    materialId = Number(materialId);
    const material = MATERI_DATA.find(item => Number(item.id) === materialId);
    if(!Number.isInteger(materialId) || !material) {
        closeReadingModal();
        showToast('Materi tidak terbaca, coba buka lagi.', 'error');
        return;
    }
    if (materialState.readTimer) {
        clearInterval(materialState.readTimer);
        materialState.readTimer = null;
    }
    
    if (materialState.completed.includes(materialId)) {
        closeReadingModal();
        showToast('Materi sudah selesai!');
        return;
    }
    
    showLoading('Menyimpan progress...');
    
    const saved = await saveMaterialProgress(materialId);
    
    if (saved) {
        const lockEl = document.getElementById(`pathLock-${materialId}`);
        if (lockEl) {
            lockEl.classList.add('unlocking');
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 400));
        }
        
        renderMaterialsPath();
        showToast(`${material.title || 'Materi'} selesai!`);
        
        if (materialState.completed.length === MATERI_DATA.length) {
            showToast('SELAMAT! Kamu telah menyelesaikan semua materi NeverEra Academy!');
        }
    } else {
        showToast('Gagal menyimpan progress, coba lagi nanti');
    }
    
    hideLoading();
    closeReadingModal();
}

function closeReadingModal() {
    if (materialState.readTimer) {
        clearInterval(materialState.readTimer);
        materialState.readTimer = null;
    }
    const modal = document.getElementById('readingModal');
    modal.classList.remove('open');
    materialState.currentlyReading = null;
    materialState.activeChapterIndex = 0;
    materialState.currentChapters = [];
    materialState.chapterCompleted = [];
}

async function initMaterials() {
    await loadMaterialCatalog();
    await loadMaterialProgress();
    renderMaterialsPath();
}

// ========== CHECK SAVED SESSION ==========
async function checkSavedSession() {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem('ne_session');
  if(saved) {
    try {
      const s = JSON.parse(saved);
      if(s.phone && s.verified && Date.now() - s.timestamp < 86400000) {
        state.phone = s.phone;
        state.verifyCode = s.verifyCode || '';
        state.uniqueCode = s.uniqueCode || '';
        state.theme = s.theme || 'light';
        state.uiMode = normalizeUiMode(s.uiMode || state.uiMode || 'old');
        state.accentColor = normalizeAccentColor(s.accentColor || state.accentColor || '#27a44f');
        state.displayName = s.displayName || '';
        state.profilePictureUrl = '';
        state.avatarBorder = s.avatarBorder || state.avatarBorder || 'white';
        state.winnerDisplayMode = s.winnerDisplayMode || 'display_name';
        state.gender = s.gender || '';
        state.interests = normalizeInterests(s.interests);
        state.timezone = normalizeDisplayTimezone(s.timezone || state.timezone);
        applyAppearancePreferences();
        if(sbClient) {
          const profile = await loadUserProfile();
          if(!profile && state.profileLoadStatus === 'missing') {
            returnToHomeBecauseAccountMissing();
            return false;
          }
          if(!profile) {
            returnToHomeBecauseProfileBlocked();
            return false;
          }
        }
        if(!(await enforceUpdatingServerGate())) return true;
        await initDashboard({ resetPage: false });
        if(!ensureAccountTuningComplete()) return true;
        await loadPvpState();
        await navigateTo(getSavedMemberRoute(), { replaceRoute: true });
        return true;
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem('ne_session');
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }
  return false;
}

async function checkPendingVerification() {
  const pending = localStorage.getItem('ne_pending');
  if(pending) {
    try {
      const p = JSON.parse(pending);
      if(Date.now() - p.timestamp < 3600000) {
        state.phone = p.phone;
        state.verifyCode = p.code;
        state.displayName = p.displayName || state.displayName || '';
        const verified = await checkVerified(p.phone, p.code);
        if(verified) {
          localStorage.removeItem('ne_pending');
          triggerVerified();
          return true;
        } else {
          document.getElementById('phone-form-area').style.display = 'none';
          document.getElementById('waitingState').style.display = 'block';
          document.getElementById('verifyCodeDisplay').textContent = p.code;
          startPolling();
          return true;
        }
      } else {
        localStorage.removeItem('ne_pending');
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }
  return false;
}
